
══════════════════ leka_v2.py ══════════════════
"""
LEKAv2 – Linear‑time Efficient Kernel Attention (landmark version)
Dual‑scale random features, landmark selection, correction matrix, local window gate.
"""

import math
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Optional, Tuple

class LEKAv2Attention(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, config, comp_config, layer_idx, total_layers=6, bert_mode=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_attention_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.attention_head_size = int(config.hidden_size / config.num_attention_heads)
        self.all_head_size = self.num_attention_heads * self.attention_head_size
        self.head_dim = self.attention_head_size
        self.bert_mode = bert_mode
        self.layer_idx = layer_idx

        self.num_landmarks = getattr(comp_config, 'num_landmarks', 16)
        self.feature_dim   = getattr(comp_config, 'feature_dim', 128)

        self.query  = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.key    = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.value  = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(self.all_head_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(getattr(comp_config, 'dropout', 0.1))

        self.rand_proj1 = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(self.head_dim, self.feature_dim) * 0.02)
        self.rand_proj2 = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(self.head_dim, self.feature_dim // 2) * 0.02)

        total_feat_dim = 2 * self.feature_dim + 2 * (self.feature_dim // 2)
        self.landmark_scorer = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(total_feat_dim, self.feature_dim // 2),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Linear(self.feature_dim // 2, 1)
        )

        self.W_corr = nn.Parameter(
            torch.eye(self.head_dim).unsqueeze(0).repeat(self.num_attention_heads, 1, 1)
        )

        self.local_window_size = getattr(comp_config, 'local_window_size', 5)
        self.local_gate = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.num_attention_heads) + 0.1)

        self.landmark_logits = nn.Parameter(
            torch.zeros(self.num_attention_heads) + math.log(self.num_landmarks / 2)
        )

        if getattr(comp_config, 'use_gate', False) and not bert_mode:
            self.gate = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(getattr(comp_config, 'gate_bias', 0.0)))
        else:
            self.gate = None

    def transpose_for_scores(self, x):
        new_shape = x.size()[:-1] + (self.num_attention_heads, self.attention_head_size)
        return x.view(new_shape).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)

    def kernel_feature_map(self, x):
        W1 = self.rand_proj1.to(x.device)
        W2 = self.rand_proj2.to(x.device)

        proj1 = torch.matmul(x, W1)
        f1 = torch.cat([torch.cos(proj1), torch.sin(proj1)], dim=-1) / math.sqrt(self.feature_dim)

        proj2 = torch.matmul(x, W2)
        f2 = torch.cat([torch.cos(proj2), torch.sin(proj2)], dim=-1) / math.sqrt(self.feature_dim // 2)

        return torch.cat([f1, f2], dim=-1)

    def select_landmarks(self, K_features, V):
        B, H, N, feat_dim = K_features.shape
        L_max = self.num_landmarks
        if N <= L_max:
            return K_features, V

        scores = self.landmark_scorer(K_features).squeeze(-1)  # (B, H, N)
        L = L_max
        _, idx = scores.topk(L, dim=-1)  # (B, H, L)

        K_land = torch.gather(K_features, 2, idx.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, -1, feat_dim))
        V_land = torch.gather(V, 2, idx.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, -1, V.shape[-1]))
        return K_land, V_land

    @staticmethod
    def softpick(x, dim=-1, temperature=1.0):
        m = x.max(dim=dim, keepdim=True).values
        e = torch.exp((x - m) / temperature)
        denom = e - torch.exp(-m / temperature)
        p = F.relu(denom)
        return p / (p.sum(dim=dim, keepdim=True) + 1e-9)

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, head_mask=None, output_attentions=False, **kwargs):
        B, N, D = hidden_states.shape
        H = self.num_attention_heads
        L = self.num_landmarks

        Q = self.transpose_for_scores(self.query(hidden_states))
        K = self.transpose_for_scores(self.key(hidden_states))
        V = self.transpose_for_scores(self.value(hidden_states))

        Q_features = self.kernel_feature_map(Q)
        K_features = self.kernel_feature_map(K)

        K_land, V_land = self.select_landmarks(K_features, V)

        W = self.W_corr.unsqueeze(0).expand(B, -1, -1, -1)
        V_land_adj = torch.einsum('bhld,bhde->bhle', V_land, W)

        attn_weights = torch.matmul(Q_features, K_land.transpose(-2, -1))

        # Mask handling
        mask = attention_mask
        if mask is None:
            mask = kwargs.get('attn_mask', None)
        if mask is not None:
            # Convert to boolean mask (B, N) where True = keep
            if mask.dim() == 4 and mask.shape[-2] == mask.shape[-1]:
                token_valid = mask[:, 0, 0, :] > -1e9
            elif mask.dim() == 4:
                token_valid = mask.squeeze(1).squeeze(1) > -1e9
            elif mask.dim() == 3:
                token_valid = mask.squeeze(1).bool()
            elif mask.dim() == 2:
                token_valid = mask.bool()
            else:
                token_valid = None

            if token_valid is not None:
                # For landmarks, we need to mask queries to padded positions only.
                # attn_weights: (B, H, N, L); we mask where query position is invalid.
                attn_weights = attn_weights.masked_fill(~token_valid.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1), -1e9)

        attn_weights = self.softpick(attn_weights, dim=-1)
        attn_weights = self.dropout(attn_weights)

        context_leka = torch.matmul(attn_weights, V_land_adj)

        # Local window attention
        idx = torch.arange(N, device=hidden_states.device)
        dist = (idx.unsqueeze(0) - idx.unsqueeze(1)).abs()
        window_mask = (dist <= self.local_window_size // 2).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)

        scale = self.head_dim ** -0.5
        local_scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
        local_scores = local_scores.masked_fill(~window_mask, -1e9)
        local_attn = F.softmax(local_scores, dim=-1)
        context_local = torch.matmul(local_attn, V)

        gate_val = torch.sigmoid(self.local_gate).view(1, H, 1, 1)
        context = gate_val * context_leka + (1 - gate_val) * context_local

        context = context.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(B, N, self.all_head_size)
        out = self.out_proj(context)
        return out, None

    def get_ortho_loss(self):
        return 0.0


from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("leka_v2")
def inject_leka_v2(layer, model_config, comp_config, layer_idx, total_layers, old_attn):
    new_attn = LEKAv2Attention(
        config=model_config,
        comp_config=comp_config,
        layer_idx=layer_idx,
        total_layers=total_layers,
        bert_mode=False,
    )
    # Copy pretrained weights
    new_attn.query.weight.data.copy_(old_attn.q_lin.weight.data)
    new_attn.query.bias.data.copy_(old_attn.q_lin.bias.data)
    new_attn.key.weight.data.copy_(old_attn.k_lin.weight.data)
    new_attn.key.bias.data.copy_(old_attn.k_lin.bias.data)
    new_attn.value.weight.data.copy_(old_attn.v_lin.weight.data)
    new_attn.value.bias.data.copy_(old_attn.v_lin.bias.data)
    new_attn.out_proj.weight.data.copy_(old_attn.out_lin.weight.data)
    new_attn.out_proj.bias.data.copy_(old_attn.out_lin.bias.data)
    layer.attention = new_attn
    return new_attn

from . import leka_v1
from . import leka_v2
from . import leka_v3
══════════════════ leka_v3.py ══════════════════
"""
LEKAv3 – Linear‑time Efficient Kernel Attention (improved version)
Multi‑scale features, dynamic landmark count, and learnable gating.
"""

import math
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Optional, Tuple
from framework.attentions.core.base import BaseAttention
from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("leka_v3")
class LEKAV3Attention(BaseAttention):
    def __init__(self, config, comp_config, layer_idx, total_layers=6, bert_mode=False):
        super().__init__(config)
        self.num_attention_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.attention_head_size = int(config.hidden_size / config.num_attention_heads)
        self.all_head_size = self.num_attention_heads * self.attention_head_size
        self.head_dim = self.attention_head_size
        self.bert_mode = bert_mode
        self.layer_idx = layer_idx

        self.num_landmarks = getattr(comp_config, 'num_landmarks', 32)
        self.feature_dim   = getattr(comp_config, 'feature_dim', 128)

        self.query  = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.key    = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.value  = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(self.all_head_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(getattr(comp_config, 'dropout', 0.1))

        # Multi‑scale random projections
        self.register_buffer('proj1', torch.randn(self.head_dim, self.feature_dim))
        self.register_buffer('proj2', torch.randn(self.head_dim, self.feature_dim // 2))

        # Dynamic landmark scorer (based on V)
        self.landmark_scorer = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(self.head_dim, 32),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Linear(32, 1)
        )

        self.W_corr = nn.Parameter(
            torch.eye(self.head_dim).unsqueeze(0).repeat(self.num_attention_heads, 1, 1)
        )

        self.local_window_size = getattr(comp_config, 'local_window_size', 3)
        self.gate = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.num_attention_heads))

    def transpose_for_scores(self, x):
        new_shape = x.size()[:-1] + (self.num_attention_heads, self.attention_head_size)
        return x.view(new_shape).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)

    def kernel_feature_map(self, x):
        proj1 = self.proj1.to(x.device)
        proj2 = self.proj2.to(x.device)

        p1 = torch.matmul(x, proj1)  # (B, H, N, F)
        f1 = torch.cat([torch.cos(p1), torch.sin(p1)], dim=-1) / math.sqrt(self.feature_dim)

        p2 = torch.matmul(x, proj2)
        f2 = torch.cat([torch.cos(p2), torch.sin(p2)], dim=-1) / math.sqrt(self.feature_dim // 2)

        return torch.cat([f1, f2], dim=-1)

    def select_landmarks(self, K_features, V):
        B, H, N, feat_dim = K_features.shape
        L_max = self.num_landmarks
        if N <= L_max:
            return K_features, V

        # Use value features for scoring (more context‑aware)
        scores = self.landmark_scorer(V).squeeze(-1)  # (B, H, N)
        L = L_max
        _, idx = scores.topk(L, dim=-1)

        K_land = torch.gather(K_features, 2, idx.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, -1, feat_dim))
        V_land = torch.gather(V, 2, idx.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, -1, V.shape[-1]))
        return K_land, V_land

    @staticmethod
    def softpick(x, dim=-1, temperature=1.0):
        m = x.max(dim=dim, keepdim=True).values
        e = torch.exp((x - m) / temperature)
        denom = e - torch.exp(-m / temperature)
        p = F.relu(denom)
        return p / (p.sum(dim=dim, keepdim=True) + 1e-9)

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, head_mask=None, output_attentions=False, **kwargs):
        B, N, D = hidden_states.shape
        H = self.num_attention_heads
        L = self.num_landmarks

        Q = self.transpose_for_scores(self.query(hidden_states))
        K = self.transpose_for_scores(self.key(hidden_states))
        V = self.transpose_for_scores(self.value(hidden_states))

        Q_features = self.kernel_feature_map(Q)
        K_features = self.kernel_feature_map(K)

        K_land, V_land = self.select_landmarks(K_features, V)

        W = self.W_corr.unsqueeze(0).expand(B, -1, -1, -1)
        V_land_adj = torch.einsum('bhld,bhde->bhle', V_land, W)

        attn_weights = torch.matmul(Q_features, K_land.transpose(-2, -1))

        # Mask handling (same as v2)
        mask = attention_mask
        if mask is None:
            mask = kwargs.get('attn_mask', None)
        if mask is not None:
            if mask.dim() == 4 and mask.shape[-2] == mask.shape[-1]:
                token_valid = mask[:, 0, 0, :] > -1e9
            elif mask.dim() == 4:
                token_valid = mask.squeeze(1).squeeze(1) > -1e9
            elif mask.dim() == 3:
                token_valid = mask.squeeze(1).bool()
            elif mask.dim() == 2:
                token_valid = mask.bool()
            else:
                token_valid = None
            if token_valid is not None:
                attn_weights = attn_weights.masked_fill(~token_valid.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1), -1e9)

        attn_weights = self.softpick(attn_weights, dim=-1)
        attn_weights = self.dropout(attn_weights)

        context_land = torch.matmul(attn_weights, V_land_adj)

        # Local window attention
        gate_val = torch.sigmoid(self.gate).view(1, H, 1, 1)
        window_size = self.local_window_size
        idx = torch.arange(N, device=hidden_states.device)
        dist = (idx.unsqueeze(0) - idx.unsqueeze(1)).abs()
        window_mask = (dist <= window_size // 2).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)

        scale = self.head_dim ** -0.5
        local_scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
        local_scores = local_scores.masked_fill(~window_mask, -1e9)
        local_attn = F.softmax(local_scores, dim=-1)
        context_local = torch.matmul(local_attn, V)

        context = gate_val * context_land + (1 - gate_val) * context_local

        context = context.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(B, N, self.all_head_size)
        out = self.out_proj(context)
        return out, None

    def get_ortho_loss(self):
        return 0.0


from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("leka_v3")
def inject_leka_v3(layer, model_config, comp_config, layer_idx, total_layers, old_attn):
    new_attn = LEKAV3Attention(
        config=model_config,
        comp_config=comp_config,
        layer_idx=layer_idx,
        total_layers=total_layers,
        bert_mode=False,
    )
    # Copy pretrained weights
    new_attn.query.weight.data.copy_(old_attn.q_lin.weight.data)
    new_attn.query.bias.data.copy_(old_attn.q_lin.bias.data)
    new_attn.key.weight.data.copy_(old_attn.k_lin.weight.data)
    new_attn.key.bias.data.copy_(old_attn.k_lin.bias.data)
    new_attn.value.weight.data.copy_(old_attn.v_lin.weight.data)
    new_attn.value.bias.data.copy_(old_attn.v_lin.bias.data)
    new_attn.out_proj.weight.data.copy_(old_attn.out_lin.weight.data)
    new_attn.out_proj.bias.data.copy_(old_attn.out_lin.bias.data)
    layer.attention = new_attn
    return new_attn
══════════════════ leka_v1.py ══════════════════
"""
LEKAv1 – Linear‑time Efficient Kernel Attention (basic version)
Pure kernel‑based linear attention (no landmarks).
"""

import math
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Optional, Tuple

class LEKAV1Attention(nn.Module):
    """
    Simple linear attention using random Fourier features + Softpick.
    """
    def __init__(self, config, comp_config, layer_idx, total_layers=6, bert_mode=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_attention_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.attention_head_size = int(config.hidden_size / config.num_attention_heads)
        self.all_head_size = self.num_attention_heads * self.attention_head_size
        self.head_dim = self.attention_head_size
        self.bert_mode = bert_mode
        self.layer_idx = layer_idx

        self.feature_dim = getattr(comp_config, 'feature_dim', 256)

        self.query  = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.key    = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.value  = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(self.all_head_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(getattr(comp_config, 'dropout', 0.1))

        # Random projection matrix (one per head, shared)
        self.register_buffer(
            'proj',
            torch.randn(self.num_attention_heads, self.head_dim, self.feature_dim)
        )

    def transpose_for_scores(self, x):
        new_shape = x.size()[:-1] + (self.num_attention_heads, self.attention_head_size)
        return x.view(new_shape).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)

    def kernel_feature_map(self, x):
        # x: (B, H, N, D)
        proj = self.proj.to(x.device)   # (H, D, F)
        x_proj = torch.einsum('bhnd,hdf->bhnf', x, proj)  # (B, H, N, F)
        phi = torch.cat([torch.cos(x_proj), torch.sin(x_proj)], dim=-1)  # (B, H, N, 2F)
        phi = phi / math.sqrt(self.feature_dim)
        return phi

    @staticmethod
    def softpick(x, dim=-1, temperature=1.0):
        m = x.max(dim=dim, keepdim=True).values
        e = torch.exp((x - m) / temperature)
        denom = e - torch.exp(-m / temperature)
        p = F.relu(denom)
        return p / (p.sum(dim=dim, keepdim=True) + 1e-9)

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, head_mask=None, output_attentions=False, **kwargs):
        B, N, D = hidden_states.shape
        Q = self.transpose_for_scores(self.query(hidden_states))
        K = self.transpose_for_scores(self.key(hidden_states))
        V = self.transpose_for_scores(self.value(hidden_states))

        Q_features = self.kernel_feature_map(Q)   # (B, H, N, 2F)
        K_features = self.kernel_feature_map(K)

        # Linear attention
        KV = torch.einsum('bhnf,bhnd->bhfd', K_features, V)   # (B, H, F, D)
        K_sum = K_features.sum(dim=2, keepdim=True)           # (B, H, 1, F)

        num = torch.einsum('bhnf,bhfd->bhnd', Q_features, KV)
        denom = torch.einsum('bhnf,bhf->bhn', Q_features, K_sum.squeeze(2)).unsqueeze(-1) + 1e-8
        context = num / denom

        if attention_mask is not None:
            # assume 2D mask (B,N) with 1 for keep
            if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
                mask = attention_mask[:, None, None, :].to(context.dtype)
            elif attention_mask.dim() == 4:
                mask = attention_mask[:, 0, 0, :].unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)
            else:
                mask = attention_mask
            context = context * mask

        context = context.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(B, N, self.all_head_size)
        out = self.out_proj(context)
        return out, None

    def get_ortho_loss(self):
        return 0.0


from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("leka_v1")
def inject_leka_v1(layer, model_config, comp_config, layer_idx, total_layers, old_attn):
    new_attn = LEKAV1Attention(
        config=model_config,
        comp_config=comp_config,
        layer_idx=layer_idx,
        total_layers=total_layers,
        bert_mode=False,
    )
    # Copy pretrained weights
    new_attn.query.weight.data.copy_(old_attn.q_lin.weight.data)
    new_attn.query.bias.data.copy_(old_attn.q_lin.bias.data)
    new_attn.key.weight.data.copy_(old_attn.k_lin.weight.data)
    new_attn.key.bias.data.copy_(old_attn.k_lin.bias.data)
    new_attn.value.weight.data.copy_(old_attn.v_lin.weight.data)
    new_attn.value.bias.data.copy_(old_attn.v_lin.bias.data)
    new_attn.out_proj.weight.data.copy_(old_attn.out_lin.weight.data)
    new_attn.out_proj.bias.data.copy_(old_attn.out_lin.bias.data)
    layer.attention = new_attn
    return new_attn
══════════════════ unified_rope.py ══════════════════
# framework/attentions/ssm/unified_rope.py
"""
Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) – pre‑computes cos/sin tables.
Based on RoFormer (https://arxiv.org/abs/2104.09864)
"""

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from ..core.registry import AUX_REGISTRY


@AUX_REGISTRY.register("unified_rope")
class UnifiedRoPE(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, dim: int, max_seq_len: int = 4096, base: float = 10000.0):
        super().__init__()
        self.dim = dim
        self.max_seq_len = max_seq_len
        inv_freq = 1.0 / (base ** (torch.arange(0, dim, 2).float() / dim))
        self.register_buffer("inv_freq", inv_freq)
        self._build_cache(max_seq_len)

    def _build_cache(self, seq_len: int):
        t = torch.arange(seq_len, device=self.inv_freq.device).float()
        freqs = torch.einsum("i,j->ij", t, self.inv_freq)
        emb = torch.cat((freqs, freqs), dim=-1)
        self.register_buffer("cos_cached", emb.cos().unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(2))
        self.register_buffer("sin_cached", emb.sin().unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(2))

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor, offset: int = 0) -> torch.Tensor:
        batch, seq_len, dim = x.shape
        if dim != self.dim:
            raise ValueError(f"Expected dim {self.dim}, got {dim}")
        if seq_len > self.max_seq_len:
            self._build_cache(seq_len)
            self.max_seq_len = seq_len
        cos = self.cos_cached[:, offset:offset+seq_len]
        sin = self.sin_cached[:, offset:offset+seq_len]
        x_even, x_odd = x[..., 0::2], x[..., 1::2]
        out_even = x_even * cos - x_odd * sin
        out_odd = x_odd * cos + x_even * sin
        return torch.stack((out_even, out_odd), dim=-1).flatten(-2)

══════════════════ gated_delta_net.py ══════════════════
# framework/attentions/ssm/gated_delta_net.py
"""
Recurrent Gated DeltaNet layer with learnable forget gate.
Based on ICLR 2025 Gated Delta Networks (simplified recurrent version).
"""

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from ..core.registry import AUX_REGISTRY


@AUX_REGISTRY.register("gated_delta_net")
class GatedDeltaNetLayer(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, d_model: int, use_spectral_norm: bool = False):
        super().__init__()
        self.forget_gate = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)
        self.input_gate = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)
        self.hidden_transform = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)

        if use_spectral_norm:
            nn.utils.spectral_norm(self.forget_gate)
            nn.utils.spectral_norm(self.input_gate)
            nn.utils.spectral_norm(self.hidden_transform)
            nn.utils.spectral_norm(self.out_proj)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor, state: torch.Tensor = None) -> torch.Tensor:
        batch, seq_len, d_model = x.shape
        if state is None:
            state = torch.zeros(batch, d_model, device=x.device)

        outputs = []
        for t in range(seq_len):
            xt = x[:, t]
            f = torch.sigmoid(self.forget_gate(xt))
            i = torch.sigmoid(self.input_gate(xt))
            h_cand = torch.tanh(self.hidden_transform(xt))
            state = f * state + i * h_cand
            outputs.append(self.out_proj(state))

        return torch.stack(outputs, dim=1)
══════════════════ simple_ssm.py ══════════════════
# framework/attentions/ssm/simple_ssm.py
"""
A lightweight State‑Space Model (SSM) block based on the Mamba / S4 architecture.
Features:
- 1D convolution on the input sequence.
- Diagonal state matrix A (learnable).
- Input‑dependent discretisation step (dt).
- Recurrent scan (chunk‑wise for efficiency).
Suitable for hybrid attention‑SSM blocks that expect a simple SSM interface.
"""

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
import math
from ..core.registry import AUX_REGISTRY


@AUX_REGISTRY.register("simple_ssm")
class SimpleSSM(nn.Module):
    """
    Simplified SSM block with convolution, diagonal state, and discretisation.
    Args:
        d_model: feature dimension (hidden size)
        d_state: state dimension (default 16)
        d_conv: convolution kernel size (default 4)
        expand: expansion factor for inner dimension (default 2)
    """
    def __init__(self, d_model: int, d_state: int = 16, d_conv: int = 4, expand: int = 2):
        super().__init__()
        self.d_model = d_model
        self.d_state = d_state
        self.d_conv = d_conv
        inner_dim = d_model * expand

        # Projection
        self.in_proj = nn.Linear(d_model, inner_dim * 2, bias=False)

        # 1D convolution (depthwise)
        self.conv1d = nn.Conv1d(inner_dim, inner_dim, kernel_size=d_conv, groups=inner_dim, padding=d_conv - 1)

        # dt projection (step size)
        self.dt_proj = nn.Linear(inner_dim, d_model, bias=True)

        # State matrix A (diagonal) – learned in log space for stability
        self.A_log = nn.Parameter(torch.log(torch.rand(d_model, d_state) * 0.1))

        # D skip connection
        self.D = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(d_model))

        # Output projection
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(inner_dim, d_model)

        # Activation
        self.act = nn.SiLU()

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Args:
            x: (batch, seq_len, d_model)
        Returns:
            (batch, seq_len, d_model)
        """
        batch, seq_len, d_model = x.shape

        # 1. Project input
        x_proj = self.in_proj(x)                      # (B, L, 2*inner)
        x_ssm, z = x_proj.chunk(2, dim=-1)            # each (B, L, inner)

        # 2. 1D convolution along sequence
        x_ssm = x_ssm.transpose(1, 2)                 # (B, inner, L)
        x_ssm = self.conv1d(x_ssm)[..., :seq_len]     # causal padding: keep only first L
        x_ssm = x_ssm.transpose(1, 2)                 # (B, L, inner)

        # 3. Discretisation step
        dt = F.softplus(self.dt_proj(x_ssm))          # (B, L, d_model)
        A = -torch.exp(self.A_log)                    # (d_model, d_state)

        # 4. Recurrent scan (simplified per‑timestep, can be chunked for speed)
        # For clarity, we use a loop; replace with parallel scan in production
        h = torch.zeros(batch, d_model, self.d_state, device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype)
        outputs = []
        for t in range(seq_len):
            xt = x_ssm[:, t, :]                       # (B, inner) – but we need d_model? Actually inner = d_model*expand
            # For simplicity, we use the first d_model channels as input to SSM
            # In a full implementation we would handle the expanded dimension properly.
            # Here we take a slice to keep dimensions compatible.
            xt_slice = xt[:, :d_model]                # (B, d_model)
            h = h * torch.exp(dt[:, t].unsqueeze(-1) * A) + (xt_slice.unsqueeze(-1) * dt[:, t].unsqueeze(-1))
            out_t = (h * self.D.unsqueeze(-1)).sum(dim=-1)
            outputs.append(out_t)
        out_ssm = torch.stack(outputs, dim=1)          # (B, L, d_model)

        # 5. Gate and output
        out = out_ssm * self.act(z[:, :, :d_model])   # gate with first d_model channels of z
        return self.out_proj(out)
══════════════════ goldfinch_ahn.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from ..core.base import BaseAttention
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

# Placeholder for GatedDeltaNetLayer in case the real module is missing
class GatedDeltaNetLayer(nn.Module):
    """Stub for GatedDeltaNetLayer – replace with actual implementation."""
    def __init__(self, d_model, use_spectral_norm=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.net = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)

    def forward(self, x):
        return self.net(x)

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("goldfinch_ahn")
class GoldFinchAHNBlock(BaseAttention):
    """
    GoldFinch‑AHN block: short‑term sliding window + long‑term GatedDeltaNet memory.
    """
    def __init__(self, config, rank=64, window_size=256):
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.dim = config.hidden_size
        self.window_size = window_size

        self.W_q = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
        self.W_k = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
        self.W_v = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
        self.delta_mem = GatedDeltaNetLayer(self.dim, use_spectral_norm=True)
        self.gate = nn.Linear(self.dim, 1)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim)

    def forward(self, x, mask=None, **kwargs):
        B, L, D = x.shape

        # Short‑term sliding window (causal)
        q = self.W_q(x)
        k = self.W_k(x)
        v = self.W_v(x)
        scores = torch.bmm(q, k.transpose(1, 2)) / (D ** 0.5)

        idx = torch.arange(L, device=x.device)
        causal = idx.unsqueeze(1) >= idx.unsqueeze(0)
        window = (idx.unsqueeze(1) - idx.unsqueeze(0)) < self.window_size
        mask_val = ~(causal & window)
        scores = scores.masked_fill(mask_val.unsqueeze(0), float('-inf'))
        short_out = torch.bmm(F.softmax(scores, dim=-1), v)

        long_out = self.delta_mem(x)          # (B, L, D)
        gate = torch.sigmoid(self.gate(x))    # (B, L, 1)
        out = self.out_proj(gate * short_out + (1 - gate) * long_out)
        return (out, None)
══════════════════ kda_attention.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("kda")
class KDAAttention(nn.Module):
    """Kernelised‑Dynamic‑Decay Attention: per‑head decay linear scan."""
    def __init__(self, dim, num_heads, chunk_size=64):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_heads = num_heads
        self.head_dim = dim // num_heads
        self.q_proj = nn.Linear(dim, dim)
        self.k_proj = nn.Linear(dim, dim)
        self.v_proj = nn.Linear(dim, dim)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(dim, dim)
        self.beta = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(num_heads, 1))

    def forward(self, x):
        B, L, D = x.shape
        q = self.q_proj(x).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
        k = F.normalize(self.k_proj(x).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim), dim=-1)
        v = self.v_proj(x).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)

        h = torch.zeros(B, self.num_heads, self.head_dim, self.head_dim, device=x.device)
        outputs = []
        for t in range(L):
            qt = q[:, t]
            kt = k[:, t]
            vt = v[:, t]
            decay = torch.sigmoid(self.beta).unsqueeze(0)   # (1, H, 1)
            h = decay.unsqueeze(-1) * h + kt.unsqueeze(-1) @ vt.unsqueeze(1)
            out_t = (h @ qt.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)       # (B, H, d)
            outputs.append(out_t)
        out = torch.stack(outputs, dim=2).permute(0, 2, 1).contiguous().view(B, L, D)
        return self.out_proj(out)
══════════════════ hgrn2_gated_deltanet.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY, AUX_REGISTRY

@AUX_REGISTRY.register("hgrn2_layer")
class HGRN2Layer(nn.Module):
    """HGRN‑2 linear recurrence with controlled forgetting."""
    def __init__(self, d_model):
        super().__init__()
        self.forget_gate = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)
        self.input_gate = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)
        self.output = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)
        self.res_clip = 1.0

    def forward(self, x):
        B, L, D = x.shape
        h = torch.zeros(B, D, device=x.device)
        outs = []
        for t in range(L):
            xt = x[:, t]
            f = torch.sigmoid(self.forget_gate(xt))
            i = torch.sigmoid(self.input_gate(xt))
            h = f * h + i * xt
            h = torch.clamp(h, -self.res_clip, self.res_clip)
            outs.append(self.output(h))
        return torch.stack(outs, dim=1)

@AUX_REGISTRY.register("gated_deltanet_block")
class GatedDeltaNetBlock(nn.Module):
    """Block with HGRN2 and optional full attention."""
    def __init__(self, d_model, n_heads, use_full_attention=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.use_full_attention = use_full_attention
        if use_full_attention:
            self.attn = nn.MultiheadAttention(d_model, n_heads, batch_first=True)
        self.hgrn = HGRN2Layer(d_model)
        self.norm = nn.LayerNorm(d_model)

    def forward(self, x):
        if self.use_full_attention:
            attn_out, _ = self.attn(x, x, x)
            x = self.norm(x + attn_out)
        else:
            x = self.norm(x + self.hgrn(x))
        return x

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("hgrn2_gated_deltanet")
class HGRN2GatedDeltaNet(nn.Module):
    """Hybrid model with configurable linear‑to‑full ratio."""
    def __init__(self, d_model, n_heads, n_layers, ratio=3):
        super().__init__()
        self.layers = nn.ModuleList()
        for i in range(n_layers):
            use_full = (i % (ratio+1) == ratio)
            self.layers.append(GatedDeltaNetBlock(d_model, n_heads, use_full_attention=use_full))

    def forward(self, x):
        for layer in self.layers:
            x = layer(x)
        return x

══════════════════ hgrn2_outer.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("hgrn2_outer")
class HGRN2OuterBlock(nn.Module):
    """Outer‑product state update for linear attention."""
    def __init__(self, dim):
        super().__init__()
        self.dim = dim
        self.W_f = nn.Linear(dim, dim)
        self.W_k = nn.Linear(dim, dim)
        self.W_v = nn.Linear(dim, dim)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(dim, dim)

    def forward(self, x, state=None):
        B, L, D = x.shape
        if state is None:
            state = torch.zeros(B, D, D, device=x.device)
        outputs = []
        for t in range(L):
            xt = x[:, t]
            f = torch.sigmoid(self.W_f(xt))
            k = self.W_k(xt)
            v = self.W_v(xt)
            state = f.unsqueeze(-1) * state + (1 - f.unsqueeze(-1)) * (k.unsqueeze(-1) @ v.unsqueeze(1))
            yt = (state @ xt.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)
            outputs.append(yt)
        out = torch.stack(outputs, dim=1)
        return self.out_proj(out), state

══════════════════ upi_stuffed_mamba.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("upi_stuffed_mamba")
class UPIMambaBlock(nn.Module):
    """DPLR selective scan with UPI stabilization."""
    def __init__(self, dim, d_state=16, d_conv=4, expand=2):
        super().__init__()
        self.dim = dim
        self.d_state = d_state
        self.expand = expand
        inner_dim = dim * expand
        self.in_proj = nn.Linear(dim, inner_dim * 2)
        self.conv1d = nn.Conv1d(inner_dim, inner_dim, d_conv, groups=inner_dim, padding=d_conv-1)
        self.A_log = nn.Parameter(torch.log(torch.rand(dim, d_state) * 0.1))
        self.D = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(dim))
        self.dt_proj = nn.Linear(inner_dim, dim)
        self.gamma = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(1))
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(inner_dim, dim)

    def forward(self, x):
        B, L, D = x.shape
        x_proj = self.in_proj(x)                     # (B, L, 2*inner)
        x_ssm, z = x_proj.chunk(2, dim=-1)           # each (B, L, inner)
        x_ssm = self.conv1d(x_ssm.transpose(1,2))[..., :L].transpose(1,2)
        dt = F.softplus(self.dt_proj(x_ssm))         # (B, L, dim)
        A = -torch.exp(self.A_log)                   # (dim, d_state)

        # Recurrent scan over the first `dim` channels (simplified)
        x_ssm_trunc = x_ssm[:, :, :self.dim]         # use first dim channels
        h = torch.zeros(B, self.dim, self.d_state, device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype)
        outputs = []
        for t in range(L):
            xt = x_ssm_trunc[:, t]                    # (B, dim)
            h = h * torch.exp(dt[:, t].unsqueeze(-1) * A.unsqueeze(0)) + \
                (xt.unsqueeze(-1) * dt[:, t].unsqueeze(-1))
            h = h - self.gamma * h                   # UPI stabilization
            out_t = (h * self.D.unsqueeze(-1)).sum(dim=-1)   # (B, dim)
            outputs.append(out_t)
        out_ssm = torch.stack(outputs, dim=1)         # (B, L, dim)

        # Pad ssm output back to inner_dim and combine with gate
        out_ssm_full = F.pad(out_ssm, (0, x_ssm.size(-1) - self.dim))
        out = out_ssm_full * F.silu(z)
        return self.out_proj(out)

══════════════════ granite_hybrid.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

class Mamba2(nn.Module):
    """Simplified Mamba‑2 stub (replace with official mamba_ssm)."""
    def __init__(self, d_model, d_state=16, d_conv=4, expand=2):
        super().__init__()
        self.d_model = d_model
        self.expand = expand
        inner_dim = d_model * expand
        self.in_proj = nn.Linear(d_model, inner_dim * 2)
        self.conv1d = nn.Conv1d(inner_dim, inner_dim, d_conv, groups=inner_dim, padding=d_conv-1)
        self.dt_proj = nn.Linear(inner_dim, d_model)
        self.A_log = nn.Parameter(torch.log(torch.rand(d_model, d_state) * 0.1))
        self.D = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(d_model))
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(inner_dim, d_model)

    def forward(self, x):
        B, L, D = x.shape
        x_proj = self.in_proj(x)            # (B, L, 2*inner)
        x_ssm, z = x_proj.chunk(2, dim=-1)  # each (B, L, inner)
        x_ssm = self.conv1d(x_ssm.transpose(1,2))[..., :L].transpose(1,2)
        dt = F.softplus(self.dt_proj(x_ssm))   # (B, L, D)
        A = -torch.exp(self.A_log)             # (D, d_state)
        # Recurrent scan (slow but functional)
        h = torch.zeros(B, D, self.d_state, device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype)
        outputs = []
        for t in range(L):
            xt = x_ssm[:, t, :self.d_model]   # take first d_model channels as input
            h = h * torch.exp(dt[:, t].unsqueeze(-1) * A) + (xt.unsqueeze(-1) * dt[:, t].unsqueeze(-1))
            out_t = (h * self.D.unsqueeze(-1)).sum(dim=-1)
            outputs.append(out_t)
        out = torch.stack(outputs, dim=1) * F.silu(z)
        return self.out_proj(out)

class MixtureOfExperts(nn.Module):
    """Top‑k token‑choice MoE."""
    def __init__(self, dim, ff_dim, num_experts, top_k=2):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_experts = num_experts
        self.top_k = top_k
        self.experts = nn.ModuleList([nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(dim, ff_dim),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Linear(ff_dim, dim)
        ) for _ in range(num_experts)])
        self.router = nn.Linear(dim, num_experts)

    def forward(self, x):
        B, L, D = x.shape
        scores = self.router(x)            # (B, L, E)
        weights, indices = torch.topk(scores, self.top_k, dim=-1)
        weights = F.softmax(weights, dim=-1)
        out = torch.zeros_like(x)
        for e in range(self.num_experts):
            mask = (indices == e).any(dim=-1)   # (B, L)
            if mask.any():
                selected_weights = (weights * (indices == e).float()).sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
                out += mask.unsqueeze(-1) * selected_weights * self.experts[e](x * mask.unsqueeze(-1))
        return out

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("granite_hybrid")
class GraniteHybridBlock(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, dim, num_experts=8, top_k=2, ratio=9):
        super().__init__()
        self.mamba = Mamba2(d_model=dim)
        self.attn = nn.MultiheadAttention(dim, 8, batch_first=True)
        self.moe = MixtureOfExperts(dim, dim * 4, num_experts, top_k)
        self.ratio = ratio
        self.register_buffer('counter', torch.tensor(0, dtype=torch.long))   # use buffer for state

    def forward(self, x):
        self.counter += 1
        if self.counter % (self.ratio + 1) < self.ratio:
            x = self.mamba(x)
        else:
            x, _ = self.attn(x, x, x)
        x = x + self.moe(x)
        return x
══════════════════ hymba_transx.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from ..core.base import BaseAttention
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY
from ..ssm.simple_ssm import SimpleSSM    # assumed available
import torch
import torch.nn as nn

class UnifiedRoPE(nn.Module):
    """
    Unified Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) that can be applied to any hidden states.
    Rotates half of the feature dimensions based on position indices.
    """
    def __init__(self, dim, max_seq_len=4096, base=10000.0):
        super().__init__()
        self.dim = dim
        self.max_seq_len = max_seq_len
        self.base = base
        
        # Precompute frequency bands: shape (dim//2,)
        inv_freq = 1.0 / (base ** (torch.arange(0, dim, 2).float() / dim))
        self.register_buffer("inv_freq", inv_freq)
        
        # Precompute cos and sin tables for all positions up to max_seq_len
        self._build_cache(max_seq_len)
    
    def _build_cache(self, seq_len):
        """Precompute cos and sin for positions 0..seq_len-1."""
        t = torch.arange(seq_len, device=self.inv_freq.device).float()
        freqs = torch.einsum("i,j->ij", t, self.inv_freq)          # (seq_len, dim//2)
        emb = torch.cat((freqs, freqs), dim=-1)                    # (seq_len, dim)
        self.register_buffer("cos_cached", emb.cos().unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(2))   # (1, seq_len, 1, dim)
        self.register_buffer("sin_cached", emb.sin().unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(2))
    
    def forward(self, x, offset=0):
        """
        Apply RoPE to input tensor.
        
        Args:
            x: (batch_size, seq_len, hidden_dim)
            offset: starting position offset (for incremental decoding)
        Returns:
            Rotated tensor of same shape.
        """
        batch, seq_len, dim = x.shape
        assert dim == self.dim, f"Expected input dim {self.dim}, got {dim}"
        
        if seq_len > self.max_seq_len:
            self._build_cache(seq_len)
            self.max_seq_len = seq_len
        
        # Get cos and sin for the required positions
        cos = self.cos_cached[:, offset:offset+seq_len, :, :]   # (1, seq_len, 1, dim)
        sin = self.sin_cached[:, offset:offset+seq_len, :, :]
        
        # Reshape input to separate even and odd channels
        x_even = x[..., 0::2]
        x_odd = x[..., 1::2]
        # Stack to apply rotation: (x_even, x_odd) -> (x_even * cos - x_odd * sin, x_odd * cos + x_even * sin)
        rotated_even = x_even * cos.squeeze(2)[..., 0::2] - x_odd * sin.squeeze(2)[..., 0::2]
        rotated_odd = x_odd * cos.squeeze(2)[..., 0::2] + x_even * sin.squeeze(2)[..., 0::2]
        # Interleave back
        rotated = torch.stack((rotated_even, rotated_odd), dim=-1).flatten(-2)
        return rotated
    
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("hymba_transx")
class HymbaTransXBlock(BaseAttention):
    """Unified RoPE, differential attention, and SSM fusion."""
    def __init__(self, config, rank=64, num_heads=None):
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.dim = config.hidden_size
        self.num_heads = num_heads or config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = self.dim // self.num_heads

        self.rope = UnifiedRoPE(self.dim)
        self.W_q1 = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
        self.W_k1 = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
        self.W_q2 = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
        self.W_k2 = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
        self.W_v  = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
        self.mamba = SimpleSSM(self.dim)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim)
        self.alpha = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(0.5))

    def forward(self, x, mask=None, **kwargs):
        B, L, D = x.shape
        x_rope = self.rope(x)

        q1 = self.W_q1(x_rope).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1,2)
        k1 = self.W_k1(x_rope).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1,2)
        q2 = self.W_q2(x_rope).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1,2)
        k2 = self.W_k2(x_rope).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1,2)
        v  = self.W_v(x_rope).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1,2)

        scale = self.head_dim ** -0.5
        attn1 = F.softmax(torch.matmul(q1, k1.transpose(-2,-1)) * scale, dim=-1)
        attn2 = F.softmax(torch.matmul(q2, k2.transpose(-2,-1)) * scale, dim=-1)
        diff_attn = F.relu(attn1 - 0.5 * attn2)
        A_out = torch.matmul(diff_attn, v).transpose(1,2).contiguous().view(B, L, D)

        M_out = self.mamba(x_rope)
        alpha = torch.sigmoid(self.alpha)
        out = self.out_proj(x + alpha * A_out + (1 - alpha) * M_out)
        return (out, None)
══════════════════ griffin_ssd.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from ..core.base import BaseAttention
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

def ssd_kernel(q, k, v):
    """Causal linear attention (SSD‑style simplified). q,k,v: (B, L, D)."""
    B, L, D = q.shape
    h = torch.zeros(B, D, D, device=q.device, dtype=q.dtype)
    outputs = []
    for t in range(L):
        kt = k[:, t]
        vt = v[:, t]
        qt = q[:, t]
        h = h + kt.unsqueeze(-1) @ vt.unsqueeze(1)   # outer product
        out_t = (qt.unsqueeze(1) @ h).squeeze(1)     # (B, D)
        outputs.append(out_t)
    return torch.stack(outputs, dim=1)

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("griffin_ssd")
class GriffinSSDBlock(BaseAttention):
    """Local sliding window + global SSD recurrence."""
    def __init__(self, config, rank=64, window_size=256):
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.dim = config.hidden_size
        self.window_size = window_size

        self.W_q = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
        self.W_k = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
        self.W_v = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
        self.ssd_proj = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim)

    def forward(self, x, mask=None, **kwargs):
        B, L, D = x.shape

        # Local sliding window (causal)
        q_local = self.W_q(x)
        k_local = self.W_k(x)
        v_local = self.W_v(x)
        scores = torch.bmm(q_local, k_local.transpose(1,2)) / (D**0.5)
        idx = torch.arange(L, device=x.device)
        causal = idx.unsqueeze(1) >= idx.unsqueeze(0)
        window = (idx.unsqueeze(1) - idx.unsqueeze(0)) < self.window_size
        local_mask = ~(causal & window)
        scores = scores.masked_fill(local_mask.unsqueeze(0), float('-inf'))
        local_out = torch.bmm(F.softmax(scores, dim=-1), v_local)

        # Global linear attention
        q_global = self.ssd_proj(x)
        global_out = ssd_kernel(q_global, self.W_k(x), self.W_v(x))

        return self.out_proj(local_out + global_out), None
══════════════════ jamba_info.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from ..core.base import BaseAttention
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY
from ..ssm.simple_ssm import SimpleSSM   # assumed

class InfoMambaConceptBottleneck(nn.Module):
    """Minimal‑bandwidth concept bottleneck."""
    def __init__(self, dim, bottleneck_dim=64):
        super().__init__()
        self.W_down = nn.Linear(dim, bottleneck_dim, bias=False)
        self.W_up = nn.Linear(bottleneck_dim, dim, bias=False)
    def forward(self, x):
        return self.W_up(F.gelu(self.W_down(x)))

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("jamba_info")
class JambaInfoBlock(BaseAttention):
    def __init__(self, config, rank=64, mode="retrieval", sparsity=0.15):
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.dim = config.hidden_size
        self.mode = mode
        if mode == "retrieval":
            self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
            self.head_dim = self.dim // self.num_heads
            self.active_heads = max(1, int(self.num_heads * sparsity))
            self.W_q1 = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
            self.W_k1 = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
            self.W_q2 = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
            self.W_k2 = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
            self.W_v = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
            self.out_proj = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim)
        else:
            self.bottleneck = InfoMambaConceptBottleneck(self.dim)
            self.ssm = SimpleSSM(self.dim)
            self.out_proj = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim)

    def forward(self, x, mask=None, **kwargs):
        B, L, D = x.shape
        if self.mode == "retrieval":
            q1 = self.W_q1(x).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1,2)
            k1 = self.W_k1(x).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1,2)
            q2 = self.W_q2(x).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1,2)
            k2 = self.W_k2(x).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1,2)
            v  = self.W_v(x).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1,2)
            scale = self.head_dim ** -0.5
            attn1 = F.softmax(torch.matmul(q1, k1.transpose(-2,-1)) * scale, dim=-1)
            attn2 = F.softmax(torch.matmul(q2, k2.transpose(-2,-1)) * scale, dim=-1)
            diff_attn = attn1 - 0.5 * attn2
            diff_attn[:, self.active_heads:] = 0
            out = torch.matmul(diff_attn, v).transpose(1,2).contiguous().view(B, L, D)
        else:
            out = x + self.ssm(self.bottleneck(x))
        return self.out_proj(out), None
══════════════════ samba_moe.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from .samba_m3 import SAMBAM3Block
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY, AUX_REGISTRY

@AUX_REGISTRY.register("reap_moe")
class REAPMoE(nn.Module):
    """Token‑choice MoE with top‑2 gating."""
    def __init__(self, d_model, n_experts=8, top_k=2):
        super().__init__()
        self.n_experts = n_experts
        self.top_k = top_k
        self.experts = nn.ModuleList([nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(d_model, d_model*4),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Linear(d_model*4, d_model)
        ) for _ in range(n_experts)])
        self.router = nn.Linear(d_model, n_experts)

    def forward(self, x):
        B, L, D = x.shape
        scores = self.router(x)
        weights, indices = torch.topk(scores, self.top_k, dim=-1)
        weights = F.softmax(weights, dim=-1)
        out = torch.zeros_like(x)
        for e in range(self.n_experts):
            mask = (indices == e).any(dim=-1)
            if mask.any():
                selected_weights = (weights * (indices == e).float()).sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
                out += selected_weights * self.experts[e](x * mask.unsqueeze(-1))
        return out

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("samba_moe")
class SambaMoEBlock(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, d_model, n_heads, n_experts=8, window=128):
        super().__init__()
        self.swa_mamba = SAMBAM3Block(d_model, n_heads, window=window)
        self.moe = REAPMoE(d_model, n_experts)
        self.norm = nn.LayerNorm(d_model)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = self.swa_mamba(x)
        x = self.norm(x + self.moe(x))
        return x

══════════════════ samba_m3.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from .jamba2 import Mamba   # import the stub or replace with official Mamba
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY, AUX_REGISTRY

@AUX_REGISTRY.register("spectral_mamba")
class SpectralMamba(nn.Module):
    """Mamba with learnable spectral scaling."""
    def __init__(self, d_model):
        super().__init__()
        self.mamba = Mamba(d_model)
        self.dt_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(d_model))

    def forward(self, x):
        out = self.mamba(x)
        out = out * torch.sigmoid(self.dt_scale).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)
        return out

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("samba_m3")
class SAMBAM3Block(nn.Module):
    """Sliding window attention + spectral Mamba."""
    def __init__(self, d_model, n_heads, window=128):
        super().__init__()
        self.window_size = window
        self.attn = nn.MultiheadAttention(d_model, n_heads, batch_first=True)
        self.mamba = SpectralMamba(d_model)
        self.norm1 = nn.LayerNorm(d_model)
        self.norm2 = nn.LayerNorm(d_model)

    def sliding_window_mask(self, L):
        idx = torch.arange(L)
        causal = idx.unsqueeze(1) >= idx.unsqueeze(0)
        window = (idx.unsqueeze(1) - idx.unsqueeze(0)) < self.window_size
        mask = ~(causal & window)
        return mask.float().masked_fill(mask, float('-inf')).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)

    def forward(self, x):
        B, L, D = x.shape
        mask = self.sliding_window_mask(L).to(x.device)
        attn_out, _ = self.attn(x, x, x, attn_mask=mask)
        x = self.norm1(x + attn_out)
        x = self.norm2(x + self.mamba(x))
        return x

══════════════════ jamba2.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY, AUX_REGISTRY

@AUX_REGISTRY.register("mamba")
class Mamba(nn.Module):
    """Trivial Mamba placeholder – replace with official implementation."""
    def __init__(self, d_model):
        super().__init__()
        self.d_model = d_model
        self.in_proj = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model*2)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)
    def forward(self, x):
        return self.out_proj(F.silu(self.in_proj(x)))

@AUX_REGISTRY.register("expert_choice_moe")
class ExpertChoiceMoE(nn.Module):
    """Top‑2 token‑choice MoE."""
    def __init__(self, d_model, n_experts=8, top_k=2):
        super().__init__()
        self.n_experts = n_experts
        self.top_k = top_k
        self.experts = nn.ModuleList([nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(d_model, d_model*4),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Linear(d_model*4, d_model)
        ) for _ in range(n_experts)])
        self.router = nn.Linear(d_model, n_experts)

    def forward(self, x):
        B, L, D = x.shape
        scores = self.router(x)
        weights, indices = torch.topk(scores, self.top_k, dim=-1)
        weights = F.softmax(weights, dim=-1)
        out = torch.zeros_like(x)
        for e in range(self.n_experts):
            mask = (indices == e).any(dim=-1)
            if mask.any():
                selected_weights = (weights * (indices == e).float()).sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
                out += selected_weights * self.experts[e](x * mask.unsqueeze(-1))
        return out

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("jamba2")
class Jamba2Block(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, d_model, n_heads, n_experts=8, top_k=2):
        super().__init__()
        self.mamba = Mamba(d_model)
        self.attn = nn.MultiheadAttention(d_model, n_heads, batch_first=True)
        self.moe = ExpertChoiceMoE(d_model, n_experts, top_k)
        self.norm1 = nn.LayerNorm(d_model)
        self.norm2 = nn.LayerNorm(d_model)
        self.norm3 = nn.LayerNorm(d_model)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = self.norm1(x + self.mamba(x))
        attn_out, _ = self.attn(x, x, x)
        x = self.norm2(x + attn_out)
        x = self.norm3(x + self.moe(x))
        return x

══════════════════ halo_regla.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

# ---- simple RoPE helpers ----
def rotate_half(x):
    x1, x2 = x.chunk(2, dim=-1)
    return torch.cat((-x2, x1), dim=-1)

class RotaryEmbedding(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, dim, max_seq_len=4096):
        super().__init__()
        inv_freq = 1.0 / (10000 ** (torch.arange(0, dim, 2).float() / dim))
        t = torch.arange(max_seq_len).float()
        freqs = torch.einsum('i,j->ij', t, inv_freq)   # (L, dim/2)
        emb = torch.cat((freqs, freqs), dim=-1)
        self.register_buffer('cos_cached', emb.cos())
        self.register_buffer('sin_cached', emb.sin())

    def forward(self, x, offset=0):
        L = x.shape[1]
        cos = self.cos_cached[offset:offset+L].unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(2)
        sin = self.sin_cached[offset:offset+L].unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(2)
        return (x * cos) + (rotate_half(x) * sin)

# ---- Linear attention layer (ReGLA‑style) ----
class ReGLALayer(nn.Module):
    """ReLU‑kernel linear attention."""
    def __init__(self, dim, heads=8):
        super().__init__()
        self.heads = heads
        self.head_dim = dim // heads
        self.q_proj = nn.Linear(dim, dim)
        self.k_proj = nn.Linear(dim, dim)
        self.v_proj = nn.Linear(dim, dim)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(dim, dim)

    def forward(self, x):
        B, L, D = x.shape
        q = F.relu(self.q_proj(x)).view(B, L, self.heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1,2)
        k = F.relu(self.k_proj(x)).view(B, L, self.heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1,2)
        v = self.v_proj(x).view(B, L, self.heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1,2)
        kv = torch.einsum('bhld,bhlm->bhldm', k, v)   # (B, H, d, d)
        z = k.cumsum(dim=2)                           # (B, H, L, d)
        num = torch.einsum('bhld,bhldm->bhldm', q, kv)
        den = torch.einsum('bhld,bhld->bhl', q, z) + 1e-8
        out = (num.sum(-1) / den.unsqueeze(-1)).transpose(1,2).contiguous().view(B, L, D)
        return self.out_proj(out)

# ---- Standard Transformer layer (with RoPE placeholder) ----
class TransformerLayer(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, dim, heads=8):
        super().__init__()
        self.attn = nn.MultiheadAttention(dim, heads, batch_first=True)
        self.rope = RotaryEmbedding(dim)
        self.ffn = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(dim, dim*4),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Linear(dim*4, dim)
        )
        self.norm1 = nn.LayerNorm(dim)
        self.norm2 = nn.LayerNorm(dim)

    def forward(self, x):
        # RoPE not integrated into MHA for simplicity; a real implementation would apply it
        attn_out, _ = self.attn(x, x, x)
        x = self.norm1(x + attn_out)
        x = self.norm2(x + self.ffn(x))
        return x

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("halo_regla")
class HALOReGLAStudent(nn.Module):
    """Hybrid model alternating linear (ReGLA) and full Transformer layers."""
    def __init__(self, dim, num_layers, hybrid_ratio=4):
        super().__init__()
        self.layers = nn.ModuleList()
        for i in range(num_layers):
            if i % hybrid_ratio == 0:
                self.layers.append(ReGLALayer(dim))
            else:
                self.layers.append(TransformerLayer(dim))

    def forward(self, x):
        for layer in self.layers:
            x = layer(x)
        return x
══════════════════ __init__.py ══════════════════
from .gqa_attention import GroupedQueryAttention
from .mla_attention import MLAAttention
from .qcqa_attention import QCQAAttention
from .wgqa_attention import WeightedGroupedQueryAttention
══════════════════ gqa_attention.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import math
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("gqa")
class GroupedQueryAttention(nn.Module):
    """
    Grouped-Query Attention (GQA).
    Shares a smaller number of key/value heads across multiple query heads.
    """
    def __init__(self, config, num_kv_heads=4):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.num_kv_heads = num_kv_heads
        self.head_dim = config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        # Projections
        self.q_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.num_heads * self.head_dim)
        self.k_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim)
        self.v_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(self.num_heads * self.head_dim, config.hidden_size)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(config.attention_probs_dropout_prob)

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, **kwargs):
        batch_size, seq_len, _ = hidden_states.shape

        # Project Q (full heads), K/V (fewer heads)
        q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
        k = self.k_proj(hidden_states).view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
        v = self.v_proj(hidden_states).view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)

        # Replicate K/V heads to match Q head count
        repeat_factor = self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads
        k = k.repeat_interleave(repeat_factor, dim=2)   # (B, L, num_heads, D)
        v = v.repeat_interleave(repeat_factor, dim=2)

        # Standard scaled dot‑product attention
        q = q.transpose(1, 2)      # (B, num_heads, L, D)
        k = k.transpose(1, 2)
        v = v.transpose(1, 2)

        scores = torch.matmul(q, k.transpose(-2, -1)) / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        if attention_mask is not None:
            scores = scores + attention_mask.unsqueeze(1)

        attn_probs = torch.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
        attn_probs = self.dropout(attn_probs)

        context = torch.matmul(attn_probs, v)                     # (B, num_heads, L, D)
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(batch_size, seq_len, -1)
        out = self.out_proj(context)

        return out, attn_probs   # returns attention weights for inspection
══════════════════ mla_attention.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import math
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("mla")
class MLAAttention(nn.Module):
    """
    Multi-Head Latent Attention (MLA) – DeepSeek‑V2.
    Compresses the KV cache into a low‑rank latent space.
    """
    def __init__(self, config, rank=128):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.rank = rank

        # Query projection (standard)
        self.q_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size, bias=False)

        # Low‑rank KV bottleneck
        self.kv_down = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, rank, bias=False)   # compress
        self.k_up = nn.Linear(rank, config.hidden_size, bias=False)      # expand to key
        self.v_up = nn.Linear(rank, config.hidden_size, bias=False)      # expand to value

        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(config.attention_probs_dropout_prob)

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, **kwargs):
        batch_size, seq_len, _ = hidden_states.shape

        # Queries – standard projection
        q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)

        # Compress into latent vector
        latent = self.kv_down(hidden_states)            # (B, L, rank)
        k = self.k_up(latent).view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
        v = self.v_up(latent).view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)

        # Rearrange for attention
        q = q.transpose(1, 2)    # (B, num_heads, L, D)
        k = k.transpose(1, 2)
        v = v.transpose(1, 2)

        scores = torch.matmul(q, k.transpose(-2, -1)) / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        if attention_mask is not None:
            scores = scores + attention_mask.unsqueeze(1)

        attn_probs = torch.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
        attn_probs = self.dropout(attn_probs)

        context = torch.matmul(attn_probs, v)
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(batch_size, seq_len, -1)
        out = self.out_proj(context)

        return out, attn_probs
══════════════════ wgqa_attention.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import math
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("wgqa")
class WeightedGroupedQueryAttention(nn.Module):
    """
    Weighted Grouped-Query Attention (WGQA).
    Fixed grouping + learnable per‑channel importance weights on K/V heads.
    """
    def __init__(self, config, num_kv_heads=4):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.num_kv_heads = num_kv_heads
        self.head_dim = config.hidden_size // self.num_heads

        # Learnable weights for KV heads (broadcast across sequence & batch)
        self.k_weights = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(self.num_kv_heads, 1))   # (H_kv, 1)
        self.v_weights = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(self.num_kv_heads, 1))

        # Projections
        self.q_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.num_heads * self.head_dim)
        self.k_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim)
        self.v_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(self.num_heads * self.head_dim, config.hidden_size)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(config.attention_probs_dropout_prob)

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, **kwargs):
        batch_size, seq_len, _ = hidden_states.shape

        q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)

        k = self.k_proj(hidden_states).view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
        v = self.v_proj(hidden_states).view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)

        # Apply per‑head importance: multiply each head by its scalar weight
        # weights have shape (H_kv, 1) -> (1,1,H_kv,1) after unsqueezing
        k = k * self.k_weights.view(1, 1, self.num_kv_heads, 1)
        v = v * self.v_weights.view(1, 1, self.num_kv_heads, 1)

        # Repeat to match query heads (fixed grouping)
        repeat_factor = self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads
        k = k.repeat_interleave(repeat_factor, dim=2)
        v = v.repeat_interleave(repeat_factor, dim=2)

        q = q.transpose(1, 2)   # (B, num_heads, L, D)
        k = k.transpose(1, 2)
        v = v.transpose(1, 2)

        scores = torch.matmul(q, k.transpose(-2, -1)) / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        if attention_mask is not None:
            scores = scores + attention_mask.unsqueeze(1)

        attn_probs = torch.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
        attn_probs = self.dropout(attn_probs)

        context = torch.matmul(attn_probs, v)
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(batch_size, seq_len, -1)
        out = self.out_proj(context)

        return out, attn_probs
══════════════════ qcqa_attention.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
import math
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("qcqa")
class QCQAAttention(nn.Module):
    """
    Quality & Capacity‑aware Grouped Query Attention.
    Dynamically mixes KV heads per query head using a learned router.
    No fixed grouping – each query head soft‑selects which KV heads to attend to.
    """
    def __init__(self, config, num_kv_heads=4, router_temp=1.0):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.num_kv_heads = num_kv_heads
        self.head_dim = config.hidden_size // self.num_heads

        # Projections: Q full heads, K/V compressed to num_kv_heads
        self.q_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.num_heads * self.head_dim)
        self.k_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim)
        self.v_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim)

        # Routing: for each query head, compute a score over KV heads
        self.router = nn.Linear(self.head_dim, self.num_kv_heads, bias=False)
        self.router_temp = router_temp

        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(self.num_heads * self.head_dim, config.hidden_size)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(config.attention_probs_dropout_prob)

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, **kwargs):
        batch_size, seq_len, _ = hidden_states.shape

        # Project Q, K, V
        q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)  # (B, L, H_q, D)
        k = self.k_proj(hidden_states).view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)  # (B, L, H_kv, D)
        v = self.v_proj(hidden_states).view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)

        # Compute routing weights from queries (including a learned quality metric)
        # q: (B, L, H_q, D) -> route_logits: (B, L, H_q, H_kv)
        route_logits = self.router(q) / self.router_temp
        route_weights = F.softmax(route_logits, dim=-1)          # (B, L, H_q, H_kv)

        # Mix K and V per query head: (B, L, H_q, H_kv) x (B, L, H_kv, D) -> (B, L, H_q, D)
        # Using einsum for clarity
        k_mixed = torch.einsum('blhn,bmnd->bhlmd', route_weights, k)   # (B, H_q, L, H_kv, D) -> sum over H_kv later
        # Better: reshape and matmul
        # route_weights: (B, L, H_q, H_kv) -> (B*L, H_q, H_kv)
        # k: (B, L, H_kv, D) -> (B*L, H_kv, D)
        # mixed_k = (B*L, H_q, H_kv) @ (B*L, H_kv, D) = (B*L, H_q, D)
        B, L, H_q, H_kv = route_weights.shape
        k_flat = k.reshape(B*L, H_kv, self.head_dim)                     # (B*L, H_kv, D)
        w_flat = route_weights.reshape(B*L, H_q, H_kv)                   # (B*L, H_q, H_kv)
        k_mixed_flat = torch.bmm(w_flat, k_flat)                         # (B*L, H_q, D)
        k_mixed = k_mixed_flat.reshape(B, L, H_q, self.head_dim)         # (B, L, H_q, D)

        v_flat = v.reshape(B*L, H_kv, self.head_dim)
        v_mixed_flat = torch.bmm(w_flat, v_flat)                         # (B*L, H_q, D)
        v_mixed = v_mixed_flat.reshape(B, L, H_q, self.head_dim)

        # Now standard multi‑head attention with the mixed K/V
        q = q.transpose(1, 2)         # (B, H_q, L, D)
        k_mixed = k_mixed.transpose(1, 2)
        v_mixed = v_mixed.transpose(1, 2)

        scores = torch.matmul(q, k_mixed.transpose(-2, -1)) / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        if attention_mask is not None:
            scores = scores + attention_mask.unsqueeze(1)

        attn_probs = torch.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
        attn_probs = self.dropout(attn_probs)

        context = torch.matmul(attn_probs, v_mixed)                # (B, H_q, L, D)
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, -1)
        out = self.out_proj(context)

        return out, attn_probs
══════════════════ registry.py ══════════════════
# framework/attentions/core/registry.py
from functools import lru_cache

class Registry:
    def __init__(self, name):
        self.name = name
        self._dict = {}
        self._keys_cache = None
        self._cache_version = 0

    def register(self, key=None):
        def decorator(obj):
            nonlocal key
            if key is None:
                key = obj.__name__
            self._dict[key] = obj
            self._cache_version += 1  # Invalidate cache on new registration
            self._keys_cache = None
            return obj
        return decorator

    def get(self, key):
        return self._dict.get(key)

    def keys(self):
        # Cache the keys list to avoid repeated list() conversions
        if self._keys_cache is None:
            self._keys_cache = list(self._dict.keys())
        return self._keys_cache

    def __contains__(self, key):
        return key in self._dict

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self._dict)

    def __repr__(self):
        return f"Registry('{self.name}', entries={list(self._dict.keys())})"


# Core registries for the efficient attention framework
ATTENTION_REGISTRY = Registry("attentions")      # attention modules (Nexus, LEKA, AURORA, etc.)
AUX_REGISTRY = Registry("auxiliary")             # auxiliary modules (RoPE, SSM, DeltaNet, token pruning, etc.)
COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY = Registry("compressors")    # compression strategies (SVD, SLiM, NMF, etc.)
METHOD_REGISTRY = Registry("methods")            # training methods (LoRA, R‑Drop, R3F, Fisher, etc.)

# Optional (if your framework grows)
MODEL_REGISTRY = Registry("models")              # different base models (DistilBERT, BERT, RoBERTa)
DATASET_REGISTRY = Registry("datasets")          # GLUE, SuperGLUE, custom tasks
LOSS_REGISTRY = Registry("losses")               # custom loss functions

══════════════════ utils.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F

def softpick(x: torch.Tensor, dim: int = -1, temperature: float = 1.0) -> torch.Tensor:
    """
    Rectified softmax — ~47% sparse, no attention sink.
    Reference: Zuhri et al., arXiv 2504.20966 (Apr 2025).
    """
    m = x.max(dim=dim, keepdim=True).values
    e = torch.exp((x - m) / temperature)
    denom = e - torch.exp(-m / temperature)
    p = F.relu(denom)
    return p / (p.sum(dim=dim, keepdim=True) + 1e-9)

def select_rank_svd(W: torch.Tensor, energy: float, min_r: int = 4) -> int:
    """
    Energy‑thresholded rank selection from singular values.
    
    Args:
        W: Weight matrix or tensor to analyze.
        energy: Cumulative energy threshold (0.0 to 1.0).
        min_r: Minimum allowable rank.
        
    Returns:
        int: Selected rank.
    """
    with torch.no_grad():
        # Ensure we are on CPU/float for SVD if needed, though torch.linalg.svd is usually fine
        _, S, _ = torch.linalg.svd(W.float(), full_matrices=False)
        total = (S ** 2).sum()
        cum = torch.cumsum(S ** 2, dim=0) / total
        r = int(torch.searchsorted(cum, energy).item()) + 1
        return max(min_r, min(r, min(W.shape[0], W.shape[1])))

def transpose_for_scores(x: torch.Tensor, num_heads: int, head_dim: int) -> torch.Tensor:
    """
    (B, N, all_head_size) → (B, num_heads, N, head_dim)
    """
    new_shape = x.size()[:-1] + (num_heads, head_dim)
    x = x.view(*new_shape)
    return x.permute(0, 2, 1, 3)

"""
NEBULA attention module – 18 compression techniques unified.
- SVD low‑rank projections
- ARMOR block‑diagonal structured sparsity
- AST (Adaptive Sparse Token pruning)
- OATS (token triage: critical / marginal)
- SLA (Scatterbrain‑like linear attention for marginal tokens)
- Softpick (sparse rectified softmax for critical tokens)
- Sliding window local attention
- DeltaKV (residual KV compression)
- KVT (PCA quantisation of K/V)
- Block‑sparse routing (top‑k inter‑block)
- Dual‑pathway gating
- Energy‑threshold rank selection per projection
- Learnable scaling for Q/K
- Periodic dense rectification
- LoRA adaptation
- Channel‑wise gating
- Token‑level importance scoring
- Subspace projection
"""

import math
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Optional, Tuple, List
from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass
class NEBULAConfig:
    hidden_size: int = 768
    num_heads: int = 12
    head_dim: int = 64
    dropout: float = 0.1

    # Projection compression
    svd_enabled: bool = True
    svd_energy: float = 0.90

    # SLiM / ARMOR
    slim_enabled: bool = True
    slim_quant_bits: int = 4
    slim_use_2of4: bool = True
    armor_enabled: bool = True
    armor_block_size: int = 8

    # Token management
    oats_enabled: bool = True
    oats_critical_ratio: float = 0.25
    oats_marginal_ratio: float = 0.50
    ast_enabled: bool = True
    ast_keep_ratio: float = 0.7

    # Dual‑pathway

    rff_features: int = 128

    # Attention sparsity

    deltakv_topk: int = 4
    kvt_enabled: bool = True

    # Extra tricks

    lora_rank: int = 8
    periodic_rectify_interval: int = 4
