# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helper utilities
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _select_rank_svd(W, energy, min_r=4, max_r=64):
    with torch.no_grad():
        _, S, _ = torch.linalg.svd(W.float(), full_matrices=False)
        total = (S**2).sum()
        cum = torch.cumsum(S**2, dim=0) / total
        r = int(torch.searchsorted(cum, energy).item()) + 1
        return max(min_r, min(r, min(W.shape[0], W.shape[1]), max_r))


def softpick(x, dim=-1, temperature=1.0):
    m = x.max(dim=dim, keepdim=True).values
    e = torch.exp((x - m) / temperature)
    denom = e - torch.exp(-m / temperature)
    p = F.relu(denom)
    return p / (p.sum(dim=dim, keepdim=True) + 1e-9)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Projection layers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class FactorisedLinear(nn.Module):
    """SVD‑factorised linear layer."""
    def __init__(self, pretrained_weight, pretrained_bias, energy=0.95, min_r=4, max_r=64):
        super().__init__()
        out_dim, in_dim = pretrained_weight.shape
        rank = _select_rank_svd(pretrained_weight, energy, min_r, max_r)
        self.rank = rank
        with torch.no_grad():
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(pretrained_weight.float(), full_matrices=False)
            A = U[:, :rank] * S[:rank].unsqueeze(0)
            B = Vt[:rank, :]
        self.down = nn.Linear(in_dim, rank, bias=False)
        self.up = nn.Linear(rank, out_dim, bias=(pretrained_bias is not None))
        self.down.weight.data = B.float()
        self.up.weight.data = A.float()
        if pretrained_bias is not None:
            self.up.bias.data = pretrained_bias.float()
        else:
            self.up.bias.data.zero_()

    def forward(self, x):
        return self.up(self.down(x))


class ARMORBlockDiagonal(nn.Module):
    """Block‑diagonal matrix applied after up‑projection for ARMOR sparsity."""
    def __init__(self, dim, block_size=8):
        super().__init__()
        self.dim = dim
        self.block_size = block_size
        padded = ((dim + block_size - 1) // block_size) * block_size
        n_blocks = padded // block_size
        self.weight = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(n_blocks, block_size, block_size))
        nn.init.normal_(self.weight, std=0.02)
        self.padded = padded

    def forward(self, x):
        *batch, D = x.shape
        if D < self.padded:
            pad = torch.zeros(*batch, self.padded - D, device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype)
            x = torch.cat([x, pad], dim=-1)
        n_blocks = self.padded // self.block_size
        x = x.view(*batch, n_blocks, self.block_size)
        x = torch.einsum('...nb,nbc->...nc', x, self.weight)
        x = x.reshape(*batch, self.padded)
        return x[..., :D]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# DeltaKV & KVT (reused from previous modules)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class DeltaKVStore:
    def __init__(self, max_refs=128, topk=4):
        self.max_refs = max_refs
        self.topk = topk
        self.ref_k = None
        self.ref_v = None

    @torch.no_grad()
    def update(self, k, v):
        B, H, N, D = k.shape
        if B == 0: return
        ks = k[0]; vs = v[0]
        if self.ref_k is None:
            self.ref_k, self.ref_v = ks, vs
        else:
            self.ref_k = torch.cat([self.ref_k, ks], dim=1)
            self.ref_v = torch.cat([self.ref_v, vs], dim=1)
        if self.ref_k.size(1) > self.max_refs:
            self.ref_k = self.ref_k[:, -self.max_refs:, :]
            self.ref_v = self.ref_v[:, -self.max_refs:, :]

    @torch.no_grad()
    def compress(self, k, v):
        if self.ref_k is None: return k, v
        B, H, N, D = k.shape
        tk = min(self.topk, self.ref_k.size(1))
        sim = torch.matmul(k, self.ref_k.transpose(-2, -1))
        _, idx = sim.topk(tk, dim=-1)
        idx_e = idx.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, -1, -1, D)
        rk = self.ref_k.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(2).expand(B, H, N, -1, -1).gather(3, idx_e).mean(dim=3)
        rv = self.ref_v.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(2).expand(B, H, N, -1, -1).gather(3, idx_e).mean(dim=3)
        return k - rk, v - rv


class KVTQuantiser:
    def __init__(self, n_bits=4, pca_ratio=0.95):
        self.n_bits = n_bits
        self.pca_ratio = pca_ratio
        self.basis = None
        self.scale = None
        self.zp = None
        self.fitted = False

    @torch.no_grad()
    def fit(self, k, v):
        B, H, N, D = k.shape
        X = torch.cat([k.reshape(-1, D), v.reshape(-1, D)], dim=0)

══════════════════ aegis_v4.py ══════════════════
"""
AEGIS‑Attention V4 – Adaptive Enhanced Global‑Local Integrated Sparse Attention
Drop‑in replacement for DistilBERT’s attention layer.

Combines:
  1. SVD‑factorised Q/K/V/Out projections (zero‑shot, >20× param reduction)
  2. Head‑wise hybrid: local window attention + global kernel linear attention
  3. Softpick rectified softmax for natural sparsity in local heads
  4. Per‑head adaptive gate to blend local and global information
"""

import math
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Optional, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass
class AegisConfig:
    svd_energy: float = 0.95
    min_rank: int = 4
    max_rank: int = 64
    num_local_heads: int = 8
    num_global_heads: int = 4
    local_window: int = 64
    softpick_temperature: float = 1.0
    rff_features: int = 256
    use_zero_sum_keys: bool = True
    use_adaptive_gate: bool = True
    gate_init_bias: float = 1.5
    dropout: float = 0.1


class FactorisedLinear(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, pretrained_weight, pretrained_bias, energy, min_rank=4, max_rank=64):
        super().__init__()
        out_dim, in_dim = pretrained_weight.shape
        with torch.no_grad():
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(pretrained_weight.float(), full_matrices=False)
            total = (S ** 2).sum()
            cum = torch.cumsum(S ** 2, dim=0) / total
            rank = int(torch.searchsorted(cum, energy).item()) + 1
            rank = max(min_rank, min(rank, min(out_dim, in_dim), max_rank))
            self.rank = rank
            A = U[:, :rank] * S[:rank].unsqueeze(0)
            B = Vt[:rank, :]
        self.down = nn.Linear(in_dim, rank, bias=False)
        self.up   = nn.Linear(rank, out_dim, bias=(pretrained_bias is not None))
        self.down.weight.data = B.float()
        self.up.weight.data   = A.float()
        if pretrained_bias is not None:
            self.up.bias.data = pretrained_bias.float()
        else:
            self.up.bias.data.zero_()
    def forward(self, x): return self.up(self.down(x))


def softpick(x, dim=-1, temperature=1.0):
    m = x.max(dim=dim, keepdim=True).values
    e = torch.exp((x - m) / temperature)
    denom = e - torch.exp(-m / temperature)
    p = F.relu(denom)
    return p / (p.sum(dim=dim, keepdim=True) + 1e-9)


def local_window_attention(Q, K, V, window, temperature, token_valid=None):
    B, H, N, D = Q.shape
    scale = D ** -0.5
    idx = torch.arange(N, device=Q.device)
    dist = (idx.unsqueeze(0) - idx.unsqueeze(1)).abs()
    mask = (dist <= window // 2).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)
    scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
    scores = scores.masked_fill(~mask, -1e9)
    if token_valid is not None:
        scores = scores.masked_fill(~token_valid.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e9)
    attn = softpick(scores, dim=-1, temperature=temperature)
    return torch.matmul(attn, V)


def kernel_linear_attention(Q, K, V, rff_proj, use_zero_sum=True):
    B, H, N, D = Q.shape
    F_dim = rff_proj.size(-1)
    if use_zero_sum:
        K = K - K.mean(dim=2, keepdim=True)
    Q_norm = F.normalize(Q, p=2, dim=-1)
    K_norm = F.normalize(K, p=2, dim=-1)
    Q_proj = torch.einsum('bhnd,hdf->bhnf', Q_norm, rff_proj)
    K_proj = torch.einsum('bhnd,hdf->bhnf', K_norm, rff_proj)
    Q_feat = torch.exp(Q_proj) / math.sqrt(F_dim)
    K_feat = torch.exp(K_proj) / math.sqrt(F_dim)
    KV = torch.einsum('bhnf,bhnd->bhfd', K_feat, V)
    Z  = K_feat.sum(dim=2, keepdim=True)
    ctx = torch.einsum('bhnf,bhfd->bhnd', Q_feat, KV)
    ctx = ctx / (torch.einsum('bhnf,bhfd->bhnd', Q_feat, Z) + 1e-8)
    return ctx


class AegisAttention(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, model_config, aegis_cfg: AegisConfig, pretrained_attn=None, bert_mode=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_heads = model_config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = model_config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.hidden_size = model_config.hidden_size
        self.cfg = aegis_cfg
        self.bert_mode = bert_mode
        assert aegis_cfg.num_local_heads + aegis_cfg.num_global_heads == self.num_heads
        self.num_local_heads = aegis_cfg.num_local_heads
        self.num_global_heads = aegis_cfg.num_global_heads
        if pretrained_attn is None:
            raise ValueError("AegisAttention requires pretrained_attn for zero‑shot init")
        self.q_proj = FactorisedLinear(pretrained_attn.q_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.q_lin.bias.data,
                                       aegis_cfg.svd_energy, aegis_cfg.min_rank, aegis_cfg.max_rank)
        self.k_proj = FactorisedLinear(pretrained_attn.k_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.k_lin.bias.data,
                                       aegis_cfg.svd_energy, aegis_cfg.min_rank, aegis_cfg.max_rank)
        self.v_proj = FactorisedLinear(pretrained_attn.v_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.v_lin.bias.data,
                                       aegis_cfg.svd_energy, aegis_cfg.min_rank, aegis_cfg.max_rank)
        self.out_proj = FactorisedLinear(pretrained_attn.out_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.out_lin.bias.data,
                                         aegis_cfg.svd_energy, aegis_cfg.min_rank, aegis_cfg.max_rank)
        self.rff_proj = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(self.num_global_heads, self.head_dim, aegis_cfg.rff_features) * 0.02)
        if aegis_cfg.use_adaptive_gate:
            self.blend_logits = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.num_heads))
            self.gate_bias = aegis_cfg.gate_init_bias
        else:
            self.blend_logits = None
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(aegis_cfg.dropout)

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, head_mask=None, output_attentions=False, **kwargs):
        B, N, _ = hidden_states.shape
        H, D = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        Hl, Hg = self.num_local_heads, self.num_global_heads

        Q_all = self.q_proj(hidden_states)
        K_all = self.k_proj(hidden_states)
        V_all = self.v_proj(hidden_states)
        Q_all = Q_all.view(B, N, H, D).transpose(1, 2)
        K_all = K_all.view(B, N, H, D).transpose(1, 2)
        V_all = V_all.view(B, N, H, D).transpose(1, 2)

        Q_local, Q_global = Q_all[:, :Hl], Q_all[:, Hl:]
        K_local, K_global = K_all[:, :Hl], K_all[:, Hl:]
        V_local, V_global = V_all[:, :Hl], V_all[:, Hl:]

        mask = attention_mask if attention_mask is not None else kwargs.get('attn_mask', None)
        token_valid = None
        if mask is not None:
            if mask.dim() == 4 and mask.shape[-2] == mask.shape[-1]:
                token_valid = mask[:, 0, 0, :] > -1e9
            elif mask.dim() == 4:
                token_valid = mask.squeeze(1).squeeze(1) > -1e9
            elif mask.dim() == 3:
                token_valid = mask.squeeze(1).bool()
            elif mask.dim() == 2:
                token_valid = mask.bool()

        ctx_local = local_window_attention(Q_local, K_local, V_local, self.cfg.local_window,
                                           self.cfg.softpick_temperature, token_valid)
        ctx_global = kernel_linear_attention(Q_global, K_global, V_global, self.rff_proj,
                                            self.cfg.use_zero_sum_keys)

        if self.blend_logits is not None:
            global_signal = ctx_global.mean(dim=1, keepdim=True).expand(-1, H, -1, -1)
            local_full = torch.cat([ctx_local, torch.zeros(B, Hg, N, D, device=ctx_local.device)], dim=1)
            gate = torch.sigmoid(self.blend_logits.unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1) + self.gate_bias)
            context = gate * local_full + (1 - gate) * global_signal
        else:
            context = torch.cat([ctx_local, ctx_global], dim=1)

        if token_valid is not None:
            context = context * token_valid.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1).to(context.dtype)

        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N, H * D)
        output = self.out_proj(context)
        return (output, None)

    def get_ortho_loss(self):
        return torch.tensor(0.0, device=self.out_proj.up.weight.device)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Framework registration
# ------------------------------------------------------------
from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("aegis_v4")
def inject_aegis_v4(layer, model_config, comp_config, layer_idx, total_layers, old_attn):
    cfg = AegisConfig(
        num_local_heads=8,
        num_global_heads=4,
        local_window=getattr(comp_config, 'block_size', 64),
        rff_features=getattr(comp_config, 'rff_features', 256),
        dropout=getattr(comp_config, 'dropout', 0.1),
    )
    new_attn = AegisAttention(model_config=model_config, aegis_cfg=cfg, pretrained_attn=old_attn)
    layer.attention = new_attn
    return new_attn

══════════════════ aegis_v5.py ══════════════════
"""
AEGIS‑Attention V5 – Hybrid Efficient Transformer Attention
------------------------------------------------------------
Drop‑in replacement for DistilBERT’s attention layer.

Combines:
  1. SVD‑factorised Q/K/V/Out projections (zero‑shot, >20× param reduction)
  2. Head‑wise hybrid: local window attention + global kernel linear attention
  3. Softpick rectified softmax for natural sparsity in local heads
  4. Per‑head adaptive gate to blend local and global information
"""

import math
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Optional, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
@dataclass
class AegisConfigV5:
    svd_energy: float = 0.95          # fraction of singular‑value energy to keep
    min_rank: int = 4
    max_rank: int = 64
    num_local_heads: int = 8          # local window attention
    num_global_heads: int = 4         # global kernel linear attention
    local_window: int = 64            # sliding window radius
    softpick_temperature: float = 1.0
    rff_features: int = 256           # random Fourier feature dimension
    use_zero_sum_keys: bool = True    # stabilise kernel approximation
    use_adaptive_gate: bool = True    # per‑head blending
    gate_init_bias: float = 1.5       # initial bias towards global
    dropout: float = 0.1


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Factorised Linear Layer (truncated SVD)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("aegis_v5")
class FactorisedLinear(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, pretrained_weight: torch.Tensor,
                 pretrained_bias: Optional[torch.Tensor],
                 energy: float, min_rank: int = 4, max_rank: int = 64):
        super().__init__()
        out_dim, in_dim = pretrained_weight.shape
        with torch.no_grad():
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(pretrained_weight.float(), full_matrices=False)
            total = (S ** 2).sum()
            cum = torch.cumsum(S ** 2, dim=0) / total
            rank = int(torch.searchsorted(cum, energy).item()) + 1
            rank = max(min_rank, min(rank, min(out_dim, in_dim), max_rank))
            self.rank = rank
            A = U[:, :rank] * S[:rank].unsqueeze(0)   # (out, r)
            B = Vt[:rank, :]                           # (r, in)

        self.down = nn.Linear(in_dim, rank, bias=False)
        self.up   = nn.Linear(rank, out_dim, bias=(pretrained_bias is not None))
        self.down.weight.data = B.float()
        self.up.weight.data   = A.float()
        if pretrained_bias is not None:
            self.up.bias.data = pretrained_bias.float()
        else:
            self.up.bias.data.zero_()

    def forward(self, x):
        return self.up(self.down(x))


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Softpick rectified softmax
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
def softpick(x: torch.Tensor, dim: int = -1, temperature: float = 1.0) -> torch.Tensor:
    m = x.max(dim=dim, keepdim=True).values
    e = torch.exp((x - m) / temperature)
    denom = e - torch.exp(-m / temperature)
    p = F.relu(denom)
    return p / (p.sum(dim=dim, keepdim=True) + 1e-9)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Local Window Attention (with Softpick)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
def local_window_attention(Q, K, V, window, temperature, token_valid=None):
    B, H, N, D = Q.shape
    scale = D ** -0.5
    idx = torch.arange(N, device=Q.device)
    dist = (idx.unsqueeze(0) - idx.unsqueeze(1)).abs()
    mask = (dist <= window // 2).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)

    scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
    scores = scores.masked_fill(~mask, -1e9)
    if token_valid is not None:
        scores = scores.masked_fill(~token_valid.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e9)
    attn = softpick(scores, dim=-1, temperature=temperature)
    return torch.matmul(attn, V)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Global Kernel Linear Attention (Performer style)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# (Used directly inside the class, not as a separate callable)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# AEGIS‑Attention Module
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
class AegisAttention(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, model_config, aegis_cfg: AegisConfigV5,
                 pretrained_attn=None, bert_mode: bool = False):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_heads = model_config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = model_config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.hidden_size = model_config.hidden_size
        self.cfg = aegis_cfg
        self.bert_mode = bert_mode

        assert (aegis_cfg.num_local_heads + aegis_cfg.num_global_heads == self.num_heads)
        self.num_local_heads = aegis_cfg.num_local_heads
        self.num_global_heads = aegis_cfg.num_global_heads

        if pretrained_attn is None:
            raise ValueError("AegisAttention requires pretrained_attn for zero‑shot init")

        # SVD‑factorised projections
        self.q_proj = FactorisedLinear(
            pretrained_attn.q_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.q_lin.bias.data,
            aegis_cfg.svd_energy, aegis_cfg.min_rank, aegis_cfg.max_rank)
        self.k_proj = FactorisedLinear(
            pretrained_attn.k_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.k_lin.bias.data,
            aegis_cfg.svd_energy, aegis_cfg.min_rank, aegis_cfg.max_rank)
        self.v_proj = FactorisedLinear(
            pretrained_attn.v_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.v_lin.bias.data,
            aegis_cfg.svd_energy, aegis_cfg.min_rank, aegis_cfg.max_rank)
        self.out_proj = FactorisedLinear(
            pretrained_attn.out_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.out_lin.bias.data,
            aegis_cfg.svd_energy, aegis_cfg.min_rank, aegis_cfg.max_rank)

        # Random Fourier features for global heads
        self.rff_proj = nn.Parameter(
            torch.randn(self.num_global_heads, self.head_dim, aegis_cfg.rff_features) * 0.02)

        # Adaptive gating
        if aegis_cfg.use_adaptive_gate:
            self.blend_logits = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.num_heads))
            self.gate_bias = aegis_cfg.gate_init_bias
        else:
            self.blend_logits = None

        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(aegis_cfg.dropout)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Mask helper (handles DistilBERT's various mask shapes)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _prepare_mask(attention_mask, B, N, device):
        if attention_mask is None:
            return None
        if attention_mask.dim() == 4:
            if attention_mask.shape[-2] == attention_mask.shape[-1]:
                token_valid = attention_mask[:, 0, 0, :] > -1e9
            else:
                token_valid = attention_mask.squeeze(1).squeeze(1) > -1e9
        elif attention_mask.dim() == 3:
            token_valid = attention_mask.squeeze(1).bool()
        elif attention_mask.dim() == 2:
            token_valid = attention_mask.bool()
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"Unexpected mask dim: {attention_mask.dim()}")
        return token_valid.to(device)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward pass
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, head_mask=None,
                output_attentions=False, **kwargs):
        B, N, _ = hidden_states.shape
        H, D = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        Hl, Hg = self.num_local_heads, self.num_global_heads

        # 1. Low‑rank projections
        Q_all = self.q_proj(hidden_states)
        K_all = self.k_proj(hidden_states)
        V_all = self.v_proj(hidden_states)

        Q_all = Q_all.view(B, N, H, D).transpose(1, 2)  # (B, H, N, D)
        K_all = K_all.view(B, N, H, D).transpose(1, 2)
        V_all = V_all.view(B, N, H, D).transpose(1, 2)

        # split heads into local and global
        Q_local, Q_global = Q_all[:, :Hl], Q_all[:, Hl:]
        K_local, K_global = K_all[:, :Hl], K_all[:, Hl:]
        V_local, V_global = V_all[:, :Hl], V_all[:, Hl:]


══════════════════ aegis_v1.py ══════════════════
"""
AEGIS‑Attention V1 – Adaptive Efficient Global‑Local Integrated Sparse Attention
CUR‑factorised projections, local block attention (Softpick), global kernel linear attention.
"""

import math
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Optional, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass
from framework.attentions.core.utils import softpick


@dataclass
class AEGISConfigV1:
    cur_rank: int = 16
    cur_method: str = "interpolative"
    use_tile_sharing: bool = True
    tile_size: int = 32
    num_shared_tiles: int = 64
    num_local_heads: int = 8
    num_global_heads: int = 4
    local_window: int = 64
    softpick_enabled: bool = True
    softpick_temperature: float = 1.0
    rff_features: int = 256
    use_adaptive_gate: bool = True
    gate_init_bias: float = 1.5
    dropout: float = 0.1


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# CUR‑Factorised Linear Layer
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
class CURLinear(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, pretrained_weight, pretrained_bias, rank, method="interpolative",
                 tile_size=32, num_shared_tiles=64, use_tile_sharing=True):
        super().__init__()
        out_dim, in_dim = pretrained_weight.shape
        self.out_dim = out_dim
        self.in_dim = in_dim
        self.rank = rank
        self.tile_size = tile_size
        self.num_tiles = num_shared_tiles
        self.use_tile_sharing = use_tile_sharing

        W = pretrained_weight.float()
        if method == "interpolative":
            from scipy.linalg import interpolative
            W_np = W.cpu().numpy()
            idx, proj = interpolative.interp_decomp(W_np, rank)
            C = W[:, idx[:rank]].clone()
            R = torch.tensor(proj[:rank, :], dtype=torch.float32)
        else:  # leverage
            col_norms = W.norm(dim=0)
            probs = col_norms / col_norms.sum()
            selected = torch.multinomial(probs, rank, replacement=False)
            C = W[:, selected].clone()
            R = torch.linalg.lstsq(C, W).solution

        if use_tile_sharing:
            self.register_buffer("C_tiles", self._tile_encode(C))
            self.register_buffer("R_tiles", self._tile_encode(R))
        else:
            self.register_buffer("C", C)
            self.register_buffer("R", R)

        if pretrained_bias is not None:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(pretrained_bias.clone().float())
        else:
            self.register_buffer('bias', None)

    def _tile_encode(self, M):
        rows, cols = M.shape
        ts = self.tile_size
        tiles = []
        for i in range(0, rows, ts):
            for j in range(0, cols, ts):
                tile = M[i:i+ts, j:j+ts].flatten()
                tiles.append(tile)
        tiles = torch.stack(tiles)
        idx = torch.randperm(len(tiles))[:self.num_tiles]
        return tiles[idx]

    def _tile_decode(self, proto, out_rows, out_cols):
        ts = self.tile_size
        K = proto.size(0)
        mat = torch.zeros(out_rows, out_cols, device=proto.device, dtype=proto.dtype)
        for i in range(0, out_rows, ts):
            hi = min(i+ts, out_rows)
            for j in range(0, out_cols, ts):
                proto_idx = (i // ts + j // ts) % K
                tile_val = proto[proto_idx].view(ts, ts)
                wj = min(j+ts, out_cols)
                mat[i:hi, j:wj] = tile_val[:hi-i, :wj-j]
        return mat

    def _get_matrices(self):
        if self.use_tile_sharing:
            C = self._tile_decode(self.C_tiles, self.out_dim, self.rank)
            R = self._tile_decode(self.R_tiles, self.rank, self.in_dim)
        else:
            C = self.C
            R = self.R
        return C, R

    def forward(self, x):
        C, R = self._get_matrices()
        y = F.linear(x, R)
        y = F.linear(y, C.t())
        if self.bias is not None:
            y = y + self.bias
        return y


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Local block attention (with Softpick)
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
def local_block_attention(Q, K, V, window, temperature=1.0,
                          softpick_enabled=True, token_valid=None):
    B, H, N, D = Q.shape
    scale = D ** -0.5
    idx = torch.arange(N, device=Q.device)
    dist = (idx.unsqueeze(0) - idx.unsqueeze(1)).abs()
    mask = (dist <= window // 2).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)

    scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
    scores = scores.masked_fill(~mask, -1e9)

    if token_valid is not None:
        scores = scores.masked_fill(~token_valid.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e9)

    if softpick_enabled:
        attn = softpick(scores, dim=-1, temperature=temperature)
    else:
        attn = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)

    return torch.matmul(attn, V)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Global kernel linear attention (Performer)
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
def kernel_linear_attention(Q, K, V, rff_proj):
    B, H, N, D = Q.shape
    F_dim = rff_proj.size(-1)

    Q_norm = F.normalize(Q, p=2, dim=-1)
    K_norm = F.normalize(K, p=2, dim=-1)

    Q_proj = torch.einsum('bhnd,hdf->bhnf', Q_norm, rff_proj)
    K_proj = torch.einsum('bhnd,hdf->bhnf', K_norm, rff_proj)

    Q_feat = torch.exp(Q_proj) / math.sqrt(F_dim)
    K_feat = torch.exp(K_proj) / math.sqrt(F_dim)

    KV = torch.einsum('bhnf,bhnd->bhfd', K_feat, V)
    Z = K_feat.sum(dim=2, keepdim=True)
    ctx = torch.einsum('bhnf,bhfd->bhnd', Q_feat, KV)
    ctx = ctx / (torch.einsum('bhnf,bhfd->bhnd', Q_feat, Z) + 1e-8)
    return ctx


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# AEGIS Attention Module
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
class AEGISAttention(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, model_config, aegis_cfg: AEGISConfigV1,
                 pretrained_attn=None, bert_mode=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_heads = model_config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = model_config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.hidden_size = model_config.hidden_size
        self.cfg = aegis_cfg
        self.bert_mode = bert_mode

        assert aegis_cfg.num_local_heads + aegis_cfg.num_global_heads == self.num_heads
        self.num_local_heads = aegis_cfg.num_local_heads
        self.num_global_heads = aegis_cfg.num_global_heads

        if pretrained_attn is None:
            raise ValueError("AEGIS requires pretrained_attn for zero‑shot init")

        q_w = pretrained_attn.q_lin.weight.data
        k_w = pretrained_attn.k_lin.weight.data
        v_w = pretrained_attn.v_lin.weight.data
        o_w = pretrained_attn.out_lin.weight.data

        self.q_proj = CURLinear(q_w, pretrained_attn.q_lin.bias,
                                aegis_cfg.cur_rank, aegis_cfg.cur_method,
                                aegis_cfg.tile_size, aegis_cfg.num_shared_tiles,
                                aegis_cfg.use_tile_sharing)
        self.k_proj = CURLinear(k_w, pretrained_attn.k_lin.bias,
                                aegis_cfg.cur_rank, aegis_cfg.cur_method,
                                aegis_cfg.tile_size, aegis_cfg.num_shared_tiles,
                                aegis_cfg.use_tile_sharing)
        self.v_proj = CURLinear(v_w, pretrained_attn.v_lin.bias,
                                aegis_cfg.cur_rank, aegis_cfg.cur_method,

══════════════════ aegis_hybrid.py ══════════════════
"""Aegis-Hybrid: low‑rank projections + local window + global linear + adaptive pruning."""
import math, torch, torch.nn as nn, torch.nn.functional as F
from dataclasses import dataclass
from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@dataclass
class AegisHybridConfig:
    hidden_size: int = 768
    num_attention_heads: int = 12
    head_dim: int = 64
    local_window: int = 64
    rank: int = 64
    kernel_feature_dim: int = 128
    dropout: float = 0.1
    use_adaptive_pruning: bool = True

class AegisHybridAttention(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, config: AegisHybridConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.hidden_size = config.hidden_size
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = config.head_dim
        self.local_window = config.local_window
        self.rank = config.rank
        self.kernel_dim = config.kernel_feature_dim
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(config.dropout)

        self.q_proj1 = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.rank, bias=False)
        self.q_proj2 = nn.Linear(self.rank, self.hidden_size, bias=True)
        self.k_proj1 = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.rank, bias=False)
        self.k_proj2 = nn.Linear(self.rank, self.hidden_size, bias=True)
        self.v_proj1 = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.rank, bias=False)
        self.v_proj2 = nn.Linear(self.rank, self.hidden_size, bias=True)
        self.out_proj1 = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.rank, bias=False)
        self.out_proj2 = nn.Linear(self.rank, self.hidden_size, bias=True)

        if config.use_adaptive_pruning:
            self.prune_gate = nn.Sequential(
                nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.hidden_size // 4),
                nn.ReLU(),
                nn.Linear(self.hidden_size // 4, self.num_heads))
        else:
            self.prune_gate = None
        self.fusion_gate = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.num_heads)

    @staticmethod
    def _svd_init(orig_lin, lin1, lin2, rank):
        W = orig_lin.weight.data
        bias = orig_lin.bias.data if orig_lin.bias is not None else None
        U, S, Vh = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
        U_r = U[:, :rank]; S_r = S[:rank]; Vh_r = Vh[:rank, :]
        lin1.weight.data = Vh_r
        lin2.weight.data = U_r @ torch.diag(S_r)
        if bias is not None:
            lin2.bias.data = bias

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, head_mask=None, output_attentions=False, **kwargs):
        B, N, H = hidden_states.shape

        def lr(p1, p2, x): return p2(p1(x))
        Q = lr(self.q_proj1, self.q_proj2, hidden_states)                   # (B, N, H)
        K = lr(self.k_proj1, self.k_proj2, hidden_states)
        V = lr(self.v_proj1, self.v_proj2, hidden_states)

        Q = Q.view(B, N, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)                     # (B, N, h, d)
        K = K.view(B, N, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
        V = V.view(B, N, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)

        # -------- padding mask (2D bool, True = keep) --------
        mask_2d = None
        if attention_mask is not None:
            if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
                mask_2d = attention_mask.bool()                              # (B, N)
            elif attention_mask.dim() == 4:
                mask_2d = (attention_mask[:, 0, 0, :] > -1e9)               # (B, N)

        # zero out padded tokens in Q, K, V  (B, N, 1, 1)
        if mask_2d is not None:
            mask_4d = mask_2d.unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)                   # (B, N, 1, 1)
            Q = Q * mask_4d
            K = K * mask_4d
            V = V * mask_4d

        # ============== 1. Local window attention ==============
        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        scores = torch.einsum('bnhd,bmhd->bhnm', Q, K) * scale               # (B, h, N, N)

        # local band mask
        idx = torch.arange(N, device=hidden_states.device)
        band_mask = (idx.unsqueeze(1) - idx.unsqueeze(0)).abs() <= self.local_window   # (N, N)
        band_mask = band_mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)                     # (1, 1, N, N)
        scores = scores.masked_fill(~band_mask, float('-inf'))

        # also apply padding mask to keys (B, 1, 1, N)
        if mask_2d is not None:
            key_mask = mask_2d.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)                    # (B, 1, 1, N)
            scores = scores.masked_fill(~key_mask, float('-inf'))

        attn_local = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
        attn_local = self.dropout(attn_local)
        local_out = torch.einsum('bhnm,bmhd->bnhd', attn_local, V)          # (B, N, h, d)

        # ============== 2. Global linear attention ==============
        phi = lambda x: F.elu(x) + 1

        if self.prune_gate is not None:
            keep = torch.sigmoid(self.prune_gate(hidden_states))            # (B, N, h)
            Kg = K * keep.unsqueeze(-1)                                     # (B, N, h, d)
            Vg = V * keep.unsqueeze(-1)
        else:
            Kg, Vg = K, V

        # zero out padded tokens again (mask_4d already applied to K,V but we re-check Kg,Vg)
        if mask_2d is not None:
            Kg = Kg * mask_4d
            Vg = Vg * mask_4d

        phi_K = phi(Kg)                                                     # (B, N, h, d)
        phi_Q = phi(Q)

        phi_K_t = phi_K.permute(0, 2, 3, 1)                                 # (B, h, d, N)
        Vg_t = Vg.permute(0, 2, 1, 3)                                       # (B, h, N, d)

        KV = torch.matmul(phi_K_t, Vg_t)                                    # (B, h, d, d)

        ones = torch.ones(B, self.num_heads, N, 1, device=Kg.device)        # (B, h, N, 1)
        Z = torch.matmul(phi_K_t, ones)                                     # (B, h, d, 1)

        phi_Q_t = phi_Q.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).unsqueeze(-2)                  # (B, h, N, 1, d)

        global_out = torch.matmul(phi_Q_t, KV.unsqueeze(2)).squeeze(-2)     # (B, h, N, d)
        norm = torch.matmul(phi_Q_t, Z.unsqueeze(2)).squeeze(-2)            # (B, h, N, 1)
        global_out = global_out / (norm + 1e-8)
        global_out = global_out.permute(0, 2, 1, 3)                         # (B, N, h, d)

        # ============== 3. Adaptive fusion ==============
        alpha = torch.sigmoid(self.fusion_gate(hidden_states)).unsqueeze(-1)  # (B, N, h, 1)
        fused = alpha * local_out + (1 - alpha) * global_out                   # (B, N, h, d)

        # ============== 4. Output projection ==============
        fused = fused.reshape(B, N, H)
        out = self.out_proj2(self.out_proj1(fused))
        out = self.dropout(out)

        if mask_2d is not None:
            out = out * mask_2d.unsqueeze(-1)                               # zero out padding in output

        return (out, None)
    

    
    def get_ortho_loss(self): return torch.tensor(0.0, device=self.out_proj2.weight.device)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Injection function – registers 'aegis_hybrid' in the registry
# ------------------------------------------------------------
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("aegis_hybrid")
def inject_aegis_hybrid(layer, model_config, comp_config, layer_idx, total_layers, old_attn):
    cfg = AegisHybridConfig(
        hidden_size=model_config.hidden_size,
        num_attention_heads=model_config.num_attention_heads,
        head_dim=model_config.hidden_size//model_config.num_attention_heads,
        local_window=getattr(comp_config, 'block_size', 64),
        rank=getattr(comp_config, 'lora_r', 64),
        kernel_feature_dim=getattr(comp_config, 'rff_features', 128),
        dropout=getattr(comp_config, 'dropout', 0.1),
        use_adaptive_pruning=getattr(comp_config, 'use_gate', True),
    )
    new_attn = AegisHybridAttention(cfg)
    new_attn._svd_init(old_attn.q_lin, new_attn.q_proj1, new_attn.q_proj2, cfg.rank)
    new_attn._svd_init(old_attn.k_lin, new_attn.k_proj1, new_attn.k_proj2, cfg.rank)
    new_attn._svd_init(old_attn.v_lin, new_attn.v_proj1, new_attn.v_proj2, cfg.rank)
    new_attn._svd_init(old_attn.out_lin, new_attn.out_proj1, new_attn.out_proj2, cfg.rank)
    layer.attention = new_attn
    return new_attn

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
from dataclasses import dataclass
import math
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Optional, Tuple, Literal
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from framework.attentions.core.base import BaseAttention
from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@dataclass
class AegisConfigV3:
    hidden_size: int = 768
    num_heads: int = 12
    head_dim: int = 64
    window_size: int = 64          # half‑window (total context = 2*window)
    svd_rank: int = 128
    low_rank_update: int = 16
    kernel_feature_dim: int = 128  # m
    dropout: float = 0.1
    use_query_gate: bool = True
    use_input_gate: bool = True
    use_pre_norm: bool = True
    long_context_threshold: int = 2048   # apply top‑k reduction beyond this length
    max_global_tokens: int = 1024
    kernel_method: str = "orthogonal"      # "orthogonal", "random", "cosine", "custom"
    kernel_clamp_min: float = -20.0
    kernel_clamp_max: float = 20.0
    kernel_use_float64: bool = False
    kernel_seed: Optional[int] = None



# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Factorised Low‑Rank Projection
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
class FactorisedLowRankProjection(nn.Module):
    """
    Implements W = U diag(S) V^T + A B as:
        y = x V diag(S) U^T + (x B^T) A^T
    without ever materialising the full weight.
    """
    def __init__(self, in_features: int, out_features: int,
                 svd_rank: int, lora_rank: int,
                 pretrained_weight: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None):
        super().__init__()
        self.in_features = in_features
        self.out_features = out_features

        # SVD factors (frozen U, V; trainable S)
        if pretrained_weight is not None:
            U, S, V = torch.linalg.svd(pretrained_weight.detach(), full_matrices=False)
            # Note: torch.svd returns V (not V^T), shape (H, H)
            U = U[:, :svd_rank].clone()
            S = S[:svd_rank].clone()
            V = V[:, :svd_rank].clone()   # V (in, rank)
        else:
            U = torch.randn(out_features, svd_rank) * 0.02
            S = torch.ones(svd_rank)
            V = torch.randn(in_features, svd_rank) * 0.02

        self.register_buffer('U', U)
        self.S = nn.Parameter(S)
        self.register_buffer('V', V)

        # LoRA factors
        self.A = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(out_features, lora_rank))
        self.B = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(lora_rank, in_features))
        nn.init.normal_(self.B, std=0.02)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        # x: (B, N, in_features)
        # SVD path: x V diag(S) U^T
        xV = torch.matmul(x, self.V)                   # (B,N, r_svd)
        xV_S = xV * self.S.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)   # broadcast S
        out_svd = torch.matmul(xV_S, self.U.t())       # (B,N, out)

        # LoRA path: x B^T A^T
        xB = torch.matmul(x, self.B.t())               # (B,N, r_lora)
        out_lora = torch.matmul(xB, self.A.t())        # (B,N, out)

        return out_svd + out_lora


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Kernel Feature Map (stable float32)
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
"""
Kernel Feature Map for Linear Attention
========================================
Implements the positive orthogonal random feature map (FAVOR+) used in
Performers and similar linear‑attention models, with multiple initialisation
strategies, aggressive numerical stability measures, and full shape‑safety.

Mathematical core:
    φ(x) = (1 / √m) · exp( x·Π  -  ‖x‖² / 2 )
where Π ∈ R^{d×m} is a (semi‑)orthogonal matrix scaled by √d.
The dot product φ(q)ᵀφ(k) approximates exp(qᵀk / √d).

Supported variants:
    - Orthogonal random features (Performer, FAVOR+)
    - Unstructured random Fourier features (original Rahimi & Recht)
    - Cosine / Sine deterministic features (for reproducibility)
    - Custom projection matrix

Stability features:
    - Clamped exponential input (default [-20, 20]) to avoid float32 overflow
    - RMS pre‑norm friendly (variance ~1 after projection when using √d scaling)
    - EPS‑protected scaling factor
    - Optional float64 upcast for exp (disabled by default for speed)
    - Safe initialisation of projection matrix with fallbacks
"""
import math
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Optional, Literal

import torch
import torch.nn as nn


# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
@dataclass
class KernelFeatureConfig:
    head_dim: int            # d (e.g. 64)
    num_features: int        # m (e.g. 128)
    method: Literal["orthogonal", "random", "cosine", "custom"] = "orthogonal"
    clamp_min: float = -20.0
    clamp_max: float = 20.0
    use_float64_exp: bool = False
    eps: float = 1e-8
    scaling: Optional[float] = None   # if None, auto‑set to 1/√m
    projection_scale: Optional[float] = None  # if None, auto‑set to √d
    seed: Optional[int] = None        # reproducibility
    # For custom method only:
    custom_proj: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None


# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main Kernel Feature Map class
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
class StableKernelFeatureMap(nn.Module):
    """
    A feature map φ: R^d → R^m for linear attention that satisfies
        φ(q)ᵀ φ(k) ≈ exp(qᵀ k / √d).

    Args:
        config: KernelFeatureConfig dataclass with all hyperparameters.
    """

    def __init__(self, config: KernelFeatureConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.cfg = config
        self.head_dim = config.head_dim
        self.m = config.num_features

        # ---- 1. Set scaling factor ----
        if config.scaling is not None:
            self.scale = config.scaling
        else:
            self.scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.m + config.eps)

        # ---- 2. Build projection matrix ----
        proj = self._build_projection()
        self.register_buffer('proj', proj)

        # ---- 3. Validate final shape ----
        assert self.proj.shape == (self.head_dim, self.m), \
            f"Projection matrix shape {self.proj.shape} != ({self.head_dim}, {self.m})"

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Projection initialisation strategies
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _build_projection(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Return a tensor of shape (d, m) according to self.cfg.method."""
        if self.cfg.seed is not None:
            torch.manual_seed(self.cfg.seed)

        d, m = self.head_dim, self.m

        if self.cfg.method == "orthogonal":
            return self._orthogonal_random(d, m)
        elif self.cfg.method == "random":
            return self._random_fourier(d, m)
        elif self.cfg.method == "cosine":
            return self._cosine_features(d, m)
        elif self.cfg.method == "custom":
            if self.cfg.custom_proj is None:
                raise ValueError("custom_proj must be provided when method='custom'")
            return self.cfg.custom_proj.to(dtype=torch.float32)
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown method: {self.cfg.method}")


══════════════════ aegis_v2.py ══════════════════
"""
Aegis‑Attention V2: Low‑rank projections, local dense window,
global linear kernel attention, and adaptive soft masking.
"""

import math
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass
class AegisAttentionConfigV2:
    hidden_size: int = 768
    num_attention_heads: int = 12
    head_dim: int = 64
    local_window: int = 64
    rank: int = 64
    kernel_feature_dim: int = 128
    dropout: float = 0.1
    use_adaptive_pruning: bool = True


class AegisAttentionV2(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, config: AegisAttentionConfigV2):
        super().__init__()
        self.hidden_size = config.hidden_size
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = config.head_dim
        self.local_window = config.local_window
        self.rank = config.rank
        self.kernel_dim = config.kernel_feature_dim
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(config.dropout)

        self.q_proj1 = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.rank, bias=False)
        self.q_proj2 = nn.Linear(self.rank, self.hidden_size, bias=True)
        self.k_proj1 = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.rank, bias=False)
        self.k_proj2 = nn.Linear(self.rank, self.hidden_size, bias=True)
        self.v_proj1 = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.rank, bias=False)
        self.v_proj2 = nn.Linear(self.rank, self.hidden_size, bias=True)

        self.out_proj1 = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.rank, bias=False)
        self.out_proj2 = nn.Linear(self.rank, self.hidden_size, bias=True)

        if config.use_adaptive_pruning:
            self.prune_gate = nn.Sequential(
                nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.hidden_size // 4),
                nn.ReLU(),
                nn.Linear(self.hidden_size // 4, self.num_heads)
            )
        else:
            self.prune_gate = None

        self.fusion_gate = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.num_heads)

    @staticmethod
    def _svd_init(original_linear, lin1, lin2, rank):
        W = original_linear.weight.data
        bias = original_linear.bias.data if original_linear.bias is not None else None
        U, S, Vh = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
        U_r = U[:, :rank]
        S_r = S[:rank]
        Vh_r = Vh[:rank, :]
        lin1.weight.data = Vh_r
        lin2.weight.data = U_r @ torch.diag(S_r)
        if bias is not None:
            lin2.bias.data = bias

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, head_mask=None, output_attentions=False, **kwargs):
        B, N, H = hidden_states.shape
        # ---- convert mask to 2D bool where True = keep ----
        token_valid = None
        if attention_mask is not None:
            if attention_mask.dim() == 4:
                if attention_mask.shape[-2] == attention_mask.shape[-1]:
                    token_valid = attention_mask[:, 0, 0, :] > -1e9
                else:
                    token_valid = attention_mask.squeeze(1).squeeze(1) > -1e9
            elif attention_mask.dim() == 3:
                token_valid = attention_mask.squeeze(1).bool()
            elif attention_mask.dim() == 2:
                token_valid = attention_mask.bool()

        mask_2d = token_valid   # (B,N) bool, True = valid

        def lr(lin1, lin2, x): return lin2(lin1(x))
        Q = lr(self.q_proj1, self.q_proj2, hidden_states)
        K = lr(self.k_proj1, self.k_proj2, hidden_states)
        V = lr(self.v_proj1, self.v_proj2, hidden_states)

        Q = Q.view(B, N, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
        K = K.view(B, N, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
        V = V.view(B, N, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)

        if mask_2d is not None:
            m4 = mask_2d.unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)   # (B,N,1,1)
            Q = Q * m4
            K = K * m4
            V = V * m4

        # ---- Local attention ----
        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        scores = torch.einsum('bnhd,bmhd->bhnm', Q, K) * scale
        idx = torch.arange(N, device=hidden_states.device)
        band = (idx.unsqueeze(1) - idx.unsqueeze(0)).abs() <= self.local_window
        band = band.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)
        scores = scores.masked_fill(~band, float('-inf'))
        if mask_2d is not None:
            key_mask = mask_2d.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)   # (B,1,1,N)
            scores = scores.masked_fill(~key_mask, float('-inf'))

        attn_local = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
        attn_local = self.dropout(attn_local)
        local_out = torch.einsum('bhnm,bmhd->bnhd', attn_local, V)   # (B,N,h,d)

        # ---- Global linear attention ----
        phi = lambda x: F.elu(x) + 1

        if self.prune_gate is not None:
            keep = torch.sigmoid(self.prune_gate(hidden_states))   # (B,N,h)
            Kg = K * keep.unsqueeze(-1)
            Vg = V * keep.unsqueeze(-1)
        else:
            Kg, Vg = K, V
        if mask_2d is not None:
            m4 = mask_2d.unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)
            Kg = Kg * m4
            Vg = Vg * m4

        phi_K = phi(Kg)
        phi_Q = phi(Q)
        phi_K_t = phi_K.permute(0, 2, 3, 1)          # (B,h,d,N)
        Vg_t = Vg.permute(0, 2, 1, 3)                # (B,h,N,d)

        KV = torch.matmul(phi_K_t, Vg_t)              # (B,h,d,d)
        ones = torch.ones(B, self.num_heads, N, 1, device=Kg.device)
        Z = torch.matmul(phi_K_t, ones)               # (B,h,d,1)

        phi_Q_t = phi_Q.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).unsqueeze(-2)   # (B,h,N,1,d)
        global_out = torch.matmul(phi_Q_t, KV.unsqueeze(2)).squeeze(-2)  # (B,h,N,d)
        norm = torch.matmul(phi_Q_t, Z.unsqueeze(2)).squeeze(-2)          # (B,h,N,1)
        global_out = global_out / (norm + 1e-8)
        global_out = global_out.permute(0, 2, 1, 3)     # (B,N,h,d)

        if mask_2d is not None:
            global_out = global_out * mask_2d.unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)

        # ---- Adaptive fusion ----
        alpha = torch.sigmoid(self.fusion_gate(hidden_states))   # (B,N,h)
        alpha = alpha.unsqueeze(-1)   # (B,N,h,1)
        fused = alpha * local_out + (1 - alpha) * global_out

        fused = fused.reshape(B, N, H)
        out = self.out_proj2(self.out_proj1(fused))
        out = self.dropout(out)
        if mask_2d is not None:
            out = out * mask_2d.unsqueeze(-1)

        return (out, None)

    def get_ortho_loss(self):
        return torch.tensor(0.0, device=self.out_proj2.weight.device)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Registry integration
# ------------------------------------------------------------
from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("aegis_v2")
def inject_aegis_v2(layer, model_config, comp_config, layer_idx, total_layers, old_attn):
    cfg = AegisAttentionConfigV2(
        hidden_size=model_config.hidden_size,
        num_attention_heads=model_config.num_attention_heads,
        head_dim=model_config.hidden_size // model_config.num_attention_heads,
        local_window=getattr(comp_config, 'block_size', 64),
        rank=getattr(comp_config, 'lora_r', 64),
        kernel_feature_dim=getattr(comp_config, 'rff_features', 128),
        dropout=getattr(comp_config, 'dropout', 0.1),
        use_adaptive_pruning=getattr(comp_config, 'use_gate', True),
    )
    new_attn = AegisAttentionV2(cfg)

    # Transfer weights from pretrained
    AegisAttentionV2._svd_init(old_attn.q_lin, new_attn.q_proj1, new_attn.q_proj2, cfg.rank)
    AegisAttentionV2._svd_init(old_attn.k_lin, new_attn.k_proj1, new_attn.k_proj2, cfg.rank)
    AegisAttentionV2._svd_init(old_attn.v_lin, new_attn.v_proj1, new_attn.v_proj2, cfg.rank)
    AegisAttentionV2._svd_init(old_attn.out_lin, new_attn.out_proj1, new_attn.out_proj2, cfg.rank)

    layer.attention = new_attn
    return new_attn

══════════════════ aegis_v3.py ══════════════════
"""
AEGIS‑Attention V3 — Simplified hybrid with SVD‑factorised projections,
local window attention, global kernel linear attention, and per‑head blending.
"""
import math, torch, torch.nn as nn, torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Optional, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass
class AegisConfig:
    svd_energy: float = 0.95
    min_rank: int = 4
    max_rank: int = 64
    num_local_heads: int = 8
    num_global_heads: int = 4
    local_window: int = 64
    softpick_temperature: float = 1.0
    rff_features: int = 256
    use_zero_sum_keys: bool = True
    use_adaptive_gate: bool = True
    gate_init_bias: float = 1.5
    dropout: float = 0.1

class FactorisedLinear(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, pretrained_weight, pretrained_bias, energy, min_rank=4, max_rank=64):
        super().__init__()
        out_dim, in_dim = pretrained_weight.shape
        with torch.no_grad():
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(pretrained_weight.float(), full_matrices=False)
            total = (S ** 2).sum()
            cum = torch.cumsum(S ** 2, dim=0) / total
            rank = int(torch.searchsorted(cum, energy).item()) + 1
            rank = max(min_rank, min(rank, min(out_dim, in_dim), max_rank))
            self.rank = rank
            A = U[:, :rank] * S[:rank].unsqueeze(0)
            B = Vt[:rank, :]
        self.down = nn.Linear(in_dim, rank, bias=False)
        self.up   = nn.Linear(rank, out_dim, bias=(pretrained_bias is not None))
        self.down.weight.data = B.float()
        self.up.weight.data   = A.float()
        if pretrained_bias is not None:
            self.up.bias.data = pretrained_bias.float()
        else:
            self.up.bias.data.zero_()
    def forward(self, x): return self.up(self.down(x))

def softpick(x, dim=-1, temperature=1.0):
    m = x.max(dim=dim, keepdim=True).values
    e = torch.exp((x - m) / temperature)
    denom = e - torch.exp(-m / temperature)
    p = F.relu(denom)
    return p / (p.sum(dim=dim, keepdim=True) + 1e-9)

def local_window_attention(Q, K, V, window, temperature, token_valid=None):
    B, H, N, D = Q.shape
    scale = D ** -0.5
    idx = torch.arange(N, device=Q.device)
    dist = (idx.unsqueeze(0) - idx.unsqueeze(1)).abs()
    mask = (dist <= window // 2).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)
    scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
    scores = scores.masked_fill(~mask, -1e9)
    if token_valid is not None:
        scores = scores.masked_fill(~token_valid.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e9)
    attn = softpick(scores, dim=-1, temperature=temperature)
    return torch.matmul(attn, V)

def kernel_linear_attention(Q, K, V, rff_proj, use_zero_sum=True):
    B, H, N, D = Q.shape
    F_dim = rff_proj.size(-1)
    if use_zero_sum:
        K = K - K.mean(dim=2, keepdim=True)
    Q_norm = F.normalize(Q, p=2, dim=-1)
    K_norm = F.normalize(K, p=2, dim=-1)
    Q_proj = torch.einsum('bhnd,hdf->bhnf', Q_norm, rff_proj)
    K_proj = torch.einsum('bhnd,hdf->bhnf', K_norm, rff_proj)
    Q_feat = torch.exp(Q_proj) / math.sqrt(F_dim)
    K_feat = torch.exp(K_proj) / math.sqrt(F_dim)
    KV = torch.einsum('bhnf,bhnd->bhfd', K_feat, V)
    Z  = K_feat.sum(dim=2, keepdim=True)
    ctx = torch.einsum('bhnf,bhfd->bhnd', Q_feat, KV)
    ctx = ctx / (torch.einsum('bhnf,bhfd->bhnd', Q_feat, Z) + 1e-8)
    return ctx

class AegisAttention(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, model_config, aegis_cfg: AegisConfig, pretrained_attn=None, bert_mode=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_heads = model_config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = model_config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.hidden_size = model_config.hidden_size
        self.cfg = aegis_cfg
        self.bert_mode = bert_mode
        assert aegis_cfg.num_local_heads + aegis_cfg.num_global_heads == self.num_heads
        self.num_local_heads = aegis_cfg.num_local_heads
        self.num_global_heads = aegis_cfg.num_global_heads
        if pretrained_attn is None:
            raise ValueError("AegisAttention requires pretrained_attn for zero‑shot init")
        self.q_proj = FactorisedLinear(pretrained_attn.q_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.q_lin.bias.data,
                                       aegis_cfg.svd_energy, aegis_cfg.min_rank, aegis_cfg.max_rank)
        self.k_proj = FactorisedLinear(pretrained_attn.k_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.k_lin.bias.data,
                                       aegis_cfg.svd_energy, aegis_cfg.min_rank, aegis_cfg.max_rank)
        self.v_proj = FactorisedLinear(pretrained_attn.v_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.v_lin.bias.data,
                                       aegis_cfg.svd_energy, aegis_cfg.min_rank, aegis_cfg.max_rank)
        self.out_proj = FactorisedLinear(pretrained_attn.out_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.out_lin.bias.data,
                                         aegis_cfg.svd_energy, aegis_cfg.min_rank, aegis_cfg.max_rank)
        self.rff_proj = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(self.num_global_heads, self.head_dim, aegis_cfg.rff_features) * 0.02)
        if aegis_cfg.use_adaptive_gate:
            self.blend_logits = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.num_heads))
            self.gate_bias = aegis_cfg.gate_init_bias
        else:
            self.blend_logits = None
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(aegis_cfg.dropout)

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, head_mask=None, output_attentions=False, **kwargs):
        B, N, _ = hidden_states.shape
        H, D = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        Hl, Hg = self.num_local_heads, self.num_global_heads

        Q_all = self.q_proj(hidden_states)
        K_all = self.k_proj(hidden_states)
        V_all = self.v_proj(hidden_states)
        Q_all = Q_all.view(B, N, H, D).transpose(1, 2)
        K_all = K_all.view(B, N, H, D).transpose(1, 2)
        V_all = V_all.view(B, N, H, D).transpose(1, 2)

        Q_local, Q_global = Q_all[:, :Hl], Q_all[:, Hl:]
        K_local, K_global = K_all[:, :Hl], K_all[:, Hl:]
        V_local, V_global = V_all[:, :Hl], V_all[:, Hl:]

        mask = attention_mask if attention_mask is not None else kwargs.get('attn_mask', None)
        token_valid = None
        if mask is not None:
            if mask.dim() == 4 and mask.shape[-2] == mask.shape[-1]:
                token_valid = mask[:, 0, 0, :] > -1e9
            elif mask.dim() == 4:
                token_valid = mask.squeeze(1).squeeze(1) > -1e9
            elif mask.dim() == 3:
                token_valid = mask.squeeze(1).bool()
            elif mask.dim() == 2:
                token_valid = mask.bool()

        ctx_local = local_window_attention(Q_local, K_local, V_local, self.cfg.local_window,
                                           self.cfg.softpick_temperature, token_valid)
        ctx_global = kernel_linear_attention(Q_global, K_global, V_global, self.rff_proj,
                                            self.cfg.use_zero_sum_keys)

        if self.blend_logits is not None:
            global_signal = ctx_global.mean(dim=1, keepdim=True).expand(-1, H, -1, -1)
            local_full = torch.cat([ctx_local, torch.zeros(B, Hg, N, D, device=ctx_local.device)], dim=1)
            gate = torch.sigmoid(self.blend_logits.unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1) + self.gate_bias)
            context = gate * local_full + (1 - gate) * global_signal
        else:
            context = torch.cat([ctx_local, ctx_global], dim=1)

        if token_valid is not None:
            context = context * token_valid.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1).to(context.dtype)

        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N, H * D)
        output = self.out_proj(context)
        return (output, None)

    def get_ortho_loss(self):
        return torch.tensor(0.0, device=self.out_proj.up.weight.device)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Framework registration
# ------------------------------------------------------------
from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("aegis_v3")
def inject_aegis_v3(layer, model_config, comp_config, layer_idx, total_layers, old_attn):
    cfg = AegisConfig(
        num_local_heads=8,
        num_global_heads=4,
        local_window=getattr(comp_config, 'block_size', 64),
        rff_features=getattr(comp_config, 'rff_features', 256),
        dropout=getattr(comp_config, 'dropout', 0.1),
    )
    new_attn = AegisAttention(model_config=model_config, aegis_cfg=cfg, pretrained_attn=old_attn)
    layer.attention = new_attn
    return new_attn

══════════════════ compressed_attention.py ══════════════════
"""SVD compression of existing MultiheadAttention layers."""

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
import numpy as np

class CompressedAttentionLayer(nn.Module):
    """Lightweight SVD compression of nn.MultiheadAttention."""
    def __init__(self, original_layer: nn.MultiheadAttention, rank_qkv: int, rank_out: int, method: str = "svd"):
        super().__init__()
        self.original_dim = original_layer.embed_dim
        self.num_heads = original_layer.num_heads
        self.head_dim = self.original_dim // self.num_heads
        self.method = method

        # Extract weights
        w_qkv = original_layer.in_proj_weight.data.cpu().numpy()
        w_o = original_layer.out_proj.weight.data.cpu().numpy()

        U_qkv, S_qkv, Vh_qkv = np.linalg.svd(w_qkv, full_matrices=False)
        U_out, S_out, Vh_out = np.linalg.svd(w_o, full_matrices=False)

        rank_qkv = min(rank_qkv, w_qkv.shape[1])
        rank_out = min(rank_out, w_o.shape[1])

        self.rank_qkv = rank_qkv
        self.rank_out = rank_out

        self.U_qkv = nn.Parameter(torch.from_numpy(U_qkv[:, :rank_qkv]).float())
        self.S_qkv_sqrt = nn.Parameter(torch.from_numpy(np.sqrt(S_qkv[:rank_qkv])).float())
        self.Vh_qkv = nn.Parameter(torch.from_numpy(Vh_qkv[:rank_qkv, :]).float())

        self.U_out = nn.Parameter(torch.from_numpy(U_out[:, :rank_out]).float())
        self.S_out_sqrt = nn.Parameter(torch.from_numpy(np.sqrt(S_out[:rank_out])).float())
        self.Vh_out = nn.Parameter(torch.from_numpy(Vh_out[:rank_out, :]).float())

        # Biases
        if original_layer.in_proj_bias is not None:
            self.bias_qkv = nn.Parameter(original_layer.in_proj_bias.data.clone())
        else:
            self.bias_qkv = None
        if original_layer.out_proj.bias is not None:
            self.bias_out = nn.Parameter(original_layer.out_proj.bias.data.clone())
        else:
            self.bias_out = None

        # Pass‑through attributes
        self.dropout_p = original_layer.dropout
        self.batch_first = getattr(original_layer, 'batch_first', False)
        self.kdim = getattr(original_layer, 'kdim', None)
        self.vdim = getattr(original_layer, 'vdim', None)
        self.add_bias_kv = getattr(original_layer, 'add_bias_kv', False)
        self.add_zero_attn = getattr(original_layer, 'add_zero_attn', False)

    def forward(self, query, key, value, key_padding_mask=None, need_weights=True,
                attn_mask=None, average_attn_weights=True):
        x = query
        dim = self.original_dim

        # Compressed QKV projection
        projected_x = torch.matmul(x, self.Vh_qkv.t())
        qkv_compressed = torch.matmul(projected_x, self.U_qkv.t() * self.S_qkv_sqrt.view(1, -1, 1))
        if self.bias_qkv is not None:
            qkv_compressed += self.bias_qkv

        q = qkv_compressed[:, :, :dim]
        k = qkv_compressed[:, :, dim:2*dim]
        v = qkv_compressed[:, :, 2*dim:]

        scale = 1.0 / np.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        attn_scores = torch.matmul(q, k.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
        if attn_mask is not None:
            attn_scores += attn_mask
        if key_padding_mask is not None:
            attn_scores = attn_scores.masked_fill(key_padding_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e9)

        attn_weights = F.softmax(attn_scores, dim=-1)
        attn_weights = F.dropout(attn_weights, p=self.dropout_p, training=self.training)

        context = torch.matmul(attn_weights, v)

        # Compressed output projection
        context_fused = torch.matmul(context, self.Vh_out.t() * self.S_out_sqrt.view(1, -1, 1))
        final_output = torch.matmul(context_fused, self.U_out.t())
        if self.bias_out is not None:
            final_output += self.bias_out

        return final_output, attn_weights if need_weights else None


class CompressedAttentionLayer2(CompressedAttentionLayer):
    """SVD compression with energy threshold instead of fixed rank."""
    def __init__(self, original_layer: nn.MultiheadAttention, energy_threshold: float = 0.95, min_rank: int = 16):
        w_qkv = original_layer.in_proj_weight.data.cpu().numpy()
        w_out = original_layer.out_proj.weight.data.cpu().numpy()

        U_qkv, S_qkv, Vh_qkv = np.linalg.svd(w_qkv, full_matrices=False)
        energy_qkv = np.cumsum(S_qkv) / np.sum(S_qkv)
        rank_qkv = np.argmax(energy_qkv >= energy_threshold) + 1
        rank_qkv = max(min_rank, min(rank_qkv, w_qkv.shape[1]))

        U_out, S_out, Vh_out = np.linalg.svd(w_out, full_matrices=False)
        energy_out = np.cumsum(S_out) / np.sum(S_out)
        rank_out = np.argmax(energy_out >= energy_threshold) + 1
        rank_out = max(min_rank, min(rank_out, w_out.shape[1]))

        super().__init__(original_layer, rank_qkv, rank_out)
══════════════════ linformer_attention.py ══════════════════
"""Linformer attention – registers as 'linformer'."""

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from ..core.base import BaseAttention
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("linformer")
class LinformerAttention(BaseAttention):
    def __init__(self, config, rank=64):
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.rank = rank

        self.query = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.key = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.value = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(config.attention_probs_dropout_prob)

        # Linformer projection matrices (learnable)
        self.E = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(config.hidden_size, rank) * 0.02)
        self.F = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(config.hidden_size, rank) * 0.02)

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, **kwargs):
        batch_size, seq_len, _ = hidden_states.shape
        Q = self._transpose(self.query(hidden_states))
        K = self._transpose(self.key(hidden_states))
        V = self._transpose(self.value(hidden_states))

        # Project keys and values
        K_proj = torch.matmul(self.E.T, K)  # (batch, heads, rank, head_dim)
        V_proj = torch.matmul(self.F.T, V)

        scores = torch.matmul(Q, K_proj.transpose(-2, -1)) / (self.head_dim ** 0.5)
        if attention_mask is not None:
            scores = scores + attention_mask.unsqueeze(-1)
        probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
        probs = self.dropout(probs)
        context = torch.matmul(probs, V_proj)

        context = context.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous()
        context = context.view(batch_size, seq_len, -1)
        output_attentions = kwargs.get('output_attentions', False)
        return (context, None) if output_attentions else (context,)

    def _transpose(self, x):
        new_shape = x.size()[:-1] + (self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
        x = x.view(*new_shape)
        return x.permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
══════════════════ svd_attention.py ══════════════════
"""SVD‑based linear attention – registers as 'svd'."""

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from ..core.base import BaseAttention
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("svd")
class SVDLinearAttention(BaseAttention):
    def __init__(self, config, rank=64):
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim

        self.query = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.key = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.value = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(config.attention_probs_dropout_prob)

    def transpose_for_scores(self, x):
        new_shape = x.size()[:-1] + (self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
        x = x.view(*new_shape)
        return x.permute(0, 2, 1, 3)

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, **kwargs):
        batch_size, seq_len, _ = hidden_states.shape
        Q = self.transpose_for_scores(self.query(hidden_states))
        K = self.transpose_for_scores(self.key(hidden_states))
        V = self.transpose_for_scores(self.value(hidden_states))

        # If no compression or sequence too short, use full attention
        if self.rank is None or seq_len <= self.rank:
            scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1)) / (self.head_dim ** 0.5)
            if attention_mask is not None:
                scores = scores + attention_mask
            probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
            probs = self.dropout(probs)
            context = torch.matmul(probs, V)
        else:
            # SVD compression – using low‑rank approximation
            original_device = hidden_states.device
            if original_device.type == 'mps':
                hidden_cpu = hidden_states.cpu()
            else:
                hidden_cpu = hidden_states

            U_r_list = []
            for b in range(batch_size):
                U, S, _ = torch.svd_lowrank(hidden_cpu[b], q=self.rank, niter=2)
                U_r_list.append(U[:, :self.rank])
            U_r = torch.stack(U_r_list, dim=0).to(original_device)
            U_r = U_r.unsqueeze(1)   # (batch, 1, seq_len, rank)

            K_proj = torch.matmul(U_r.transpose(-2, -1), K)
            V_proj = torch.matmul(U_r.transpose(-2, -1), V)

            scores = torch.matmul(Q, K_proj.transpose(-2, -1)) / (self.head_dim ** 0.5)
            if attention_mask is not None:
                scores = scores + attention_mask.unsqueeze(-1)
            probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
            probs = self.dropout(probs)
            context = torch.matmul(probs, V_proj)

        context = context.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous()
        new_shape = context.size()[:-2] + (self.all_head_size,)
        context = context.view(*new_shape)
        output_attentions = kwargs.get('output_attentions', False)
        return (context, None) if output_attentions else (context,)
