══════════════════ nexus.py ══════════════════
"""
NEXUS  Neural Efficient X-scoring Unified Sparse Attention

=============================================================
Novel hybrid attention that fuses the best of ICML/NeurIPS 2025:

 1. XAttention antidiagonal scoring (ICML 2025)      - training-free block scoring
 2. SeerAttention learnable gating (NeurIPS 2025)    - per-head sparsity
 3. DuSA dual-stage sparse (NeurIPS 2025)            - intra + inter block
 4. AnchorAttention stripe granularity (2025)         - fine-grain stripes
 5. Spectraformer random-feature rectification (2025) - global linear fallback
 6. Softpick rectified softmax (2025)                 - natural ~47% sparsity

Papers:
  • XAttention: Xu et al., ICML 2025 (arXiv:2503.16428)
  • SeerAttention: Gao et al., NeurIPS 2025
  • DuSA: Cao et al., NeurIPS 2025
  • AnchorAttention: Zhu et al., 2025 (arXiv:2505.23523)
  • Spectraformer: Nguyen et al., 2025 (arXiv:2405.15310)
  • Softpick: Zuhri et al., arXiv 2504.20966 (2025)

Author: Taha Majs  2026
"""

import math
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Optional, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass
from framework.attentions.core.base import BaseAttention
from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

# ============================================================================
# Configuration
# ============================================================================
@dataclass
class NEXUSConfig:
    # --- Block structure ---
    block_size: int = 32                     # tokens per block (smaller = finer)
    top_k_blocks: int = 4                    # how many inter-block blocks to keep

    # --- XAttention antidiagonal scoring ---
    xattn_enabled: bool = True
    xattn_stride: int = 2                    # stride for antidiagonal sampling

    # --- SeerAttention gating ---
    seer_enabled: bool = True
    seer_gate_rank: int = 16                 # hidden dim of gate network
    seer_top_k_blocks: int = 4               # blocks kept by the learned gate
    seer_temperature: float = 1.0

    # --- DuSA dual-stage ---
    dual_stage_enabled: bool = True          # (unused here, always on)
    intra_block_full: bool = True            # dense inside each block

    # --- AnchorAttention stripe ---
    stripe_enabled: bool = True
    stripe_width: int = 4                    # tokens per stripe

    # --- Spectraformer global rectification ---
    rectify_interval: int = 4                # every Nth layer uses kernel attn
    rff_features: int = 128

    # --- Softpick ---
    softpick_enabled: bool = True
    softpick_temperature: float = 1.0

    dropout: float = 0.1


# ============================================================================
# Utility: Softpick rectified softmax
# ============================================================================
def softpick(x: torch.Tensor, dim: int = -1, temperature: float = 1.0) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Rectified ~47% sparse softmax, no attention sink."""
    m = x.max(dim=dim, keepdim=True).values
    e = torch.exp((x - m) / temperature)
    denom = e - torch.exp(-m / temperature)
    p = F.relu(denom)
    return p / (p.sum(dim=dim, keepdim=True) + 1e-9)


# ============================================================================
# 1. XAttention antidiagonal block scoring
# ============================================================================
class XAttentionScorer:
    """
    Training‑free block importance via antidiagonal sums.
    Reference: XAttention, Xu et al., ICML 2025.
    """
    @staticmethod
    def score_blocks(Q: torch.Tensor, K: torch.Tensor,
                     block_size: int, stride: int = 2) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Q, K: (B, H, N, D)
        Returns: block_score (B, H, n_blocks, n_blocks)
        """
        B, H, N, D = Q.shape
        n_blocks = (N + block_size - 1) // block_size

        # sample tokens at stride for speed
        Q_sample = Q[:, :, ::stride, :]   # (B, H, N_s, D)
        K_sample = K[:, :, ::stride, :]

        # compute a tiny attention map on sampled tokens
        scale = D ** -0.5
        attn_sample = torch.matmul(Q_sample, K_sample.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale

        # for each block, sum antidiagonal values
        scores = torch.zeros(B, H, n_blocks, n_blocks, device=Q.device, dtype=Q.dtype)
        N_s = Q_sample.size(2)

        for bi in range(n_blocks):
            q_start = bi * (block_size // stride)
            q_end = min(q_start + block_size // stride, N_s)
            for bj in range(n_blocks):
                k_start = bj * (block_size // stride)
                k_end = min(k_start + block_size // stride, N_s)
                # antidiagonal: mean over the block
                block = attn_sample[:, :, q_start:q_end, k_start:k_end]
                scores[:, :, bi, bj] = block.mean(dim=(2, 3))

        return scores   # (B, H, n_blocks, n_blocks)


# ============================================================================
# 2. SeerAttention learnable gating
# ============================================================================
class SeerGating(nn.Module):
    """
    Learnable block‑level sparsity gate.
    Reference: SeerAttention, Gao et al., NeurIPS 2025.
    """
    def __init__(self, head_dim: int, num_heads: int,
                 gate_rank: int, top_k: int, temperature: float = 1.0):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_heads = num_heads
        self.head_dim = head_dim
        self.top_k = top_k
        self.temperature = temperature

        # pooling projection for Q and K
        self.q_pool = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(head_dim, gate_rank),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Linear(gate_rank, gate_rank),
        )
        self.k_pool = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(head_dim, gate_rank),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Linear(gate_rank, gate_rank),
        )

    def forward(self, Q: torch.Tensor, K: torch.Tensor,
                n_blocks: int, training: bool = True) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Q, K: (B, H, N, D)
        Returns: hard_mask (B, H, n_blocks, n_blocks)
        """
        B, H, N, D = Q.shape
        gr = self.q_pool[0].out_features   # gate_rank

        # pool over sequence: mean pool Q and K for each head
        Q_pooled = Q.mean(dim=2)   # (B, H, D)
        K_pooled = K.mean(dim=2)

        # project through gate networks
        q_gate = self.q_pool(Q_pooled).view(B, H, 1, gr)   # (B, H, 1, gr)
        k_gate = self.k_pool(K_pooled).view(B, H, gr, 1)

        # produce a scalar gate score per head (we need per‑block)
        gate_score = torch.sigmoid(
            torch.matmul(q_gate, k_gate).squeeze(-1).squeeze(-1) / self.temperature
        )   # (B, H)

        # for each head, build a block mask:
        keep_per_head = torch.clamp(
            (gate_score * n_blocks).long(), min=1, max=n_blocks
        )   # (B, H)

        mask = torch.zeros(B, H, n_blocks, n_blocks, device=Q.device)
        for h in range(H):
            k = keep_per_head[0, h].item()   # batch‑uniform
            mask[:, h, :k, :k] = 1.0

        return mask.bool()

    """
    DuSA‑style intra‑block full + inter‑block XAttention‑scored top‑k,
    with AnchorAttention‑style stripe granularity inside blocks.
    """
    def __init__(self, cfg: NEXUSConfig, head_dim: int):
        super().__init__()

══════════════════ replace.py ══════════════════
"""Helper to replace attention modules in HuggingFace models."""

def replace_attention_in_model(model, attention_name, attention_params=None):
    """
    Replace all self‑attention layers in a model with the specified attention type.
    Supports BERT, RoBERTa, DistilBERT, GPT‑2.
    """
    from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY
    if attention_params is None:
        attention_params = {}

    model_type = getattr(model.config, "model_type", None)

    if model_type in ("bert", "roberta"):
        _replace_bert_attention(model, attention_name, attention_params)
    elif model_type == "gpt2":
        _replace_gpt_attention(model, attention_name, attention_params)
    elif model_type == "distilbert":
        _replace_distilbert_attention(model, attention_name, attention_params)
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported model type: {model_type}")

    return model

def _replace_bert_attention(model, attention_name, attention_params):
    from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY
    for layer in model.encoder.layer:
        old_attn = layer.attention.self
        hidden_size = old_attn.query.in_features
        num_heads = old_attn.num_attention_heads
        new_attn = build_attention(attention_name, hidden_size, num_heads, **attention_params)
        layer.attention.self = new_attn

def _replace_gpt_attention(model, attention_name, attention_params):
    from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY
    for block in model.transformer.h:
        old_attn = block.attn
        hidden_size = old_attn.embed_dim
        num_heads = old_attn.num_heads
        new_attn = build_attention(attention_name, hidden_size, num_heads, **attention_params)
        block.attn = new_attn

def _replace_distilbert_attention(model, attention_name, attention_params):
    from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY
    for layer in model.distilbert.transformer.layer:
        old_attn = layer.attention
        # DistilBERT attention has q_lin, k_lin, v_lin, out_lin
        hidden_size = old_attn.q_lin.in_features
        num_heads = getattr(model.config, "num_attention_heads", 12)
        # Build a custom `inject` function instead of a class directly
        # But we rely on the registry's inject function for the given key.
        inject_fn = ATTENTION_REGISTRY.get(attention_name)
        if inject_fn is None:
            raise ValueError(f"Attention '{attention_name}' not registered.")
        inject_fn(layer, model.config, None, 0, len(model.distilbert.transformer.layer), old_attn)

def build_attention(attention_name, hidden_size, num_heads, **kwargs):
    """Helper to instantiate an attention class (not injection)."""
    # This is a simplified version; the registry expects an injection function,
    # not a class. For simplicity, we raise an error – use the injectors instead.
    raise NotImplementedError("Use the registry inject functions with a full model.")

══════════════════ prism_full.py ══════════════════
"""
PRISM — Pruning‑aware Residual Integrated Sparse Memory Attention
Full version with six compression techniques.
"""

import math
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Optional, Tuple, Dict, List
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass
class PRISMConfig:
    energy_threshold: float = 0.95
    min_rank: int = 4
    max_rank: int = 64
    armor_enabled: bool = True
    armor_block_size: int = 8
    ast_enabled: bool = True
    ast_sparsity: float = 0.5
    ast_mask_lr: float = 0.01
    deltakv_enabled: bool = True
    deltakv_max_refs: int = 128
    deltakv_similarity_topk: int = 4
    kvt_enabled: bool = True
    kvt_quant_bits: int = 4
    kvt_pca_components: float = 0.95
    softpick_enabled: bool = True
    softpick_temperature: float = 1.0
    dropout: float = 0.1

def softpick(x: torch.Tensor, dim: int = -1, temperature: float = 1.0) -> torch.Tensor:
    x = x / temperature
    p = F.softmax(x, dim=dim)
    threshold = p.mean(dim=dim, keepdim=True)
    p_rect = torch.where(p > threshold, p, torch.zeros_like(p))
    denom = p_rect.sum(dim=dim, keepdim=True) + 1e-9
    return p_rect / denom

class BlockDiagonal(nn.Module):
    """Block‑diagonal matrix for ARMOR structured sparsity."""
    def __init__(self, dim: int, block_size: int, bias: bool = False):
        super().__init__()
        self.dim = dim
        self.bs = block_size
        self.padded_dim = ((dim + block_size - 1) // block_size) * block_size
        n_blocks = self.padded_dim // block_size
        self.weight = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(n_blocks, block_size, block_size))
        nn.init.normal_(self.weight, std=0.02)
        self.bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(dim)) if bias else None

    def forward(self, x):
        *batch, D = x.shape
        if self.padded_dim > D:
            pad = torch.zeros(*batch, self.padded_dim - D, device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype)
            x = torch.cat([x, pad], dim=-1)
        n_blocks = self.padded_dim // self.bs
        x = x.view(*batch, n_blocks, self.bs)
        x = torch.einsum('...nb,nbc->...nc', x, self.weight)
        x = x.reshape(*batch, self.padded_dim)
        x = x[..., :D]
        if self.bias is not None:
            x = x + self.bias
        return x

class KVTQuantiser:
    """PCA‑based adaptive quantisation for K/V."""
    def __init__(self, n_bits: int = 4, pca_components: float = 0.95):
        self.n_bits = n_bits
        self.pca_components = pca_components
        self.pca_basis: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
        self.scale: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
        self.zero_point: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
        self.fitted = False

    @torch.no_grad()
    def fit(self, k: torch.Tensor, v: torch.Tensor):
        B, H, N, D = k.shape
        X = torch.cat([k.reshape(-1, D), v.reshape(-1, D)], dim=0)
        X = X.float()
        mean = X.mean(dim=0, keepdim=True)
        X_centered = X - mean
        U, S, _ = torch.linalg.svd(X_centered, full_matrices=False)
        total_var = S.pow(2).sum()
        cum_var = torch.cumsum(S.pow(2), dim=0) / total_var
        rank = torch.searchsorted(cum_var, self.pca_components).item() + 1
        rank = min(rank, D)
        self.pca_basis = U[:, :rank].contiguous()
        projected = X_centered @ self.pca_basis
        self.scale = (projected.max(dim=0).values - projected.min(dim=0).values) / (2 ** self.n_bits - 1)
        self.scale = self.scale.clamp(min=1e-8)
        self.zero_point = projected.min(dim=0).values
        self.fitted = True

    def compress(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        if not self.fitted or self.pca_basis is None:
            return x
        *B, D = x.shape
        xc = x.float() - x.float().mean(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
        proj = xc @ self.pca_basis.to(x.device)
        q = ((proj - self.zero_point.to(x.device)) / self.scale.to(x.device)).round()
        dq = q * self.scale.to(x.device) + self.zero_point.to(x.device)
        return (dq @ self.pca_basis.T.to(x.device)).to(x.dtype)

class PRISMAttention(nn.Module):
    """
    PRISM attention: 6 integrated compression techniques.
    - SVD rank reduction on projections
    - ARMOR block‑diagonal structured sparsity
    - AST learned sparse masking
    - DeltaKV residual compression via reference tokens
    - KVT PCA quantisation of keys/values
    - Softpick sparse activation
    """
    def __init__(self, model_config, prism_cfg: PRISMConfig, layer_idx=0, total_layers=6,
                 pretrained_attn=None, bert_mode=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_heads = model_config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = model_config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.hidden_size = model_config.hidden_size
        self.cfg = prism_cfg
        self.layer_idx = layer_idx
        self.bert_mode = bert_mode

        if pretrained_attn is not None:
            q_w = pretrained_attn.q_lin.weight.data
            k_w = pretrained_attn.k_lin.weight.data
            v_w = pretrained_attn.v_lin.weight.data
            out_w = pretrained_attn.out_lin.weight.data

            self.q_rank = self._select_rank(q_w)
            self.k_rank = self._select_rank(k_w)
            self.v_rank = self._select_rank(v_w)
            self.out_rank = self._select_rank(out_w)

            self.q_down, self.q_up = self._svd_factorise(q_w, pretrained_attn.q_lin.bias, self.q_rank, self.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
            self.k_down, self.k_up = self._svd_factorise(k_w, pretrained_attn.k_lin.bias, self.k_rank, self.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
            self.v_down, self.v_up = self._svd_factorise(v_w, pretrained_attn.v_lin.bias, self.v_rank, self.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
            self.out_down, self.out_up = self._svd_factorise(out_w, pretrained_attn.out_lin.bias, self.out_rank, self.all_head_size, self.hidden_size)
        else:
            self.q_rank = self.k_rank = self.v_rank = self.out_rank = self.head_dim
            self.q_down = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.head_dim, bias=False)
            self.q_up = nn.Linear(self.head_dim, self.all_head_size, bias=True)
            self.k_down = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.head_dim, bias=False)
            self.k_up = nn.Linear(self.head_dim, self.all_head_size, bias=True)
            self.v_down = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.head_dim, bias=False)
            self.v_up = nn.Linear(self.head_dim, self.all_head_size, bias=True)
            self.out_down = nn.Linear(self.all_head_size, self.head_dim, bias=False)
            self.out_up = nn.Linear(self.head_dim, self.hidden_size, bias=True)

        self.armor_q = self.armor_k = self.armor_v = self.armor_out = None
        if self.cfg.armor_enabled:
            self.armor_q = BlockDiagonal(self.all_head_size, self.cfg.armor_block_size)
            self.armor_k = BlockDiagonal(self.all_head_size, self.cfg.armor_block_size)
            self.armor_v = BlockDiagonal(self.all_head_size, self.cfg.armor_block_size)
            self.armor_out = BlockDiagonal(self.hidden_size, self.cfg.armor_block_size)

        self.ast_q_logits = self.ast_k_logits = self.ast_v_logits = self.ast_out_logits = None
        if self.cfg.ast_enabled:
            self.ast_q_logits = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.q_rank))
            self.ast_k_logits = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.k_rank))
            self.ast_v_logits = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.v_rank))
            self.ast_out_logits = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.out_rank))

        self.ref_k: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
        self.ref_v: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
        self.ref_count: int = 0
        self.kvt: Optional[KVTQuantiser] = None
        if self.cfg.kvt_enabled:
            self.kvt = KVTQuantiser(self.cfg.kvt_quant_bits, self.cfg.kvt_pca_components)

        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(self.cfg.dropout)

    def _select_rank(self, W):
        with torch.no_grad():
            _, S, _ = torch.linalg.svd(W.float(), full_matrices=False)
            total_e = S.pow(2).sum()
            cum_e = torch.cumsum(S.pow(2), dim=0) / total_e
            idx = torch.searchsorted(cum_e, self.cfg.energy_threshold)
            rank = int(idx.item()) + 1
            max_r = min(W.shape[0], W.shape[1])
            return max(self.cfg.min_rank, min(rank, max_r, self.cfg.max_rank))

    @staticmethod
    def _svd_factorise(W, bias, rank, in_dim, out_dim):
        U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W.float(), full_matrices=False)
        down_w = Vt[:rank, :].float()
        up_w = (U[:, :rank] * S[:rank].unsqueeze(0)).float()
        down = nn.Linear(in_dim, rank, bias=False)
        up = nn.Linear(rank, out_dim, bias=True)
        down.weight.data = down_w
        up.weight.data = up_w
        if bias is not None:
            up.bias.data = bias.float()
        else:
            up.bias.data.zero_()
        return down, up


══════════════════ minimal_prism.py ══════════════════
"""
MinimalPRISM – stable, zero‑shot, drop‑in replacement for DistilBERT.
SVD‑based low‑rank factorisation of Q/K/V/Out projections only.
"""

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Optional, Tuple

class FactorisedLinear(nn.Module):
    """SVD‑factorised linear layer."""
    def __init__(self, pretrained_weight, pretrained_bias, energy=0.95):
        super().__init__()
        out_dim, in_dim = pretrained_weight.shape
        with torch.no_grad():
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(pretrained_weight.float(), full_matrices=False)
            total = (S ** 2).sum()
            cum = torch.cumsum(S ** 2, dim=0) / total
            rank = int(torch.searchsorted(cum, energy).item()) + 1
            rank = max(4, min(rank, min(out_dim, in_dim), 64))
            self.rank = rank
            A = U[:, :rank] * S[:rank].unsqueeze(0)
            B = Vt[:rank, :]
        self.down = nn.Linear(in_dim, rank, bias=False)
        self.up   = nn.Linear(rank, out_dim, bias=(pretrained_bias is not None))
        self.down.weight.data = B.float()
        self.up.weight.data   = A.float()
        if pretrained_bias is not None:
            self.up.bias.data = pretrained_bias.float()
        else:
            self.up.bias.data.zero_()
    def forward(self, x): return self.up(self.down(x))

class MinimalPRISMAttention(nn.Module):
    """SVD‑factorised attention, full standard attention, zero‑shot."""
    def __init__(self, model_config, energy=0.95, dropout=0.1, pretrained_attn=None):
        super().__init__()
        if pretrained_attn is None:
            raise ValueError("pretrained_attn required for zero‑shot init")
        self.num_heads = model_config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = model_config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.hidden_size = model_config.hidden_size

        self.q_proj = FactorisedLinear(pretrained_attn.q_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.q_lin.bias.data, energy)
        self.k_proj = FactorisedLinear(pretrained_attn.k_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.k_lin.bias.data, energy)
        self.v_proj = FactorisedLinear(pretrained_attn.v_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.v_lin.bias.data, energy)
        self.out_proj = FactorisedLinear(pretrained_attn.out_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.out_lin.bias.data, energy)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, head_mask=None, output_attentions=False, **kwargs):
        B, N, _ = hidden_states.shape
        H, D = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        Q = self.q_proj(hidden_states)
        K = self.k_proj(hidden_states)
        V = self.v_proj(hidden_states)
        Q = Q.view(B, N, H, D).transpose(1, 2)
        K = K.view(B, N, H, D).transpose(1, 2)
        V = V.view(B, N, H, D).transpose(1, 2)

        scale = D ** -0.5
        scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale

        mask = attention_mask if attention_mask is not None else kwargs.get('attn_mask', None)
        if mask is not None:
            if mask.dim() == 4 and mask.shape[-2] == mask.shape[-1]:
                token_valid = mask[:, 0, 0, :] > -1e9
            elif mask.dim() == 4:
                token_valid = mask.squeeze(1).squeeze(1) > -1e9
            elif mask.dim() == 3:
                token_valid = mask.squeeze(1).bool()
            elif mask.dim() == 2:
                token_valid = mask.bool()
            else:
                token_valid = None
            if token_valid is not None:
                scores = scores.masked_fill(~token_valid.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e5)

        attn_weights = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
        attn_weights = self.dropout(attn_weights)
        context = torch.matmul(attn_weights, V)
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N, H * D)
        output = self.out_proj(context)
        return (output, attn_weights) if output_attentions else (output,)

    def get_ortho_loss(self):
        return torch.tensor(0.0, device=self.out_proj.up.weight.device)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Framework injection & registration
# ------------------------------------------------------------
from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("minimal_prism")
def inject_minimal_prism(layer, model_config, comp_config, layer_idx, total_layers, old_attn):
    new_attn = MinimalPRISMAttention(
        model_config=model_config,
        energy=getattr(comp_config, 'svd_energy', 0.95),
        dropout=getattr(comp_config, 'dropout', 0.1),
        pretrained_attn=old_attn
    )
    layer.attention = new_attn
    return new_attn

from . import orion
══════════════════ orion.py ══════════════════
"""
ORION — Optimized Rectified Input-aware Orthogonal Network
Hybrid attention: SVD‑factorised projections + head‑wise kernel/full attention + Softpick.
"""

import math
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Optional, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass
class ORIONConfig:
    svd_energy: float = 0.95
    svd_min_rank: int = 4
    svd_max_rank: int = 64
    num_kernel_heads: int = 8
    num_full_heads: int = 4
    rff_features: int = 256
    softpick_temperature: float = 1.0
    dropout: float = 0.1


def softpick(x: torch.Tensor, dim: int = -1, temperature: float = 1.0) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Rectified softmax – avoids attention sinks, ~47% sparse."""
    m = x.max(dim=dim, keepdim=True).values
    e = torch.exp((x - m) / temperature)
    denom = e - torch.exp(-m / temperature)
    p = F.relu(denom)
    return p / (p.sum(dim=dim, keepdim=True) + 1e-9)


class FactorisedLinear(nn.Module):
    """SVD‑factorised linear layer with energy‑based rank selection."""
    def __init__(self, pretrained_weight: torch.Tensor, pretrained_bias: Optional[torch.Tensor],
                 energy: float, min_rank: int = 4, max_rank: int = 64):
        super().__init__()
        out_dim, in_dim = pretrained_weight.shape
        with torch.no_grad():
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(pretrained_weight.float(), full_matrices=False)
            total = (S ** 2).sum()
            cum = torch.cumsum(S ** 2, dim=0) / total
            rank = int(torch.searchsorted(cum, energy).item()) + 1
            rank = max(min_rank, min(rank, min(out_dim, in_dim), max_rank))
            self.rank = rank
            A = U[:, :rank] * S[:rank].unsqueeze(0)
            B = Vt[:rank, :]
        self.down = nn.Linear(in_dim, rank, bias=False)
        self.up   = nn.Linear(rank, out_dim, bias=(pretrained_bias is not None))
        self.down.weight.data = B.float()
        self.up.weight.data   = A.float()
        if pretrained_bias is not None:
            self.up.bias.data = pretrained_bias.float()
        else:
            self.up.bias.data.zero_()

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.up(self.down(x))


class ORIONAttention(nn.Module):
    """
    ORION hybrid attention.
    Heads are split into two groups:
      - Kernel heads: perform linearised attention via Random Fourier Features.
      - Full heads: perform standard scaled dot‑product attention with Softpick.
    All projections are SVD‑factorised for parameter efficiency.
    """
    def __init__(self, model_config, orion_cfg: ORIONConfig,
                 pretrained_attn=None, bert_mode: bool = False):
        super().__init__()
        if pretrained_attn is None:
            raise ValueError("ORION requires pretrained_attn for zero‑shot SVD init")

        self.num_heads = model_config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim  = model_config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.hidden_size   = model_config.hidden_size
        self.cfg = orion_cfg
        self.bert_mode = bert_mode

        # Validate head split
        if orion_cfg.num_kernel_heads + orion_cfg.num_full_heads != self.num_heads:
            raise ValueError(
                f"num_kernel_heads ({orion_cfg.num_kernel_heads}) + "
                f"num_full_heads ({orion_cfg.num_full_heads}) must equal "
                f"total heads ({self.num_heads})"
            )
        self.num_kernel_heads = orion_cfg.num_kernel_heads
        self.num_full_heads   = orion_cfg.num_full_heads

        # Factorised Q/K/V projections
        self.q_proj = FactorisedLinear(
            pretrained_attn.q_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.q_lin.bias.data,
            orion_cfg.svd_energy, orion_cfg.svd_min_rank, orion_cfg.svd_max_rank
        )
        self.k_proj = FactorisedLinear(
            pretrained_attn.k_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.k_lin.bias.data,
            orion_cfg.svd_energy, orion_cfg.svd_min_rank, orion_cfg.svd_max_rank
        )
        self.v_proj = FactorisedLinear(
            pretrained_attn.v_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.v_lin.bias.data,
            orion_cfg.svd_energy, orion_cfg.svd_min_rank, orion_cfg.svd_max_rank
        )
        self.out_proj = FactorisedLinear(
            pretrained_attn.out_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.out_lin.bias.data,
            orion_cfg.svd_energy, orion_cfg.svd_min_rank, orion_cfg.svd_max_rank
        )

        # Random Fourier Features for kernel attention (per kernel head)
        self.rff_proj = nn.Parameter(
            torch.randn(self.num_kernel_heads, self.head_dim, orion_cfg.rff_features) * 0.02
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(orion_cfg.dropout)

    def _kernel_linear_attention(self, Q: torch.Tensor, K: torch.Tensor, V: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Linearised attention via Random Fourier Features.
        Q, K, V: (B, num_kernel_heads, N, head_dim)
        """
        B, Hk, N, D = Q.shape
        F_dim = self.cfg.rff_features

        # Normalize Q, K for stability
        Q_norm = F.normalize(Q, p=2, dim=-1)
        K_norm = F.normalize(K, p=2, dim=-1)

        proj = self.rff_proj.to(Q.device)  # (Hk, D, F)
        Q_proj = torch.einsum('bhnd,hdf->bhnf', Q_norm, proj)  # (B, Hk, N, F)
        K_proj = torch.einsum('bhnd,hdf->bhnf', K_norm, proj)

        # Apply RBF kernel approximation: exp(Q'K / √F) ≈ φ(Q)·φ(K)
        Q_feat = torch.exp(Q_proj) / math.sqrt(F_dim)
        K_feat = torch.exp(K_proj) / math.sqrt(F_dim)

        # Compute linearised attention: (φ(K)^T V) then multiply by φ(Q)
        KV = torch.einsum('bhnf,bhnd->bhfd', K_feat, V)        # (B, Hk, F, D)
        Z  = K_feat.sum(dim=2, keepdim=True)                   # (B, Hk, 1, F)

        lin_ctx = torch.einsum('bhnf,bhfd->bhnd', Q_feat, KV)  # (B, Hk, N, D)
        lin_ctx = lin_ctx / (torch.einsum('bhnf,bhfd->bhnd', Q_feat, Z) + 1e-8)
        return lin_ctx

    def _full_softpick_attention(self, Q: torch.Tensor, K: torch.Tensor, V: torch.Tensor,
                                 token_valid: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Standard scaled dot‑product attention with Softpick activation."""
        scale = self.head_dim ** -0.5
        scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
        if token_valid is not None:
            scores = scores.masked_fill(~token_valid.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e5)
        attn_w = softpick(scores, dim=-1, temperature=self.cfg.softpick_temperature)
        attn_w = self.dropout(attn_w)
        return torch.matmul(attn_w, V)

    def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
                attention_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
                head_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
                output_attentions: bool = False,
                **kwargs) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, Optional[torch.Tensor]]:
        B, N, _ = hidden_states.shape
        Hk = self.num_kernel_heads
        Hf = self.num_full_heads

        # Project and reshape
        Q_all = self.q_proj(hidden_states)
        K_all = self.k_proj(hidden_states)
        V_all = self.v_proj(hidden_states)
        Q_all = Q_all.view(B, N, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)
        K_all = K_all.view(B, N, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)
        V_all = V_all.view(B, N, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)

        # Split heads
        Q_kernel, Q_full = Q_all[:, :Hk], Q_all[:, Hk:]
        K_kernel, K_full = K_all[:, :Hk], K_all[:, Hk:]
        V_kernel, V_full = V_all[:, :Hk], V_all[:, Hk:]

        # Convert mask to token_valid boolean
        mask = attention_mask if attention_mask is not None else kwargs.get('attn_mask', None)
        token_valid = None
        if mask is not None:
            if mask.dim() == 4 and mask.shape[-2] == mask.shape[-1]:
                token_valid = mask[:, 0, 0, :] > -1e9
            elif mask.dim() == 4:
                token_valid = mask.squeeze(1).squeeze(1) > -1e9
            elif mask.dim() == 3:
                token_valid = mask.squeeze(1).bool()
            elif mask.dim() == 2:
                token_valid = mask.bool()

        # Compute attention for each group
        ctx_kernel = self._kernel_linear_attention(Q_kernel, K_kernel, V_kernel)
        ctx_full = self._full_softpick_attention(Q_full, K_full, V_full, token_valid)

        # Concatenate back
        context = torch.cat([ctx_kernel, ctx_full], dim=1)  # (B, H, N, D)

        # Apply masking to padded positions (redundant but safe)
        if token_valid is not None:

══════════════════ __init__.py ══════════════════

══════════════════ nova.py ══════════════════
"""NOVA attention module – 8 state‑of‑the‑art compression techniques."""

import math
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Optional, Tuple, List, Dict
from dataclasses import dataclass


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
@dataclass
class NOVAConfig:
    hidden_size: int = 768
    num_heads: int = 12
    head_dim: int = 64
    dropout: float = 0.1

    # ARMOR (Adaptive Rank‑Modulated Orthogonal Reduction) – SVD factorisation
    armor_enabled: bool = True
    armor_energy: float = 0.90   # energy retained in SVD truncation
    armor_quant_bits: int = 4    # quantisation bits for residual
    armor_use_2of4: bool = True  # structured sparsity 2:4

    # AST (Adaptive Sparse Token) – learnable token pruning
    ast_enabled: bool = True
    ast_keep_ratio: float = 0.7  # fraction of tokens to keep

    # DeltaKV (residual KV reference)
    deltakv_enabled: bool = True
    deltakv_max_refs: int = 128
    deltakv_topk: int = 4

    # KVT (KV quantisation)
    kvt_enabled: bool = True
    kvt_bits: int = 4
    kvt_pca_ratio: float = 0.95

    # Softpick (rectified softmax)
    softpick_enabled: bool = True
    softpick_temp: float = 1.0

    # Subspace (low‑dimensional attention)
    subspace_enabled: bool = True
    subspace_energy: float = 0.95   # for SVD‑based subspace projection

    max_seq_len: int = 128


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Utility components
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _select_rank_svd(W: torch.Tensor, energy: float, min_r: int = 4) -> int:
    with torch.no_grad():
        _, S, _ = torch.linalg.svd(W.float(), full_matrices=False)
        total = (S ** 2).sum()
        cum = torch.cumsum(S ** 2, dim=0) / total
        r = int(torch.searchsorted(cum, energy).item()) + 1
        return max(min_r, min(r, min(W.shape[0], W.shape[1])))


def softpick(x: torch.Tensor, dim: int = -1, temperature: float = 1.0) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Rectified softmax – ~47% sparse, no attention sink."""
    m = x.max(dim=dim, keepdim=True).values
    e = torch.exp((x - m) / temperature)
    denom = e - torch.exp(-m / temperature)
    p = F.relu(denom)
    return p / (p.sum(dim=dim, keepdim=True) + 1e-9)


class DeltaKVStore:
    """Residual KV reference store (DeltaKV)."""
    def __init__(self, max_refs: int = 128, topk: int = 4):
        self.max_refs = max_refs
        self.topk = topk
        self.ref_k: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
        self.ref_v: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None

    @torch.no_grad()
    def update(self, k: torch.Tensor, v: torch.Tensor):
        B, H, N, D = k.shape
        if B == 0:
            return
        ks = k[0]; vs = v[0]
        if self.ref_k is None:
            self.ref_k, self.ref_v = ks, vs
        else:
            self.ref_k = torch.cat([self.ref_k, ks], dim=1)
            self.ref_v = torch.cat([self.ref_v, vs], dim=1)
        if self.ref_k.size(1) > self.max_refs:
            self.ref_k = self.ref_k[:, -self.max_refs:, :]
            self.ref_v = self.ref_v[:, -self.max_refs:, :]

    @torch.no_grad()
    def compress(self, k: torch.Tensor, v: torch.Tensor) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        if self.ref_k is None:
            return k, v
        B, H, N, D = k.shape
        tk = min(self.topk, self.ref_k.size(1))
        sim = torch.matmul(k, self.ref_k.transpose(-2, -1))
        _, idx = sim.topk(tk, dim=-1)
        idx_e = idx.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, -1, -1, D)
        rk = self.ref_k.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(2).expand(B, H, N, -1, -1).gather(3, idx_e).mean(dim=3)
        rv = self.ref_v.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(2).expand(B, H, N, -1, -1).gather(3, idx_e).mean(dim=3)
        return k - rk, v - rv


class KVTQuantiser:
    """PCA‑decorrelated adaptive quantisation (KVTC)."""
    def __init__(self, n_bits: int = 4, pca_ratio: float = 0.95):
        self.n_bits = n_bits
        self.pca_ratio = pca_ratio
        self.basis: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
        self.scale: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
        self.zp: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
        self.fitted = False

    @torch.no_grad()
    def fit(self, x: torch.Tensor):
        X = x.float()
        m = X.mean(dim=0, keepdim=True)
        Xc = X - m
        U, S, _ = torch.linalg.svd(Xc, full_matrices=False)
        total = (S ** 2).sum()
        cum = torch.cumsum(S ** 2, dim=0) / total
        r = min(int(torch.searchsorted(cum, self.pca_ratio).item()) + 1, X.size(1))
        self.basis = U[:, :r].contiguous()
        proj = Xc @ self.basis
        self.scale = (proj.max(dim=0).values - proj.min(dim=0).values) / (2 ** self.n_bits - 1)
        self.scale = self.scale.clamp(min=1e-8)
        self.zp = proj.min(dim=0).values
        self.fitted = True

    def compress(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        if not self.fitted or self.basis is None:
            return x
        *B, D = x.shape
        xc = x.float() - x.float().mean(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
        proj = xc @ self.basis.to(x.device)
        q = ((proj - self.zp.to(x.device)) / self.scale.to(x.device)).round()
        dq = q * self.scale.to(x.device) + self.zp.to(x.device)
        return (dq @ self.basis.T.to(x.device)).to(x.dtype)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# ARMOR projection (SVD‑factorised + quantised sparse residual)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
class ARMORProjection(nn.Module):
    """ARMOR: Adaptive Rank‑Modulated Orthogonal Reduction (SVD + 2:4 sparse + quant)."""
    def __init__(self, W_pretrained: torch.Tensor, bias: Optional[torch.Tensor],
                 energy: float, bits: int, use_2of4: bool):
        super().__init__()
        out_dim, in_dim = W_pretrained.shape
        U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W_pretrained.float(), full_matrices=False)
        self.lr_rank = _select_rank_svd(W_pretrained, energy)
        self.A = nn.Parameter((U[:, :self.lr_rank] * S[:self.lr_rank].unsqueeze(0)).float())
        self.B = nn.Parameter(Vt[:self.lr_rank, :].float())

        lr_approx = self.A @ self.B
        residual = W_pretrained.float() - lr_approx
        if use_2of4:
            residual_abs = residual.abs()
            for i in range(out_dim):
                for j in range(0, in_dim, 4):
                    blk = residual_abs[i, j:j+4]
                    _, idx = blk.topk(2)
                    mask_2d = torch.zeros(4, device=residual.device)
                    mask_2d[idx] = 1.0
                    residual[i, j:j+4] *= mask_2d
        r_min, r_max = residual.min(), residual.max()
        if r_max == r_min:
            r_max = r_min + 1e-6
        scale_q = (r_max - r_min) / (2 ** bits - 1)
        self.register_buffer('sparse_q', torch.round((residual - r_min) / scale_q).to(torch.int8))
        self.register_buffer('r_min', r_min)
        self.register_buffer('r_scale', scale_q)

        self.bias = nn.Parameter(bias.float()) if bias is not None else None

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        out_lr = F.linear(F.linear(x, self.B), self.A.t())
        out_sp = F.linear(x, self.sparse_q.float() * self.r_scale + self.r_min)
        out = out_lr + out_sp
        if self.bias is not None:
            out = out + self.bias
        return out


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Subspace attention helper (for low‑dimensional projection)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
class SubspaceProjection(nn.Module):
    """Learnable SVD‑based subspace projection for Q/K/V."""
    def __init__(self, dim: int, energy: float):
        super().__init__()
        # Initialised randomly; during first forward it will adapt
        self.proj = nn.Linear(dim, dim, bias=False)
        self.energy = energy

══════════════════ minimal_nova.py ══════════════════
"""
MinimalNOVA – stable, zero‑shot, production‑ready drop‑in replacement for DistilBERT.
SVD‑factorised Q/K/V/Out projections only.
"""

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Optional, Tuple

class FactorisedLinear(nn.Module):
    """SVD‑factorised linear layer."""
    def __init__(self, pretrained_weight, pretrained_bias, energy=0.95):
        super().__init__()
        out_dim, in_dim = pretrained_weight.shape
        with torch.no_grad():
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(pretrained_weight.float(), full_matrices=False)
            total = (S ** 2).sum()
            cum = torch.cumsum(S ** 2, dim=0) / total
            rank = int(torch.searchsorted(cum, energy).item()) + 1
            rank = max(4, min(rank, min(out_dim, in_dim), 64))
            self.rank = rank
            A = U[:, :rank] * S[:rank].unsqueeze(0)
            B = Vt[:rank, :]
        self.down = nn.Linear(in_dim, rank, bias=False)
        self.up   = nn.Linear(rank, out_dim, bias=(pretrained_bias is not None))
        self.down.weight.data = B.float()
        self.up.weight.data   = A.float()
        if pretrained_bias is not None:
            self.up.bias.data = pretrained_bias.float()
        else:
            self.up.bias.data.zero_()
    def forward(self, x): return self.up(self.down(x))

class MinimalNOVAAttention(nn.Module):
    """SVD‑factorised attention, full standard attention, zero‑shot."""
    def __init__(self, model_config, energy=0.95, dropout=0.1, pretrained_attn=None):
        super().__init__()
        if pretrained_attn is None:
            raise ValueError("pretrained_attn required for zero‑shot init")
        self.num_heads = model_config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = model_config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.hidden_size = model_config.hidden_size

        self.q_proj = FactorisedLinear(pretrained_attn.q_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.q_lin.bias.data, energy)
        self.k_proj = FactorisedLinear(pretrained_attn.k_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.k_lin.bias.data, energy)
        self.v_proj = FactorisedLinear(pretrained_attn.v_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.v_lin.bias.data, energy)
        self.out_proj = FactorisedLinear(pretrained_attn.out_lin.weight.data, pretrained_attn.out_lin.bias.data, energy)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, head_mask=None, output_attentions=False, **kwargs):
        B, N, _ = hidden_states.shape
        H, D = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        Q = self.q_proj(hidden_states)
        K = self.k_proj(hidden_states)
        V = self.v_proj(hidden_states)
        Q = Q.view(B, N, H, D).transpose(1, 2)
        K = K.view(B, N, H, D).transpose(1, 2)
        V = V.view(B, N, H, D).transpose(1, 2)

        scale = D ** -0.5
        scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale

        mask = attention_mask if attention_mask is not None else kwargs.get('attn_mask', None)
        if mask is not None:
            if mask.dim() == 4 and mask.shape[-2] == mask.shape[-1]:
                token_valid = mask[:, 0, 0, :] > -1e9
            elif mask.dim() == 4:
                token_valid = mask.squeeze(1).squeeze(1) > -1e9
            elif mask.dim() == 3:
                token_valid = mask.squeeze(1).bool()
            elif mask.dim() == 2:
                token_valid = mask.bool()
            else:
                token_valid = None
            if token_valid is not None:
                scores = scores.masked_fill(~token_valid.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e5)

        attn_weights = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
        attn_weights = self.dropout(attn_weights)
        context = torch.matmul(attn_weights, V)
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N, H * D)
        output = self.out_proj(context)
        if output_attentions:
            return (output, attn_weights)
        else:
            return (output, None)

    def get_ortho_loss(self):
        return torch.tensor(0.0, device=self.out_proj.up.weight.device)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Framework injection & registration
# ------------------------------------------------------------
from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("minimal_nova")
def inject_minimal_nova(layer, model_config, comp_config, layer_idx, total_layers, old_attn):
    new_attn = MinimalNOVAAttention(
        model_config=model_config,
        energy=getattr(comp_config, 'svd_energy', 0.95),
        dropout=getattr(comp_config, 'dropout', 0.1),
        pretrained_attn=old_attn
    )
    layer.attention = new_attn
    return new_attn

# framework/attentions/__init__.py
import importlib
import pkgutil
import sys
from pathlib import Path

# Re‑export the registry and base for convenience
from .core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY
from .core.base import BaseAttention

__all__ = ["ATTENTION_REGISTRY", "BaseAttention"]

def _safe_import_all():
    base_pkg = "framework.attentions"
    base_path = Path(__file__).parent
    for py_file in base_path.rglob("*.py"):
        if py_file.name == "__init__.py":
            continue
        rel = py_file.relative_to(base_path).with_suffix("")
        mod_name = ".".join(rel.parts)
        full_name = f"{base_pkg}.{mod_name}"
        try:
            importlib.import_module(full_name)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"⚠️ Could not import {full_name}: {e}", file=sys.stderr)

_safe_import_all()

══════════════════ aurora.py ══════════════════
"""
AURORA — Adaptive Unified Robust Orthogonal Rank‑Aware Attention
Ten‑in‑one compression for DistilBERT.
"""

import math
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Optional, Tuple, List, Dict
from dataclasses import dataclass
from framework.attentions.core.base import BaseAttention
from framework.attentions.core.utils import softpick, select_rank_svd


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
@dataclass
class AURORAConfig:
    hidden_size: int = 768
    num_heads: int = 12
    head_dim: int = 64
    dropout: float = 0.1

    # OATS
    oats_enabled: bool = True
    oats_critical_ratio: float = 0.25
    oats_marginal_ratio: float = 0.50

    # SLiM
    slim_enabled: bool = True
    slim_energy: float = 0.90
    slim_quant_bits: int = 4
    slim_use_2of4: bool = True

    # RPCA
    rpca_enabled: bool = True
    rpca_rank: int = 16

    # SliceGPT
    slicegpt_enabled: bool = True
    slicegpt_reduce_ratio: float = 0.5

    # ReplaceMe
    replaceme_enabled: bool = True
    replaceme_skip_threshold: float = 0.1

    # NMF
    nmf_enabled: bool = True
    nmf_rank: int = 16

    # ProxSparse
    proxsparse_enabled: bool = True
    proxsparse_sparsity: float = 0.5

    # Dual pathway (Scatterbrain/SLA)
    dual_pathway_enabled: bool = True
    kernel_rff_features: int = 128

    # DeltaKV + KVTC
    deltakv_enabled: bool = True
    deltakv_max_refs: int = 128
    deltakv_topk: int = 4
    kvt_bits: int = 4
    kvt_pca_ratio: float = 0.95

    # Softpick
    softpick_enabled: bool = True
    softpick_temp: float = 1.0

    max_seq_len: int = 128


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Utility components
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

class DeltaKVStore:
    """Residual KV reference store (DeltaKV, arXiv 2602.08005)."""
    def __init__(self, max_refs: int = 128, topk: int = 4):
        self.max_refs = max_refs
        self.topk = topk
        self.ref_k: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
        self.ref_v: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None

    @torch.no_grad()
    def update(self, k: torch.Tensor, v: torch.Tensor):
        B, H, N, D = k.shape
        if B == 0:
            return
        ks = k[0]; vs = v[0]
        if self.ref_k is None:
            self.ref_k, self.ref_v = ks, vs
        else:
            self.ref_k = torch.cat([self.ref_k, ks], dim=1)
            self.ref_v = torch.cat([self.ref_v, vs], dim=1)
        if self.ref_k.size(1) > self.max_refs:
            self.ref_k = self.ref_k[:, -self.max_refs:, :]
            self.ref_v = self.ref_v[:, -self.max_refs:, :]

    @torch.no_grad()
    def compress(self, k: torch.Tensor, v: torch.Tensor) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        if self.ref_k is None:
            return k, v
        B, H, N, D = k.shape
        tk = min(self.topk, self.ref_k.size(1))
        sim = torch.matmul(k, self.ref_k.transpose(-2, -1))
        _, idx = sim.topk(tk, dim=-1)
        idx_e = idx.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, -1, -1, D)
        rk = self.ref_k.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(2).expand(B, H, N, -1, -1).gather(3, idx_e).mean(dim=3)
        rv = self.ref_v.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(2).expand(B, H, N, -1, -1).gather(3, idx_e).mean(dim=3)
        return k - rk, v - rv


class KVTQuantiser:
    """PCA‑decorrelated adaptive quantisation (KVTC, ICLR 2026)."""
    def __init__(self, n_bits: int = 4, pca_ratio: float = 0.95):
        self.n_bits = n_bits
        self.pca_ratio = pca_ratio
        self.basis: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
        self.scale: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
        self.zp: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
        self.fitted = False

    @torch.no_grad()
    def fit(self, x: torch.Tensor):
        X = x.float()
        m = X.mean(dim=0, keepdim=True)
        Xc = X - m
        U, S, _ = torch.linalg.svd(Xc, full_matrices=False)
        total = (S ** 2).sum()
        cum = torch.cumsum(S ** 2, dim=0) / total
        r = min(int(torch.searchsorted(cum, self.pca_ratio).item()) + 1, X.size(1))
        self.basis = U[:, :r].contiguous()
        proj = Xc @ self.basis
        self.scale = (proj.max(dim=0).values - proj.min(dim=0).values) / (2 ** self.n_bits - 1)
        self.scale = self.scale.clamp(min=1e-8)
        self.zp = proj.min(dim=0).values
        self.fitted = True

    def compress(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        if not self.fitted or self.basis is None:
            return x
        *B, D = x.shape
        xc = x.float() - x.float().mean(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
        proj = xc @ self.basis.to(x.device)
        q = ((proj - self.zp.to(x.device)) / self.scale.to(x.device)).round()
        dq = q * self.scale.to(x.device) + self.zp.to(x.device)
        return (dq @ self.basis.T.to(x.device)).to(x.dtype)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# SLiM‑style QSL projection
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
class QSLProjection(nn.Module):
    """Quantized‑Sparse‑Low‑rank decomposition (SLiM, ICML 2025)."""
    def __init__(self, W_pretrained: torch.Tensor, bias: Optional[torch.Tensor],
                 energy: float, bits: int, use_2of4: bool):
        super().__init__()
        out_dim, in_dim = W_pretrained.shape
        self.out_dim, self.in_dim = out_dim, in_dim

        self.lr_rank = select_rank_svd(W_pretrained, energy)
        U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W_pretrained.float(), full_matrices=False)
        self.A = nn.Parameter((U[:, :self.lr_rank] * S[:self.lr_rank].unsqueeze(0)).float())
        self.B = nn.Parameter(Vt[:self.lr_rank, :].float())

        lr_approx = self.A @ self.B
        residual = W_pretrained.float() - lr_approx
        if use_2of4:
            residual_abs = residual.abs()
            for i in range(out_dim):
                for j in range(0, in_dim, 4):
                    blk = residual_abs[i, j:j+4]
                    _, idx = blk.topk(2)
                    mask_2d = torch.zeros(4, device=residual.device)
                    mask_2d[idx] = 1.0
                    residual[i, j:j+4] *= mask_2d
        r_min, r_max = residual.min(), residual.max()
        if r_max == r_min:
            r_max = r_min + 1e-6
        scale_q = (r_max - r_min) / (2 ** bits - 1)
        self.register_buffer('sparse_q', torch.round((residual - r_min) / scale_q).to(torch.int8))
        self.register_buffer('r_min', r_min)
        self.register_buffer('r_scale', scale_q)

        if bias is not None:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(bias.float())
        else:
            self.bias = None

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        out_lr = F.linear(F.linear(x, self.B), self.A.t())
        out_sp = F.linear(x, self.sparse_q.float() * self.r_scale + self.r_min)
        out = out_lr + out_sp
        if self.bias is not None:
            out = out + self.bias
        return out


══════════════════ minimal_aurora.py ══════════════════
"""
MinimalAURORA – stable, zero‑shot, drop‑in replacement for DistilBERT.
SVD‑factorised Q/K/V/Out projections only.
"""

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("minimal_aurora")
class FactorisedLinear(nn.Module):
    """SVD‑factorised linear layer."""
    def __init__(self, pretrained_weight, pretrained_bias, energy=0.95):
        super().__init__()
        out_dim, in_dim = pretrained_weight.shape
        with torch.no_grad():
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(pretrained_weight.float(), full_matrices=False)
            total = (S ** 2).sum()
            cum = torch.cumsum(S ** 2, dim=0) / total
            rank = int(torch.searchsorted(cum, energy).item()) + 1
            rank = max(4, min(rank, min(out_dim, in_dim), 64))
            self.rank = rank
            A = U[:, :rank] * S[:rank].unsqueeze(0)
            B = Vt[:rank, :]
        self.down = nn.Linear(in_dim, rank, bias=False)
        self.up   = nn.Linear(rank, out_dim, bias=(pretrained_bias is not None))
        self.down.weight.data = B.float()
        self.up.weight.data   = A.float()
        if pretrained_bias is not None:
            self.up.bias.data = pretrained_bias.float()
        else:
            self.up.bias.data.zero_()

    def forward(self, x):
        return self.up(self.down(x))


class MinimalAURORAAttention(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, model_config, energy=0.95, dropout=0.1, pretrained_attn=None):
        super().__init__()
        if pretrained_attn is None:
            raise ValueError("pretrained_attn required for zero‑shot init")
        self.num_heads = model_config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = model_config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.hidden_size = model_config.hidden_size

        self.q_proj = FactorisedLinear(pretrained_attn.q_lin.weight.data,
                                       pretrained_attn.q_lin.bias.data, energy)
        self.k_proj = FactorisedLinear(pretrained_attn.k_lin.weight.data,
                                       pretrained_attn.k_lin.bias.data, energy)
        self.v_proj = FactorisedLinear(pretrained_attn.v_lin.weight.data,
                                       pretrained_attn.v_lin.bias.data, energy)
        self.out_proj = FactorisedLinear(pretrained_attn.out_lin.weight.data,
                                         pretrained_attn.out_lin.bias.data, energy)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, head_mask=None,
                output_attentions=False, **kwargs):
        B, N, _ = hidden_states.shape
        H, D = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        Q = self.q_proj(hidden_states)
        K = self.k_proj(hidden_states)
        V = self.v_proj(hidden_states)
        Q = Q.view(B, N, H, D).transpose(1, 2)
        K = K.view(B, N, H, D).transpose(1, 2)
        V = V.view(B, N, H, D).transpose(1, 2)

        scale = D ** -0.5
        scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale

        mask = attention_mask if attention_mask is not None else kwargs.get('attn_mask', None)
        if mask is not None:
            if mask.dim() == 4 and mask.shape[-2] == mask.shape[-1]:
                token_valid = mask[:, 0, 0, :] > -1e9
            elif mask.dim() == 4:
                token_valid = mask.squeeze(1).squeeze(1) > -1e9
            elif mask.dim() == 3:
                token_valid = mask.squeeze(1).bool()
            elif mask.dim() == 2:
                token_valid = mask.bool()
            else:
                token_valid = None
            if token_valid is not None:
                scores = scores.masked_fill(~token_valid.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e5)

        attn_weights = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
        attn_weights = self.dropout(attn_weights)
        context = torch.matmul(attn_weights, V)
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N, H * D)
        output = self.out_proj(context)
        return (output, attn_weights) if output_attentions else (output,)

    def get_ortho_loss(self):
        return torch.tensor(0.0, device=self.out_proj.up.weight.device)


@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("minimal_aurora")
def inject_minimal_aurora(layer, model_config, comp_config, layer_idx, total_layers, old_attn):
    new_attn = MinimalAURORAAttention(
        model_config=model_config,
        energy=getattr(comp_config, 'svd_energy', 0.95),
        dropout=getattr(comp_config, 'dropout', 0.1),
        pretrained_attn=old_attn
    )
    layer.attention = new_attn
    return new_attn

══════════════════ regla_attention.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from ..core.base import BaseAttention
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("regla")
class ReGLAAttention(BaseAttention):
    """
    Refined Gated Linear Attention (ReGLA) from NAACL 2025.
    Gated linear attention with proper normalization.
    """
    def __init__(self, config, rank=64, kernel_size=3):
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.dim = config.hidden_size
        self.heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = self.dim // self.heads

        self.q_proj = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim)
        self.k_proj = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim)
        self.v_proj = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim)
        self.gate_proj = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim)
        self.conv = nn.Conv1d(self.dim, self.dim, kernel_size, padding=kernel_size//2, groups=self.dim)

    def forward(self, x, mask=None):
        B, L, D = x.shape
        q = self.q_proj(x).view(B, L, self.heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1,2)  # (B, H, L, d)
        k = self.k_proj(x).view(B, L, self.heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1,2)
        v = self.v_proj(x).view(B, L, self.heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1,2)
        g = self.gate_proj(x).view(B, L, self.heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1,2)

        # element‑wise gate on keys
        k_gated = k * torch.sigmoid(g)

        # linear attention with proper normalisation
        kv = torch.matmul(k_gated.transpose(-2, -1), v)  # (B, H, d, d)
        z = k_gated.sum(dim=2, keepdim=True)             # (B, H, 1, d)

        num = torch.matmul(q, kv)                        # (B, H, L, d)
        den = torch.matmul(q, z.transpose(-2, -1)) + 1e-8  # (B, H, L, 1)
        out = num / den

        out = out.transpose(1,2).contiguous().view(B, L, D)

        # Local depthwise conv enhancement
        loc = self.conv(x.permute(0,2,1)).permute(0,2,1)
        return self.out_proj(out + loc), None
══════════════════ zeros_attention.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from ..core.base import BaseAttention
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("zeros")
class ZeroSAttention(BaseAttention):
    """
    Zero‑mean linear attention: removes mean of keys and uses linear attention with proper normalisation.
    """
    def __init__(self, config, rank=64):
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.head_dim = config.hidden_size // config.num_attention_heads
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.W_q = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.W_k = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.W_v = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.out = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)

    def forward(self, x, mask=None):
        B, L, D = x.shape
        q = self.W_q(x).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0,2,1,3)
        k = self.W_k(x).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0,2,1,3)
        v = self.W_v(x).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0,2,1,3)

        # Zero center keys along sequence dimension
        k_centered = k - k.mean(dim=2, keepdim=True)

        # Linear attention: O = Q (K^T V) / (Q sum(K))
        q_nonneg = torch.relu(q) + 1e-8
        k_nonneg = torch.relu(k_centered) + 1e-8

        kv = torch.einsum('bhnk,bhnd->bhkd', k_nonneg, v)   # (B, H, k, d) where k=d
        k_sum = k_nonneg.sum(dim=2, keepdim=True)            # (B, H, 1, d)

        num = torch.matmul(q_nonneg, kv)                     # (B, H, L, d)
        den = torch.matmul(q_nonneg, k_sum.transpose(-2,-1)) + 1e-8
        out = num / den
        out = out.permute(0,2,1,3).contiguous().view(B, L, D)
        return self.out(out), None
══════════════════ nystrom_attention.py ══════════════════
"""
Nyströmformer Attention: linear‑time attention via Nyström approximation.
Paper: https://arxiv.org/abs/2102.03902
"""
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from ..core.base import BaseAttention
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY


def _compute_moore_penrose(A, iters=6):
    # A: (B, H, m, m)
    # Initialize X = (1/||A||) A^T (rough approximation)
    norm_A = torch.linalg.norm(A, dim=(-2, -1), keepdim=True)
    X = A.transpose(-2, -1) / (norm_A + 1e-8)
    I = torch.eye(A.size(-1), device=A.device).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)
    for _ in range(iters):
        X = torch.matmul(X, 2 * I - torch.matmul(A, X))
    return X


@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("nystrom")
class NystromAttention(BaseAttention):
    def __init__(self, config, rank=64, pinv_iterations=6, residual=True):
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.rank = rank
        self.pinv_iterations = pinv_iterations
        self.residual = residual

        self.query = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.key   = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.value = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(self.all_head_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(config.attention_probs_dropout_prob)

    def _reshape(self, x, batch_size):
        return x.view(batch_size, -1, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, **kwargs):
        batch_size, seq_len, _ = hidden_states.shape
        Q = self._reshape(self.query(hidden_states), batch_size)
        K = self._reshape(self.key(hidden_states),   batch_size)
        V = self._reshape(self.value(hidden_states), batch_size)

        num_landmarks = self.rank
        # Landmark selection (segmented mean)
        if seq_len % num_landmarks == 0:
            seg = seq_len // num_landmarks
            Q_land = Q.reshape(batch_size, self.num_heads, num_landmarks, seg, self.head_dim).mean(dim=3)
            K_land = K.reshape(batch_size, self.num_heads, num_landmarks, seg, self.head_dim).mean(dim=3)
        else:
            idx = torch.linspace(0, seq_len-1, num_landmarks).long().to(hidden_states.device)
            Q_land = Q[:, :, idx, :]
            K_land = K[:, :, idx, :]

        scale = 1.0 / (self.head_dim ** 0.5)
        F1 = torch.matmul(Q, K_land.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
        F2 = torch.matmul(Q_land, K_land.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
        F3 = torch.matmul(Q_land, K.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale

        F2_pinv = _compute_moore_penrose(F2, self.pinv_iterations)
        attn = torch.matmul(F1, torch.matmul(F2_pinv, F3))
        if attention_mask is not None:
            attn = attn + attention_mask.unsqueeze(1)

        probs = F.softmax(attn, dim=-1)
        probs = self.dropout(probs)

        context = torch.matmul(probs, V)
        if self.residual:
            context = context + V
        context = context.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(batch_size, seq_len, self.all_head_size)
        return (self.out_proj(context), None) if kwargs.get('output_attentions') else (self.out_proj(context),)
