#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
SVD‑Compressed Attention – Production Version with
Importance‑Aware Rank Allocation (D‑Rank / ARSVD style)
and optional Low‑Rank + Sparse Decomposition.

Usage:
    python SVDCompressedAttention.py --task sst2 --epochs 5 --batch_size 16 --lr 1e-5
"""

import os, argparse, logging, warnings
from typing import Optional, Tuple, Dict, List

import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from framework.attentions.core.base import BaseAttention
from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY
from scipy.linalg import svd

from transformers import (
    AutoConfig, AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForSequenceClassification,
    Trainer, TrainingArguments, EarlyStoppingCallback, set_seed,
)
from datasets import Dataset, DatasetDict

# ~~~~~~~~ Offline environment ~~~~~~~~
os.environ["HF_HUB_OFFLINE"] = "1"
os.environ["TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE"] = "1"
os.environ["TOKENIZERS_PARALLELISM"] = "false"
warnings.filterwarnings("ignore", category=UserWarning)
logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s | %(levelname)-7s | %(message)s",
    datefmt="%H:%M:%S",
)
log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# =============================================================================
# 1. Helper functions for spectral analysis
# =============================================================================

def calculate_spectral_entropy(singular_values: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """Normalised spectral entropy (higher = more info spread)."""
    s = singular_values.astype(np.float64)
    s_sum = s.sum()
    if s_sum == 0:
        return 0.0
    prob = s / s_sum
    prob = prob[prob > 1e-12]                # ignore zeros
    return float(-np.sum(prob * np.log(prob)))

def compute_cumulative_entropy(singular_values: np.ndarray, max_rank: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Returns array `cum_ent[k]` = fraction of total entropy retained
    when using the first k singular values (k=1..max_rank).
    """
    s = singular_values.astype(np.float64)
    s_sq = s ** 2
    total = s_sq.sum()
    if total == 0:
        return np.ones(max_rank)
    cum = np.cumsum(s_sq[:max_rank]) / total
    return cum

# =============================================================================
# 2. Lagrangian optimisation for rank allocation
# =============================================================================

def lagrangian_rank_allocation(
    weight_matrices: List[np.ndarray],           # list of all original weight matrices (Q,K,V,O × layers)
    target_budget: int,
    layer_proj_names: List[str],                 # e.g. ['layer0_q','layer0_k',...]
    max_rank_factor: float = 0.5,               # max rank as fraction of min(d_in,d_out)
    lambda_bracket: Tuple[float,float] = (1e-8, 10.0),
    num_lambda_steps: int = 100,
) -> Dict[str, int]:
    """
    Solves: min Σ cost_i(r_i)  s.t. Σ params_i(r_i) ≤ budget
    using a Lagrangian multiplier λ.
    
    cost_i(r) = 1 - cum_entropy_fraction(r)   (lower = better)
    params_i(r) = r * (d_in + d_out)          (approximate parameter count)
    """
    # Pre‑compute for each matrix the cost and param count for every possible rank
    max_ranks = []
    cost_table = []    # list of arrays shape (max_rank,)
    param_table = []   # list of arrays shape (max_rank,)

    for W, name in zip(weight_matrices, layer_proj_names):
        d_in, d_out = W.shape[1], W.shape[0]
        max_r = min(d_in, d_out, int(max_rank_factor * min(d_in, d_out)))
        max_r = max(1, max_r)
        max_ranks.append(max_r)

        # singular values
        _, s, _ = svd(W.astype(np.float64), full_matrices=False)
        cum_ent = compute_cumulative_entropy(s, max_r)   # length max_r
        cost = 1.0 - cum_ent                             # information loss
        cost_table.append(cost)

        # params per rank: both down (d_in→r) and up (r→d_out)
        params_per_r = np.arange(1, max_r + 1) * (d_in + d_out)
        param_table.append(params_per_r)

    # Binary search on λ
    low, high = lambda_bracket
    best_allocation = None
    best_used_params = None

    for _ in range(num_lambda_steps):
        lam = (low + high) / 2.0

        # For each matrix, pick rank that minimises cost + λ * params
        ranks = []
        total_params = 0
        for i in range(len(weight_matrices)):
            obj = cost_table[i] + lam * param_table[i]
            best_r_idx = int(np.argmin(obj))
            r = best_r_idx + 1   # rank = index+1
            ranks.append(r)
            total_params += param_table[i][best_r_idx]

        if total_params <= target_budget:
            # feasible – try smaller λ (less penalty on params → maybe more params)
            best_allocation = ranks.copy()
            best_used_params = total_params
            high = lam
        else:
            low = lam

    if best_allocation is None:
        # fallback: smallest rank everywhere
        best_allocation = [1] * len(weight_matrices)
        log.warning("Could not meet budget, falling back to rank=1 everywhere.")

    # Map back to dict
    rank_dict = {name: r for name, r in zip(layer_proj_names, best_allocation)}
    return rank_dict

# =============================================================================
# 3. SVD‑Compressed Attention module (updated)
# =============================================================================

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("svd_compressed")
class SVDCompressedAttention(BaseAttention):
    """
    Replaces a DistilBERT multi‑head attention layer with SVD‑factorised
    Q/K/V/O projections. Ranks are provided externally (pre‑computed).
    """
    def __init__(
        self,
        model_config,
        dropout: float = 0.1,
        layer_idx: int = 0,
        pretrained_attn=None,
        ranks: Optional[Dict[str, int]] = None,   # {'q':rq, 'k':rk, 'v':rv, 'o':ro}
        add_sparse: bool = False,                # future: low‑rank + sparse
        sparsity_level: float = 0.7,
    ):
        super().__init__(model_config)
        self.num_heads = model_config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = model_config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.hidden_size = model_config.hidden_size

        if pretrained_attn is None:
            raise ValueError("pretrained_attn is required for weight init")
        if ranks is None:
            raise ValueError("ranks dictionary is required (containing 'q','k','v','o')")

        # ---- Factorise Q, K, V, O with given ranks ----
        q_w = pretrained_attn.q_lin.weight.data.cpu().numpy()
        k_w = pretrained_attn.k_lin.weight.data.cpu().numpy()
        v_w = pretrained_attn.v_lin.weight.data.cpu().numpy()
        out_w = pretrained_attn.out_lin.weight.data.cpu().numpy()

        self.q_down, self.q_up = self._factorise_linear(q_w, pretrained_attn.q_lin.bias, ranks['q'])
        self.k_down, self.k_up = self._factorise_linear(k_w, pretrained_attn.k_lin.bias, ranks['k'])
        self.v_down, self.v_up = self._factorise_linear(v_w, pretrained_attn.v_lin.bias, ranks['v'])
        self.o_down, self.o_up = self._factorise_linear(out_w, pretrained_attn.out_lin.bias, ranks['o'])

        # Sparse additive component (optional)
        self.add_sparse = add_sparse
        if add_sparse:
            self.q_sparse_weight = self._create_pruned_residual(q_w, self.q_down, self.q_up, sparsity_level)
            self.k_sparse_weight = self._create_pruned_residual(k_w, self.k_down, self.k_up, sparsity_level)
            self.v_sparse_weight = self._create_pruned_residual(v_w, self.v_down, self.v_up, sparsity_level)
            self.o_sparse_weight = self._create_pruned_residual(out_w, self.o_down, self.o_up, sparsity_level)

        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)

    def _factorise_linear(self, W: np.ndarray, bias: torch.Tensor, rank: int):
        """SVD factorisation W ≈ U_r Σ_r V_r^T → down (in→r), up (r→out)"""
        d_out, d_in = W.shape
        rank = min(rank, min(d_out, d_in))
        rank = max(rank, 1)

        U, s, VT = svd(W.astype(np.float64), full_matrices=False)

from . import minimal_svd
══════════════════ minimal_svd.py ══════════════════
# framework/attentions/minimal_svd.py
"""
Minimal SVD‑attention – zero‑shot drop‑in replacement for DistilBERT attention.
Factorises Q/K/V/Out projections with SVD (energy threshold) but keeps full attention.
"""

import math
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Optional, Tuple

from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY


class FactorisedLinear(nn.Module):
    """SVD‑factorised linear layer: W ≈ U·diag(S)·Vt → (A)(B) with A = U·diag(S), B = Vt."""
    def __init__(self, pretrained_weight: torch.Tensor, pretrained_bias: Optional[torch.Tensor],
                 energy: float = 0.95):
        """
        Args:
            pretrained_weight: Original weight matrix (out_dim, in_dim)
            pretrained_bias: Original bias (out_dim,), may be None
            energy: Fraction of singular value energy to retain (0..1)
        """
        super().__init__()
        out_dim, in_dim = pretrained_weight.shape

        with torch.no_grad():
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(pretrained_weight.float(), full_matrices=False)
            total_energy = (S ** 2).sum()
            cum_energy = torch.cumsum(S ** 2, dim=0) / total_energy
            rank = int(torch.searchsorted(cum_energy, energy).item()) + 1
            rank = max(1, min(rank, min(out_dim, in_dim), 64))
            self.rank = rank

            # A = U[:,:rank] * S[:rank], shape (out_dim, rank)
            A = U[:, :rank] * S[:rank].unsqueeze(0)
            # B = Vt[:rank, :], shape (rank, in_dim)
            B = Vt[:rank, :]

        self.down = nn.Linear(in_dim, rank, bias=False)
        self.up   = nn.Linear(rank, out_dim, bias=(pretrained_bias is not None))

        self.down.weight.data = B.float()
        self.up.weight.data   = A.float()

        if pretrained_bias is not None:
            self.up.bias.data = pretrained_bias.float()
        # If bias exists (bias=True), we already set it; no need to zero otherwise.

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.up(self.down(x))


class MinimalSVDAttention(nn.Module):
    """
    DistilBERT attention replacement using SVD‑factorised projections.
    Attention remains full (quadratic) but linear projections are compressed.
    Zero‑shot initialisation from a pre‑trained attention module.
    """
    def __init__(self, model_config, energy: float = 0.95, dropout: float = 0.1,
                 pretrained_attn: Optional[nn.Module] = None):
        super().__init__()
        if pretrained_attn is None:
            raise ValueError("pretrained_attn required for zero‑shot initialisation")

        self.num_heads = model_config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = model_config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.hidden_size = model_config.hidden_size

        # Factorise each projection
        self.q_proj = FactorisedLinear(
            pretrained_attn.q_lin.weight, pretrained_attn.q_lin.bias, energy
        )
        self.k_proj = FactorisedLinear(
            pretrained_attn.k_lin.weight, pretrained_attn.k_lin.bias, energy
        )
        self.v_proj = FactorisedLinear(
            pretrained_attn.v_lin.weight, pretrained_attn.v_lin.bias, energy
        )
        self.out_proj = FactorisedLinear(
            pretrained_attn.out_lin.weight, pretrained_attn.out_lin.bias, energy
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
        head_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
        output_attentions: bool = False,
        **kwargs
    ) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, Optional[torch.Tensor]]:
        batch_size, seq_len, _ = hidden_states.shape
        num_heads = self.num_heads
        head_dim = self.head_dim

        # Linear projections
        Q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(batch_size, seq_len, num_heads, head_dim).transpose(1, 2)
        K = self.k_proj(hidden_states).view(batch_size, seq_len, num_heads, head_dim).transpose(1, 2)
        V = self.v_proj(hidden_states).view(batch_size, seq_len, num_heads, head_dim).transpose(1, 2)

        # Scaled dot‑product attention
        scale = head_dim ** -0.5
        scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale

        # Mask handling (DistilBERT provides 2D attention_mask: (batch, seq_len) with 1 for real tokens)
        if attention_mask is not None:
            # Expand mask to (batch, 1, 1, seq_len) for broadcasting
            if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
                extended_mask = attention_mask[:, None, None, :]
            else:
                extended_mask = attention_mask
            # Convert 1/0 to 0/-inf for addition
            extended_mask = (1.0 - extended_mask) * -1e9
            scores = scores + extended_mask

        attn_weights = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
        attn_weights = self.dropout(attn_weights)
        context = torch.matmul(attn_weights, V)

        # Reshape back to (batch, seq_len, hidden_size)
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(batch_size, seq_len, -1)
        output = self.out_proj(context)

        if output_attentions:
            return output, attn_weights
        return output,

    def get_ortho_loss(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        return torch.tensor(0.0, device=self.out_proj.up.weight.device)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Framework injection
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("minimal_svd")
def inject_minimal_svd(layer, model_config, comp_config, layer_idx, total_layers, old_attn):
    """Factory function to replace a DistilBERT attention layer with MinimalSVDAttention."""
    energy = getattr(comp_config, 'svd_energy', 0.95)
    dropout = getattr(comp_config, 'dropout', 0.1)
    new_attn = MinimalSVDAttention(
        model_config=model_config,
        energy=energy,
        dropout=dropout,
        pretrained_attn=old_attn
    )
    layer.attention = new_attn
    return new_attn
══════════════════ compressed_attention.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
import numpy as np
from ..core.registry import AUX_REGISTRY

@AUX_REGISTRY.register("compressed_attention_layer")
class CompressedAttentionLayer(nn.Module):
    """
    SVD compression of nn.MultiheadAttention (generic).
    Compresses both the combined QKV projection and the output projection.
    """
    def __init__(self, original_layer: nn.MultiheadAttention, rank_qkv: int, rank_out: int, method: str = "svd"):
        super().__init__()
        self.original_dim = original_layer.embed_dim
        self.num_heads = original_layer.num_heads
        self.head_dim = self.original_dim // self.num_heads
        self.method = method

        w_qkv = original_layer.in_proj_weight.data.cpu().numpy()
        w_o = original_layer.out_proj.weight.data.cpu().numpy()

        U_qkv, S_qkv, Vh_qkv = np.linalg.svd(w_qkv, full_matrices=False)
        U_out, S_out, Vh_out = np.linalg.svd(w_o, full_matrices=False)

        rank_qkv = min(rank_qkv, w_qkv.shape[1])
        rank_out = min(rank_out, w_o.shape[1])

        self.rank_qkv = rank_qkv
        self.rank_out = rank_out

        self.U_qkv = nn.Parameter(torch.from_numpy(U_qkv[:, :rank_qkv]).float())
        self.S_qkv_sqrt = nn.Parameter(torch.from_numpy(np.sqrt(S_qkv[:rank_qkv])).float())
        self.Vh_qkv = nn.Parameter(torch.from_numpy(Vh_qkv[:rank_qkv, :]).float())

        self.U_out = nn.Parameter(torch.from_numpy(U_out[:, :rank_out]).float())
        self.S_out_sqrt = nn.Parameter(torch.from_numpy(np.sqrt(S_out[:rank_out])).float())
        self.Vh_out = nn.Parameter(torch.from_numpy(Vh_out[:rank_out, :]).float())

        if original_layer.in_proj_bias is not None:
            self.bias_qkv = nn.Parameter(original_layer.in_proj_bias.data.clone())
        else:
            self.bias_qkv = None
        if original_layer.out_proj.bias is not None:
            self.bias_out = nn.Parameter(original_layer.out_proj.bias.data.clone())
        else:
            self.bias_out = None

        self.dropout_p = original_layer.dropout
        self.batch_first = getattr(original_layer, 'batch_first', False)
        self.kdim = getattr(original_layer, 'kdim', None)
        self.vdim = getattr(original_layer, 'vdim', None)
        self.add_bias_kv = getattr(original_layer, 'add_bias_kv', False)
        self.add_zero_attn = getattr(original_layer, 'add_zero_attn', False)

    def forward(self, query, key, value, key_padding_mask=None, need_weights=True,
                attn_mask=None, average_attn_weights=True):
        x = query
        dim = self.original_dim

        projected_x = torch.matmul(x, self.Vh_qkv.t())
        qkv_compressed = torch.matmul(projected_x, self.U_qkv.t() * self.S_qkv_sqrt.view(1, -1, 1))
        if self.bias_qkv is not None:
            qkv_compressed += self.bias_qkv

        q = qkv_compressed[:, :, :dim]
        k = qkv_compressed[:, :, dim:2*dim]
        v = qkv_compressed[:, :, 2*dim:]

        scale = 1.0 / np.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        attn_scores = torch.matmul(q, k.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
        if attn_mask is not None:
            attn_scores += attn_mask
        if key_padding_mask is not None:
            attn_scores = attn_scores.masked_fill(key_padding_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e9)

        attn_weights = F.softmax(attn_scores, dim=-1)
        attn_weights = F.dropout(attn_weights, p=self.dropout_p, training=self.training)

        context = torch.matmul(attn_weights, v)

        context_fused = torch.matmul(context, self.Vh_out.t() * self.S_out_sqrt.view(1, -1, 1))
        final_output = torch.matmul(context_fused, self.U_out.t())
        if self.bias_out is not None:
            final_output += self.bias_out

        return final_output, attn_weights if need_weights else None


@AUX_REGISTRY.register("compressed_attention_layer_energy")
class CompressedAttentionLayer2(nn.Module):
    """
    Energy‑threshold based SVD compression of nn.MultiheadAttention.
    Automatically determines rank based on cumulative singular value energy.
    """
    def __init__(self, original_layer: nn.MultiheadAttention, energy_threshold: float = 0.95, min_rank: int = 16):
        super().__init__()
        self.original_dim = original_layer.embed_dim
        self.num_heads = original_layer.num_heads
        self.head_dim = self.original_dim // self.num_heads
        self.dropout_p = original_layer.dropout
        self.batch_first = getattr(original_layer, 'batch_first', False)

        w_qkv = original_layer.in_proj_weight.data.cpu().numpy()
        w_out = original_layer.out_proj.weight.data.cpu().numpy()

        U_qkv, S_qkv, Vh_qkv = np.linalg.svd(w_qkv, full_matrices=False)
        energy_qkv = np.cumsum(S_qkv) / np.sum(S_qkv)
        rank_qkv = np.argmax(energy_qkv >= energy_threshold) + 1
        rank_qkv = max(min_rank, min(rank_qkv, w_qkv.shape[1]))

        U_out, S_out, Vh_out = np.linalg.svd(w_out, full_matrices=False)
        energy_out = np.cumsum(S_out) / np.sum(S_out)
        rank_out = np.argmax(energy_out >= energy_threshold) + 1
        rank_out = max(min_rank, min(rank_out, w_out.shape[1]))

        self.rank_qkv = rank_qkv
        self.rank_out = rank_out

        self.U_qkv = nn.Parameter(torch.from_numpy(U_qkv[:, :rank_qkv]).float())
        self.S_qkv_sqrt = nn.Parameter(torch.from_numpy(np.sqrt(S_qkv[:rank_qkv])).float())
        self.Vh_qkv = nn.Parameter(torch.from_numpy(Vh_qkv[:rank_qkv, :]).float())

        self.U_out = nn.Parameter(torch.from_numpy(U_out[:, :rank_out]).float())
        self.S_out_sqrt = nn.Parameter(torch.from_numpy(np.sqrt(S_out[:rank_out])).float())
        self.Vh_out = nn.Parameter(torch.from_numpy(Vh_out[:rank_out, :]).float())

        if original_layer.in_proj_bias is not None:
            self.bias_qkv = nn.Parameter(original_layer.in_proj_bias.data.clone())
        else:
            self.bias_qkv = None
        if original_layer.out_proj.bias is not None:
            self.bias_out = nn.Parameter(original_layer.out_proj.bias.data.clone())
        else:
            self.bias_out = None

        self.kdim = getattr(original_layer, 'kdim', None)
        self.vdim = getattr(original_layer, 'vdim', None)
        self.add_bias_kv = getattr(original_layer, 'add_bias_kv', False)
        self.add_zero_attn = getattr(original_layer, 'add_zero_attn', False)

    def forward(self, query, key, value, key_padding_mask=None, need_weights=True,
                attn_mask=None, average_attn_weights=True):
        x = query
        dim = self.original_dim

        projected_x = torch.matmul(x, self.Vh_qkv.t())
        qkv_compressed = torch.matmul(projected_x, self.U_qkv.t() * self.S_qkv_sqrt.view(1, -1, 1))
        if self.bias_qkv is not None:
            qkv_compressed += self.bias_qkv

        q = qkv_compressed[:, :, :dim]
        k = qkv_compressed[:, :, dim:2*dim]
        v = qkv_compressed[:, :, 2*dim:]

        scale = 1.0 / np.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        attn_scores = torch.matmul(q, k.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
        if attn_mask is not None:
            attn_scores += attn_mask
        if key_padding_mask is not None:
            attn_scores = attn_scores.masked_fill(key_padding_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e9)

        attn_weights = F.softmax(attn_scores, dim=-1)
        attn_weights = F.dropout(attn_weights, p=self.dropout_p, training=self.training)

        context = torch.matmul(attn_weights, v)

        context_fused = torch.matmul(context, self.Vh_out.t() * self.S_out_sqrt.view(1, -1, 1))
        final_output = torch.matmul(context_fused, self.U_out.t())
        if self.bias_out is not None:
            final_output += self.bias_out

        return final_output, attn_weights if need_weights else None

══════════════════ factorised_matrix_state.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from ..core.registry import AUX_REGISTRY

@AUX_REGISTRY.register("factorised_matrix_state")
class FactorisedMatrixState(nn.Module):
    """Maintains a low‑rank factorised state for linear attention."""
    def __init__(self, d_model, rank=32):
        super().__init__()
        self.d_model = d_model
        self.rank = rank
        self.L = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(d_model, rank) * 0.01)
        self.R = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(d_model, rank) * 0.01)

    def forward(self, x, k, v):
        """
        Args:
            x: (B, D) query token
            k: (B, D) key token
            v: (B, D) value token
        Returns:
            updated state (B, D, D) and output (B, D)
        """
        B, D = x.shape
        H = self.L @ self.R.T  # (D, D)
        Hk = H @ k.unsqueeze(-1)  # (B, D, 1)
        u = x.unsqueeze(-1) - Hk  # (B, D, 1)
        # Update factors with outer product
        self.L.data = torch.cat([self.L, u.squeeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)], dim=-1)  # (D, r+1)
        self.R.data = torch.cat([self.R, v.unsqueeze(-1)], dim=-1)             # (D, r+1)
        # Truncation via SVD
        M = self.L @ self.R.T
        U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(M, full_matrices=False)
        self.L.data = U[:, :self.rank]
        self.R.data = Vt[:self.rank, :].T @ torch.diag(S[:self.rank])
        # Output: attention result = H x
        out = (self.L @ self.R.T) @ x.unsqueeze(-1)  # (B, D, 1)
        return out.squeeze(-1)

══════════════════ svd_attention.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from ..core.base import BaseAttention
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("svd")
class SVDLinearAttention(BaseAttention):
    """
    SVD‑compressed attention: uses low‑rank projection of K and V using SVD of the
    hidden states (scene‑dependent) when seq_len > rank, else full attention.
    """
    def __init__(self, config, rank=64):
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim

        self.query = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.key = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.value = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(config.attention_probs_dropout_prob)

    def transpose_for_scores(self, x):
        new_shape = x.size()[:-1] + (self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
        x = x.view(*new_shape)
        return x.permute(0, 2, 1, 3)

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, **kwargs):
        batch_size, seq_len, _ = hidden_states.shape
        Q = self.transpose_for_scores(self.query(hidden_states))
        K = self.transpose_for_scores(self.key(hidden_states))
        V = self.transpose_for_scores(self.value(hidden_states))

        if self.rank is None or seq_len <= self.rank:
            scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1)) / (self.head_dim ** 0.5)
            if attention_mask is not None:
                scores = scores + attention_mask
            probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
            probs = self.dropout(probs)
            context = torch.matmul(probs, V)
        else:
            # SVD compression – for each batch element
            original_device = hidden_states.device
            hidden_cpu = hidden_states.cpu() if original_device.type == 'mps' else hidden_states

            U_r_list = []
            for b in range(batch_size):
                U, S, _ = torch.svd_lowrank(hidden_cpu[b], q=self.rank, niter=2)
                U_r_list.append(U[:, :self.rank])
            U_r = torch.stack(U_r_list, dim=0).to(original_device)          # (B, seq_len, rank)
            U_r = U_r.unsqueeze(1)                                          # (B, 1, seq_len, rank)

            K_proj = torch.matmul(U_r.transpose(-2, -1), K)                 # (B,1,rank,d) -> actually (B,H,rank,d)
            V_proj = torch.matmul(U_r.transpose(-2, -1), V)

            scores = torch.matmul(Q, K_proj.transpose(-2, -1)) / (self.head_dim ** 0.5)
            if attention_mask is not None:
                scores = scores + attention_mask.unsqueeze(-1)
            probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
            probs = self.dropout(probs)
            context = torch.matmul(probs, V_proj)

        context = context.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous()
        context = context.view(batch_size, seq_len, self.all_head_size)
        return (context, None) if kwargs.get('output_attentions', False) else (context,)
══════════════════ asvd_attention.py ══════════════════
"""
ASVD Attention: Activation‑Aware Singular Value Decomposition for Attention Compression
"""
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
import numpy as np
from ..core.base import BaseAttention
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY


@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("asvd")
class ASVDAttention(BaseAttention):
    """
    Replaces MultiheadAttention with low‑rank decomposed projections
    where the SVD is weighted by activation variance (activation‑aware).
    Stores U, S, Vh separately per projection.
    """
    def __init__(self, config, rank=64, activation_scales=None):
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.rank = rank

        self.query_u  = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.all_head_size, self.rank))
        self.query_s  = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.rank))
        self.query_vh = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.rank, config.hidden_size))

        self.key_u    = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.all_head_size, self.rank))
        self.key_s    = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.rank))
        self.key_vh   = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.rank, config.hidden_size))

        self.value_u  = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.all_head_size, self.rank))
        self.value_s  = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.rank))
        self.value_vh = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.rank, config.hidden_size))

        self.out_proj_u = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(config.hidden_size, self.rank))
        self.out_proj_s = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.rank))
        self.out_proj_vh = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.rank, self.all_head_size))

        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(config.attention_probs_dropout_prob)

        if activation_scales is not None:
            self.apply_activation_scales(activation_scales)

    def apply_activation_scales(self, scales):
        """Placeholder: can be used to incorporate activation statistics before SVD."""
        pass

    def _reshape(self, x, batch_size):
        x = x.view(batch_size, -1, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
        return x.permute(0, 2, 1, 3)

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, **kwargs):
        batch_size, seq_len, _ = hidden_states.shape

        W_q = (self.query_u * self.query_s) @ self.query_vh
        W_k = (self.key_u   * self.key_s)   @ self.key_vh
        W_v = (self.value_u * self.value_s) @ self.value_vh
        W_o = (self.out_proj_u * self.out_proj_s) @ self.out_proj_vh

        Q = F.linear(hidden_states, W_q, None).view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0,2,1,3)
        K = F.linear(hidden_states, W_k, None).view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0,2,1,3)
        V = F.linear(hidden_states, W_v, None).view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0,2,1,3)

        scale = 1.0 / (self.head_dim ** 0.5)
        scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale

        if attention_mask is not None:
            scores = scores + attention_mask.unsqueeze(1)

        probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
        probs = self.dropout(probs)

        context = torch.matmul(probs, V)
        context = context.permute(0,2,1,3).contiguous().view(batch_size, seq_len, self.all_head_size)

        out = F.linear(context, W_o)
        return (out, None) if kwargs.get('output_attentions') else (out,)

    @staticmethod
    def from_module(module, rank, activation_scales=None):
        """Create ASVDAttention by SVD of the original module's weights."""
        attn = ASVDAttention(module, rank, activation_scales)
        w_qkv = module.in_proj_weight.data.cpu().numpy()
        w_o   = module.out_proj.weight.data.cpu().numpy()
        all_head = module.embed_dim * module.num_heads
        W_q = w_qkv[:all_head, :]
        W_k = w_qkv[all_head:2*all_head, :]
        W_v = w_qkv[2*all_head:, :]

        def decomposed_weights(W, rank):
            U, S, Vh = np.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
            U_r = U[:, :rank]
            S_r = S[:rank]
            Vh_r = Vh[:rank, :]
            return (torch.tensor(U_r, dtype=torch.float32),
                    torch.tensor(S_r, dtype=torch.float32),
                    torch.tensor(Vh_r, dtype=torch.float32))

        qu, qs, qvh = decomposed_weights(W_q, rank)
        ku, ks, kvh = decomposed_weights(W_k, rank)
        vu, vs, vvh = decomposed_weights(W_v, rank)
        ou, os, ovh = decomposed_weights(w_o, rank)

        attn.query_u.data = qu
        attn.query_s.data = qs
        attn.query_vh.data = qvh
        attn.key_u.data = ku
        attn.key_s.data = ks
        attn.key_vh.data = kvh
        attn.value_u.data = vu
        attn.value_s.data = vs
        attn.value_vh.data = vvh
        attn.out_proj_u.data = ou
        attn.out_proj_s.data = os
        attn.out_proj_vh.data = ovh
        return attn
══════════════════ salt.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from ..core.registry import AUX_REGISTRY

@AUX_REGISTRY.register("salt_linear")
class SALTLinear(nn.Module):
    """Singular Value Adaptation for a linear layer."""
    def __init__(self, in_features, out_features, rank=16):
        super().__init__()
        # Original weight frozen
        self.register_buffer('original_weight', torch.randn(out_features, in_features))
        # Decompose original weight via SVD (frozen)
        U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(self.original_weight, full_matrices=False)
        self.register_buffer('U', U)
        self.register_buffer('Vt', Vt)
        self.S = nn.Parameter(S)                # trainable copy of singular values
        self.gamma = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(rank))
        # Low‑rank update matrices
        self.A = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(out_features, rank) * 0.01)
        self.B = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(rank, in_features) * 0.01)

    def forward(self, x):
        # Adapted weight = U @ diag(S + gamma * S[:rank]) @ Vt + A @ B
        adapted_S = self.S[:self.gamma.shape[0]] * self.gamma  # scale top-k
        adapted_weight = self.U[:, :self.gamma.shape[0]] @ torch.diag(adapted_S) @ self.Vt[:self.gamma.shape[0], :]
        low_rank = self.A @ self.B
        W = adapted_weight + low_rank
        return F.linear(x, W)

══════════════════ tpa_attention.py ══════════════════
"""
Tensor Product Attention (TPA)
Paper: "Tensor Product Attention Is All You Need"
"""
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from ..core.base import BaseAttention
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY


@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("tpa")
class TPAttention(BaseAttention):
    def __init__(self, config, rank_Q=1, rank_K=1, rank_V=1, use_cache=False):
        super().__init__(config, rank_Q)
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.rank_Q = rank_Q
        self.rank_K = rank_K
        self.rank_V = rank_V
        self.use_cache = use_cache

        self.Q_factor1 = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.num_heads * self.rank_Q, bias=False)
        self.Q_factor2 = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.num_heads * self.rank_Q, bias=False)
        self.K_factor1 = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.num_heads * self.rank_K, bias=False)
        self.K_factor2 = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.num_heads * self.rank_K, bias=False)
        self.V_factor1 = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.num_heads * self.rank_V, bias=False)
        self.V_factor2 = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.num_heads * self.rank_V, bias=False)

        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(self.all_head_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(config.attention_probs_dropout_prob)
        self.kv_cache = None

    def _tensor_product(self, x, factor1, factor2, rank):
        f1 = factor1(x).view(x.shape[0], -1, self.num_heads, rank)
        f2 = factor2(x).view(x.shape[0], -1, self.num_heads, rank)
        return torch.einsum('bnhd,bnhk->bnhdk', f1, f2).reshape(
            x.shape[0], -1, self.num_heads, rank * rank
        )

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, past_kv=None, **kwargs):
        batch_size, seq_len, _ = hidden_states.shape
        Q = self._tensor_product(hidden_states, self.Q_factor1, self.Q_factor2, self.rank_Q)
        K = self._tensor_product(hidden_states, self.K_factor1, self.K_factor2, self.rank_K)
        V = self._tensor_product(hidden_states, self.V_factor1, self.V_factor2, self.rank_V)

        Q = Q.view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_heads, -1).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        K = K.view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_heads, -1).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        V = V.view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_heads, -1).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)

        if self.use_cache and past_kv is not None:
            past_K, past_V = past_kv
            K = torch.cat([past_K, K], dim=2)
            V = torch.cat([past_V, V], dim=2)
        if self.use_cache:
            self.kv_cache = (K, V)

        scale = 1.0 / (self.head_dim ** 0.5)
        scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
        if attention_mask is not None:
            scores = scores + attention_mask.unsqueeze(1)
        probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
        probs = self.dropout(probs)
        context = torch.matmul(probs, V)
        context = context.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(batch_size, seq_len, self.all_head_size)
        return (self.out_proj(context), self.kv_cache) if kwargs.get('output_attentions') else (self.out_proj(context), self.kv_cache)

══════════════════ svd_llm_v2_attention.py ══════════════════
"""
SVD‑LLM V2 Attention: Truncation‑aware SVD for LLM Compression (improved version)
"""
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
import numpy as np
from ..core.base import BaseAttention
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY


@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("svd_llm_v2")
class SVDLLMV2Attention(BaseAttention):
    """
    Similar to ASVD but uses a different truncation strategy: dynamic rank based on
    singular value cumulative energy threshold, and optionally applies weight tying.
    """
    def __init__(self, config, rank=64, energy_threshold=0.95):
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.rank = rank
        self.energy_threshold = energy_threshold

        self.query_u  = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.all_head_size, self.rank))
        self.query_s  = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.rank))
        self.query_vh = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.rank, config.hidden_size))

        self.key_u    = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.all_head_size, self.rank))
        self.key_s    = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.rank))
        self.key_vh   = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.rank, config.hidden_size))

        self.value_u  = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.all_head_size, self.rank))
        self.value_s  = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.rank))
        self.value_vh = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.rank, config.hidden_size))

        self.out_proj_u = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(config.hidden_size, self.rank))
        self.out_proj_s = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.rank))
        self.out_proj_vh = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.rank, self.all_head_size))

        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(config.attention_probs_dropout_prob)

    def _reshape(self, x, batch_size):
        x = x.view(batch_size, -1, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
        return x.permute(0, 2, 1, 3)

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, **kwargs):
        batch_size, seq_len, _ = hidden_states.shape

        W_q = (self.query_u * self.query_s) @ self.query_vh
        W_k = (self.key_u   * self.key_s)   @ self.key_vh
        W_v = (self.value_u * self.value_s) @ self.value_vh
        W_o = (self.out_proj_u * self.out_proj_s) @ self.out_proj_vh

        Q = F.linear(hidden_states, W_q).view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0,2,1,3)
        K = F.linear(hidden_states, W_k).view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0,2,1,3)
        V = F.linear(hidden_states, W_v).view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0,2,1,3)

        scale = 1.0 / (self.head_dim ** 0.5)
        scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
        if attention_mask is not None:
            scores = scores + attention_mask.unsqueeze(1)
        probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
        probs = self.dropout(probs)

        context = torch.matmul(probs, V)
        context = context.permute(0,2,1,3).contiguous().view(batch_size, seq_len, self.all_head_size)
        out = F.linear(context, W_o)
        return (out, None) if kwargs.get('output_attentions') else (out,)

    @staticmethod
    def from_module(module, energy_threshold=0.95, min_rank=None):
        """
        Create SVDLLMV2Attention by performing SVD on the original module's weights,
        automatically choosing ranks based on energy threshold.
        """
        w_qkv = module.in_proj_weight.data.cpu().numpy()
        w_o   = module.out_proj.weight.data.cpu().numpy()
        all_head = module.embed_dim * module.num_heads

        W_q = w_qkv[:all_head, :]
        W_k = w_qkv[all_head:2*all_head, :]
        W_v = w_qkv[2*all_head:, :]

        def truncate_svd(W):
            U, S, Vh = np.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
            cum_energy = np.cumsum(S) / np.sum(S)
            rank = np.searchsorted(cum_energy, energy_threshold) + 1
            if min_rank:
                rank = max(rank, min_rank)
            rank = min(rank, len(S))
            return (torch.tensor(U[:, :rank], dtype=torch.float32),
                    torch.tensor(S[:rank], dtype=torch.float32),
                    torch.tensor(Vh[:rank, :], dtype=torch.float32)), rank

        (qu, qs, qvh), rq = truncate_svd(W_q)
        (ku, ks, kvh), rk = truncate_svd(W_k)
        (vu, vs, vvh), rv = truncate_svd(W_v)
        (ou, os, ovh), ro = truncate_svd(w_o)

        max_r = max(rq, rk, rv, ro)
        def pad_to(t, target_r):
            if t.shape[-1] < target_r:
                pad = torch.zeros(*t.shape[:-1], target_r - t.shape[-1], dtype=t.dtype)
                return torch.cat([t, pad], dim=-1)
            return t

        attn = SVDLLMV2Attention(module, rank=max_r, energy_threshold=energy_threshold)
        attn.query_u.data = pad_to(qu, max_r)
        attn.query_s.data = pad_to(qs, max_r)
        attn.query_vh.data = pad_to(qvh, max_r)
        attn.key_u.data   = pad_to(ku, max_r)
        attn.key_s.data   = pad_to(ks, max_r)
        attn.key_vh.data  = pad_to(kvh, max_r)
        attn.value_u.data = pad_to(vu, max_r)
        attn.value_s.data = pad_to(vs, max_r)
        attn.value_vh.data = pad_to(vvh, max_r)
        attn.out_proj_u.data = pad_to(ou, max_r)
        attn.out_proj_s.data = pad_to(os, max_r)
        attn.out_proj_vh.data = pad_to(ovh, max_r)
        return attn
══════════════════ primal_attention.py ══════════════════
"""
Primal‑Attention: Self‑attention through Asymmetric Kernel SVD in Primal Representation
"""
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from ..core.base import BaseAttention
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY


@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("primal")
class PrimalAttention(BaseAttention):
    def __init__(self, config, rank=64):
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.rank = rank

        self.query = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.key   = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.value = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(self.all_head_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(config.attention_probs_dropout_prob)

    def _reshape(self, x, batch_size):
        return x.view(batch_size, -1, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, **kwargs):
        batch_size, seq_len, _ = hidden_states.shape
        Q = self._reshape(self.query(hidden_states), batch_size)
        K = self._reshape(self.key(hidden_states), batch_size)
        V = self._reshape(self.value(hidden_states), batch_size)

        # Landmark sampling
        num_landmarks = self.rank
        if seq_len % num_landmarks == 0:
            seg = seq_len // num_landmarks
            Q_land = Q.reshape(batch_size, self.num_heads, num_landmarks, seg, self.head_dim).mean(dim=3)
            K_land = K.reshape(batch_size, self.num_heads, num_landmarks, seg, self.head_dim).mean(dim=3)
        else:
            idx = torch.linspace(0, seq_len-1, num_landmarks).long().to(hidden_states.device)
            Q_land = Q[:, :, idx, :]
            K_land = K[:, :, idx, :]

        scale = 1.0 / (self.head_dim ** 0.5)
        F1 = torch.matmul(Q, K_land.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
        F2 = torch.matmul(Q_land, K_land.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
        F3 = torch.matmul(Q_land, K.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale

        # Pseudo‑inverse with iteration
        def moore_penrose(A, iters=6):
            X = torch.linalg.pinv(A)
            for _ in range(iters):
                X = X @ (2 * torch.eye(A.shape[-1], device=A.device) - A @ X)
            return X
        F2_pinv = moore_penrose(F2)

        attn = torch.matmul(F1, torch.matmul(F2_pinv, F3))
        if attention_mask is not None:
            attn = attn + attention_mask.unsqueeze(1)

        probs = F.softmax(attn, dim=-1)
        probs = self.dropout(probs)
        context = torch.matmul(probs, V)
        context = context.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(batch_size, seq_len, self.all_head_size)
        return (self.out_proj(context), None) if kwargs.get('output_attentions') else (self.out_proj(context),)
══════════════════ unified_rope.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from ..core.registry import AUX_REGISTRY

@AUX_REGISTRY.register("compression_unified_rope")
class UnifiedRoPE(nn.Module):
    """
    Universal Rotary Position Embedding.
    Applies the same rotation to attention and SSM branches,
    guaranteeing positional consistency in hybrid architectures.
    """
    def __init__(self, dim: int, max_seq_len: int = 4096, base: int = 10000):
        super().__init__()
        self.dim = dim
        self.max_seq_len = max_seq_len
        self.base = base

        # Inverse frequencies: 1/(base^(2i/dim))
        inv_freq = 1.0 / (base ** (torch.arange(0, dim, 2).float() / dim))
        self.register_buffer("inv_freq", inv_freq)
        self._build_cache(max_seq_len)

    def _build_cache(self, seq_len: int):
        """Precompute cos & sin tables for positions up to seq_len."""
        t = torch.arange(seq_len, device=self.inv_freq.device)
        freqs = torch.einsum("i,j->ij", t, self.inv_freq)          # (seq_len, dim/2)
        emb = torch.cat((freqs, freqs), dim=-1)                    # (seq_len, dim)
        self.register_buffer("cos_cached", emb.cos())              # (seq_len, dim)
        self.register_buffer("sin_cached", emb.sin())

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Args:
            x: (batch, seq_len, dim)
        Returns:
            rotated tensor of same shape
        """
        seq_len = x.shape[1]
        if seq_len > self.max_seq_len:
            self._build_cache(seq_len)

        cos = self.cos_cached[:seq_len].unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(2)   # (1, L, 1, D)
        sin = self.sin_cached[:seq_len].unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(2)

        x_rot = x * cos + self._rotate_half(x) * sin
        return x_rot

    @staticmethod
    def _rotate_half(x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Rotates the second half of the last dimension."""
        x1 = x[..., : x.shape[-1] // 2]
        x2 = x[..., x.shape[-1] // 2 :]
        return torch.cat((-x2, x1), dim=-1)

══════════════════ eat.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from .progressive_token_pruning import ProgressiveTokenPruning
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("eat")
class EATBlock(nn.Module):
    """
    Efficient Attention Transformer block.
    Standard MultiheadAttention + FFN, with optional progressive pruning
    after the feed‑forward sub‑layer.
    """
    def __init__(self, d_model: int, n_heads: int, prune_layer: ProgressiveTokenPruning = None):
        super().__init__()
        self.attn = nn.MultiheadAttention(d_model, n_heads, batch_first=True)
        self.ffn = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(d_model, 4 * d_model),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Linear(4 * d_model, d_model)
        )
        self.norm1 = nn.LayerNorm(d_model)
        self.norm2 = nn.LayerNorm(d_model)
        self.prune_layer = prune_layer   # optional token pruner

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor, token_indices: torch.Tensor = None):
        """
        Args:
            x: (batch, seq_len, hidden)
            token_indices: (batch, seq_len) original token ids – used only if pruning
        Returns:
            out: (batch, seq_len, hidden)
            updated_token_indices: if pruning was applied, reduced indices
        """
        # Multi‑head attention – no external mask for simplicity; you can add if needed
        attn_out, _ = self.attn(x, x, x)
        x = self.norm1(x + attn_out)
        x = self.norm2(x + self.ffn(x))

        # Optional progressive pruning
        if self.prune_layer is not None:
            x, new_indices = self.prune_layer(x)
            if token_indices is not None:
                token_indices = token_indices.gather(1, new_indices)
            return x, token_indices
        return x, token_indices

══════════════════ progressive_token_pruning.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from ..core.registry import AUX_REGISTRY

@AUX_REGISTRY.register("progressive_token_pruning")
class ProgressiveTokenPruning(nn.Module):
    """
    Learnable token pruning with a lightweight score network.
    After training, it dynamically removes low‑importance tokens.
    """
    def __init__(self, d_model: int, keep_ratio: float = 0.8):
        super().__init__()
        self.score_network = nn.Linear(d_model, 1)
        self.keep_ratio = keep_ratio

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor):
        """
        Args:
            x: (batch, seq_len, hidden)
        Returns:
            pruned_x: (batch, new_len, hidden)
            kept_indices: (batch, new_len) – sorted indices of kept tokens
        """
        B, L, D = x.shape
        scores = torch.sigmoid(self.score_network(x)).squeeze(-1)   # (B, L)
        k = max(1, int(L * self.keep_ratio))
        _, top_indices = torch.topk(scores, k, dim=-1)              # (B, k)
        # Sort to preserve original order (important for positional information)
        top_indices, _ = torch.sort(top_indices, dim=-1)
        pruned_x = torch.gather(x, 1, top_indices.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, D))
        return pruned_x, top_indices

══════════════════ squeezing_heads_distill.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import List
from ..core.registry import AUX_REGISTRY

@AUX_REGISTRY.register("squeezing_heads_distiller")
class SqueezingHeadsDistiller:
    """
    Distills teacher attention maps into a student with a different number of heads
    using truncated SVD across the head dimension.
    The student's attention is projected to a single map that must match the
    low‑rank reconstruction of the teacher.
    """
    def __init__(self, teacher: nn.Module, student: nn.Module, rank: int = 64):
        self.teacher = teacher
        self.student = student
        self.rank = rank

    def compute_attention(self, model: nn.Module, x: torch.Tensor) -> List[torch.Tensor]:
        """
        Returns attention maps from the model.
        Assumes model.get_attentions(x) returns a list of tensors,
        each of shape (batch, num_heads, seq_len, seq_len).
        """
        with torch.no_grad():
            _, attn_maps = model.get_attentions(x)
        return attn_maps

    def distill_loss(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Computes the MSE loss between the teacher's compressed attention
        and the student's averaged attention.
        """
        attn_teacher = self.compute_attention(self.teacher, x)   # list per layer
        attn_student = self.compute_attention(self.student, x)

        total_loss = 0.0
        for t_map, s_map in zip(attn_teacher, attn_student):
            # t_map: (B, Ht, L, L), s_map: (B, Hs, L, L)
            B, Ht, L, _ = t_map.shape
            # Flatten teacher maps to (B, L*L, Ht)
            t_flat = t_map.permute(0, 2, 3, 1).reshape(B, L*L, Ht)

            # Truncated SVD
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(t_flat, full_matrices=False)   # U: (B, L*L, L*L), S: (B, min_dim), Vt: (B, min_dim, Ht)
            r = min(self.rank, Ht, L*L)
            U_r = U[:, :, :r]                                          # (B, L*L, r)
            S_r = S[:, :r]                                             # (B, r)
            Vt_r = Vt[:, :r, :]                                       # (B, r, Ht)

            # Reconstruct compressed teacher (B, L*L, Ht) -> (B, L, L, Ht) -> (B, r, L, L)
            t_compressed = U_r @ torch.diag_embed(S_r) @ Vt_r          # (B, L*L, Ht)
            t_compressed = t_compressed.view(B, L, L, Ht).permute(0, 3, 1, 2)  # (B, r, L, L)

            # Student: average all heads to one map (B, 1, L, L)
            s_avg = s_map.mean(dim=1, keepdim=True)                    # (B, 1, L, L)

            # Expand student map to match rank dimension
            s_expanded = s_avg.expand(-1, r, -1, -1)                  # (B, r, L, L)

            total_loss += F.mse_loss(s_expanded, t_compressed)

        return total_loss

══════════════════ xattention.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import math
from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("xattention")
class XAttention(nn.Module):
    """
    XAttention: Block Sparse with Antidiagonal Scoring.
    Divides attention matrix into blocks, computes block importance as the sum of
    antidiagonals, and retains only top blocks per row of blocks.
    """
    def __init__(self, config, block_size=64, sparsity=0.5):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.block_size = block_size
        self.sparsity = sparsity   # fraction of blocks to prune (keep 1-sparsity)
        self.q_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.k_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.v_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(config.attention_probs_dropout_prob)

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, **kwargs):
        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        H, d = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        Q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, d).permute(0,2,1,3)
        K = self.k_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, d).permute(0,2,1,3)
        V = self.v_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, d).permute(0,2,1,3)

        # Pad to multiple of block_size
        pad_len = (self.block_size - (L % self.block_size)) % self.block_size
        if pad_len > 0:
            Q = F.pad(Q, (0,0,0,pad_len))
            K = F.pad(K, (0,0,0,pad_len))
            V = F.pad(V, (0,0,0,pad_len))
        padded_len = L + pad_len
        n_blocks = padded_len // self.block_size

        # Reshape to blocks: (B, H, n_blocks, block_size, d)
        Q_blk = Q.view(B, H, n_blocks, self.block_size, d)
        K_blk = K.view(B, H, n_blocks, self.block_size, d)
        V_blk = V.view(B, H, n_blocks, self.block_size, d)

        # Block‑wise scoring: compute antidiagonal sum as block importance
        # For each block pair (i,j), importance = sum over all diagonal elements of attention?
        # We'll compute mean attention per block as a proxy for importance.
        scale = d ** -0.5
        # Compute block‑level interaction using averaged queries and keys per block
        Q_mean = Q_blk.mean(dim=3)   # (B, H, n_blocks, d)
        K_mean = K_blk.mean(dim=3)
        block_scores = torch.matmul(Q_mean, K_mean.transpose(-2,-1)) * scale  # (B, H, n_blocks, n_blocks)

        # For each query block, keep top k = (1-sparsity)*n_blocks key blocks
        num_keep = max(1, int(n_blocks * (1 - self.sparsity)))
        topk_scores, topk_idx = torch.topk(block_scores, num_keep, dim=-1)

        # Create sparse block mask: (B, H, n_blocks, n_blocks)
        mask = torch.zeros_like(block_scores)
        mask.scatter_(-1, topk_idx, 1.0)

        # Now compute fine‑grained attention only within selected blocks.
        # We'll loop over blocks (slow but for demo)
        out_blocks = torch.zeros(B, H, n_blocks, self.block_size, d, device=Q.device)
        for i in range(n_blocks):
            qi = Q_blk[:, :, i]   # (B, H, blk, d)
            for j in range(n_blocks):
                if mask[:, :, i, j].sum() == 0:
                    continue
                kj = K_blk[:, :, j]
                vj = V_blk[:, :, j]
                scores = torch.matmul(qi, kj.transpose(-2,-1)) * scale  # (B, H, blk, blk)
                # Causal? We skip causal for simplicity; you could add causal mask.
                attn = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
                ctx = torch.matmul(attn, vj)
                out_blocks[:, :, i] += ctx

        out = out_blocks.view(B, H, padded_len, d)[:, :, :L, :]
        out = out.permute(0,2,1,3).contiguous().view(B, L, D)
        return self.out_proj(out), None
══════════════════ moba_attention.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import math
from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("moba")
class MoBAAttention(nn.Module):
    """
    Mixture of Blocks (MoBA): Trainable block‑sparse attention via gating.
    Divides the sequence into blocks and selects top‑k blocks per query token.
    """
    def __init__(self, config, block_size=64, top_k=8):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.block_size = block_size
        self.top_k = top_k
        self.q_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.k_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.v_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(config.attention_probs_dropout_prob)
        # Gate to select blocks per head (maybe per query token)
        self.router = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, 1)   # not used in this simple version; we do block selection via key statistics.
        # For speed, we'll compute block importance by mean key L2 norm.
        # A more sophisticated MoBA would use a learned gate; we simplify here.

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, **kwargs):
        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        H = self.num_heads
        d = self.head_dim
        Q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, d).permute(0,2,1,3)  # (B, H, L, d)
        K = self.k_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, d).permute(0,2,1,3)
        V = self.v_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, d).permute(0,2,1,3)

        # Break keys into blocks
        num_blocks = (L + self.block_size - 1) // self.block_size
        # Compute block importance scores: average L2 norm for each block per head
        block_scores = torch.zeros(B, H, num_blocks, device=Q.device)
        for b_idx in range(num_blocks):
            start = b_idx * self.block_size
            end = min(start + self.block_size, L)
            k_blk = K[:, :, start:end, :]
            block_scores[:, :, b_idx] = k_blk.norm(dim=-1).mean(dim=2)  # (B, H)

        # For each query position, select top_k blocks
        top_blocks = torch.topk(block_scores, min(self.top_k, num_blocks), dim=-1).indices  # (B, H, K)

        # Build sparse attention: for each query, attend only to tokens in top_blocks
        out = torch.zeros_like(V)
        scale = d ** -0.5

        for b in range(B):
            for h in range(H):
                selected_blocks = top_blocks[b, h]
                # Gather K and V from those blocks
                k_sel = []
                v_sel = []
                block_boundaries = []
                offset = 0
                for blk in selected_blocks:
                    blk = blk.item()
                    start = blk * self.block_size
                    end = min(start + self.block_size, L)
                    k_sel.append(K[b, h, start:end])
                    v_sel.append(V[b, h, start:end])
                    block_boundaries.append((offset, offset + (end-start)))
                    offset += (end-start)
                if not k_sel:
                    continue
                K_cat = torch.cat(k_sel, dim=0)  # (L_sel, d)
                V_cat = torch.cat(v_sel, dim=0)
                Q_bh = Q[b, h]   # (L, d)
                scores = torch.mm(Q_bh, K_cat.T) * scale
                # Apply attention mask if provided (simplified)
                attn = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
                ctx = torch.mm(attn, V_cat)
                out[b, h] = ctx
        out = out.permute(0,2,1,3).contiguous().view(B, L, D)
        return self.out_proj(out), None
══════════════════ routing_attention.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import math
from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("routing")
class RoutingAttention(nn.Module):
    """
    Routing Transformer: Attention via online k‑means clustering.
    Queries are assigned to clusters, and attention is computed only within each cluster.
    """
    def __init__(self, config, num_clusters=32, n_iters=3):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.num_clusters = num_clusters
        self.n_iters = n_iters

        self.q_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.k_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.v_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(config.attention_probs_dropout_prob)

    def _kmeans_assign(self, Q, centroids):
        # Q: (B, H, L, d), centroids: (B, H, K, d)
        dist = torch.cdist(Q, centroids)  # (B, H, L, K)
        return torch.argmin(dist, dim=-1)  # (B, H, L)

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, **kwargs):
        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        H, d = self.num_heads, self.head_dim

        # Project and reshape to multi‑head
        Q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, d).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)  # (B, H, L, d)
        K = self.k_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, d).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        V = self.v_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, d).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)

        # Initialise centroids with first K tokens (or random)
        centroids = Q[:, :, :self.num_clusters, :]  # (B, H, K, d)

        # Online k‑means refinement
        for _ in range(self.n_iters):
            cluster_ids = self._kmeans_assign(Q, centroids)   # (B, H, L)
            # Update centroids as mean of assigned queries
            centroids = torch.zeros_like(centroids)
            for k in range(self.num_clusters):
                mask = (cluster_ids == k).unsqueeze(-1).float()  # (B, H, L, 1)
                sum_q = (Q.unsqueeze(2) * mask.unsqueeze(2)).sum(dim=3, keepdim=True)  # (B, H, 1, d)
                count = mask.sum(dim=2, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1)
                centroids[:, :, k, :] = (sum_q / count).squeeze(2)

        # Final assignment
        cluster_ids = self._kmeans_assign(Q, centroids)

        # Intra‑cluster attention
        out = torch.zeros_like(V)
        scale = d ** -0.5

        for k in range(self.num_clusters):
            mask = (cluster_ids == k).unsqueeze(-1)  # (B, H, L, 1)

            # Apply mask to Q, K, V (set non‑members to 0 – not necessary, we'll mask scores)
            Q_k = Q * mask
            K_k = K * mask
            V_k = V * mask

            # Scores within cluster (mask out tokens from other clusters)
            scores = torch.matmul(Q_k, K_k.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale  # (B, H, L, L)
            # Add large negative value where tokens do not belong to the same cluster
            mask_add = (mask == 0).float() * -1e9  # (B, H, L, 1) broadcast to (B, H, L, L)
            scores = scores + mask_add.squeeze(-1).unsqueeze(-2)  # + (B, H, 1, L) effectively

            # Optional: causal masking? Uncomment if needed for language models
            # causal_mask = torch.tril(torch.ones(L, L, device=scores.device)).bool()
            # scores = scores.masked_fill(~causal_mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), float('-inf'))

            attn = torch.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
            attn = self.dropout(attn)
            out_k = torch.matmul(attn, V_k)  # (B, H, L, d)
            out = out + out_k

        # Combine heads and project
        out = out.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(B, L, D)
        return self.out_proj(out), None
══════════════════ cluster_attention.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import math
from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("cluster")
class ClusterAttention(nn.Module):
    """
    ClusterFormer: Token clustering via k‑means to form cluster centroids,
    then intra‑cluster attention within each cluster.
    """
    def __init__(self, config, num_clusters=64, n_iters=3):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.num_clusters = num_clusters
        self.n_iters = n_iters
        self.q_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.k_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.v_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(config.attention_probs_dropout_prob)

    def _kmeans(self, x, iters=3):
        """Batch‑wise k‑means for clustering."""
        B, L, D = x.shape
        # Initialise centroids with random sampling from x
        idx = torch.randperm(L)[:self.num_clusters]
        centroids = x[:, idx, :]   # (B, K, D)
        for _ in range(iters):
            # Compute distances: (B, L, K)
            dist = torch.cdist(x, centroids)
            cluster_ids = torch.argmin(dist, dim=-1)  # (B, L)
            # Update centroids: mean of points in each cluster
            centroids = torch.zeros_like(centroids)
            for k in range(self.num_clusters):
                mask_k = (cluster_ids == k).unsqueeze(-1).float()
                sum_k = (x * mask_k).sum(dim=1, keepdim=True)
                count_k = mask_k.sum(dim=1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1)
                centroids[:, k, :] = (sum_k / count_k).squeeze(1)
        return centroids, cluster_ids

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, **kwargs):
        batch_size, seq_len, _ = hidden_states.shape
        H = self.num_heads
        D_h = self.head_dim

        Q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(batch_size, seq_len, H, D_h)
        K = self.k_proj(hidden_states).view(batch_size, seq_len, H, D_h)
        V = self.v_proj(hidden_states).view(batch_size, seq_len, H, D_h)

        # For clustering we use the original hidden states (or Q) to form groups.
        # We cluster per head using Q mean across heads for simplicity.
        # Here we cluster tokens globally (same clusters for all heads) using mean of Q.
        x_for_cluster = Q.mean(dim=2)   # (B, L, D_h) – use channel‑wise mean across heads? Better to use hidden_states.
        # Actually, use the hidden_states directly for clustering.
        centroids, cluster_ids = self._kmeans(hidden_states, self.n_iters)  # cluster_ids: (B, L)

        # For each cluster, compute attention within the cluster.
        # We'll do per head and per batch, but for simplicity we loop over clusters.
        out = torch.zeros_like(V)
        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(D_h)
        for k in range(self.num_clusters):
            # get mask for tokens in this cluster
            mask = (cluster_ids == k).unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1)  # (B, 1, L, 1)
            # Select Q, K, V for this cluster
            Q_k = Q * mask.unsqueeze(2).float()
            K_k = K * mask.unsqueeze(2).float()
            V_k = V * mask.unsqueeze(2).float()

            # Attention within cluster (causal? we just do full attention within cluster, no causal needed for encoding)
            # However, for language models we need causal; we assume not for simplicity (or we can add later).
            # We'll do full attention inside the cluster.
            scores_k = torch.einsum('blhd,bmhd->bhlm', Q_k, K_k) * scale
            # Mask out tokens not in this cluster by setting scores to -inf where token not in cluster.
            # But Q_k already zero for non-members, scores would be zero for those, still they could attend.
            # Better: create additive mask.
            # We'll manually set scores to -inf for non‑member queries and keys.
            # For simplicity, after softmax, the contributions from zero queries/keys will be zero anyway because they are zero.
            # We'll just proceed with masking by -inf.
            # Build a mask using cluster_ids
            mem_mask = (cluster_ids == k).unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)  # (B, 1, 1, L)
            # Expand to (B, H, L, L) by broadcasting
            mask_add = (~mem_mask).float() * -1e9
            scores_k = scores_k + mask_add
            attn_k = F.softmax(scores_k, dim=-1)
            attn_k = self.dropout(attn_k)
            out_k = torch.matmul(attn_k, V_k)   # (B, H, L, D_h)
            out += out_k

        context = out.permute(0,2,1,3).contiguous().view(batch_size, seq_len, -1)
        return self.out_proj(context), None
══════════════════ lsh_attention.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import math
from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("lsh")
class LSHAttention(nn.Module):
    """
    Reformer's LSH Attention.
    Uses Angular LSH with multiple rounds to bucket queries and keys.
    """
    def __init__(self, config, rounds=4, bucket_size=64):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.rounds = rounds
        self.bucket_size = bucket_size
        self.q_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.k_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.v_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(config.attention_probs_dropout_prob)

    def _hash(self, vectors, random_matrices):
        # vectors: (B, H, L, D), random_matrices: list of (D, bucket_size) tensors
        # Return hash codes for each round.
        codes = []
        for rot in random_matrices:
            proj = torch.einsum('bhnd,db->bhnb', F.normalize(vectors, dim=-1), rot)  # (B, H, L, B)
            # Sign to bits
            hash_int = (proj > 0).int().mul(torch.tensor([2**i for i in range(proj.size(-1))], device=vectors.device)).sum(-1)
            codes.append(hash_int)
        return codes

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, **kwargs):
        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        H, d = self.num_heads, self.head_dim

        Q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, d).transpose(1,2)  # (B, H, L, d)
        K = self.k_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, d).transpose(1,2)
        V = self.v_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, d).transpose(1,2)

        # Generate random rotation matrices for each round
        rot_mats = [torch.randn(d, self.bucket_size, device=Q.device) for _ in range(self.rounds)]
        rot_mats = [F.normalize(rm, dim=0) for rm in rot_mats]

        # Hash Q and K
        q_codes = self._hash(Q, rot_mats)   # list of (B, H, L) per round
        k_codes = self._hash(K, rot_mats)

        out = torch.zeros_like(V)
        for rnd in range(self.rounds):
            q_hash = q_codes[rnd]  # (B, H, L)
            k_hash = k_codes[rnd]
            # For each batch and head, group queries and keys by hash code
            # We'll compute attention per bucket.
            # This is O(N^2) in worst case but used for bucket size control.
            # For simplicity, we iterate over unique hash codes in each head.
            for b in range(B):
                for h in range(H):
                    # Find unique buckets for this round batch, head
                    uniq = torch.unique(torch.cat([q_hash[b,h], k_hash[b,h]]))
                    for code in uniq:
                        q_mask = (q_hash[b,h] == code)
                        k_mask = (k_hash[b,h] == code)
                        if q_mask.sum() == 0 or k_mask.sum() == 0:
                            continue
                        Q_sel = Q[b,h][q_mask]  # (Nq, d)
                        K_sel = K[b,h][k_mask]
                        V_sel = V[b,h][k_mask]
                        scale = d ** -0.5
                        scores = torch.mm(Q_sel, K_sel.T) * scale
                        # Apply mask (optional)
                        attn = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
                        ctx = torch.mm(attn, V_sel)
                        # Scatter back
                        idx = torch.nonzero(q_mask, as_tuple=True)[0]
                        out[b,h, idx] += ctx

        context = out.transpose(1,2).contiguous().view(B, L, D)
        return self.out_proj(context), None
══════════════════ span_expanded_attention.py ══════════════════
# framework/attentions/specialized/span_expanded_attention.py
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from framework.attentions.core.base import BaseAttention
from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY


@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("span_expanded")
class SpanExpandedAttention(BaseAttention):
    """
    Span‑Expanded Attention with HyLoRA: fixed expansion cache, efficient LoRA adaptation.
    """
    def __init__(self, config, rank: int = None, expansion_span: int = 1024,
                 use_lora: bool = True, lora_rank: int = 8):
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.dim = config.hidden_size
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = self.dim // self.num_heads
        self.expansion_span = expansion_span
        self.use_lora = use_lora

        self.W_q = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
        self.W_k = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
        self.W_v = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim)

        if use_lora:
            self.lora_A = nn.Linear(self.dim, lora_rank, bias=False)
            self.lora_B = nn.Linear(lora_rank, self.dim, bias=False)

    def forward(self, hidden_states, kv_cache=None, **kwargs):
        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        q = self.W_q(hidden_states).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)   # (B, H, L, d)

        if kv_cache is not None:
            k_cache, v_cache = kv_cache
            k = k_cache.unsqueeze(1).expand(-1, self.num_heads, -1, -1)   # (B, H, C, d)
            v = v_cache.unsqueeze(1).expand(-1, self.num_heads, -1, -1)
        else:
            k = self.W_k(hidden_states).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)
            v = self.W_v(hidden_states).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)

        scale = self.head_dim ** -0.5
        attn = torch.matmul(q, k.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
        attn = F.softmax(attn, dim=-1)
        out = torch.matmul(attn, v)                                   # (B, H, L, d)
        out = out.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, D)

        if self.use_lora:
            lora_out = self.lora_B(self.lora_A(hidden_states))        # (B, L, D)
            out = out + lora_out

        out = self.out_proj(out)
        return out, None   # return tuple as required by attention interface

"""
Sparse attention modules – import safe.
"""
