══════════════════ mixattention_moska.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from framework.attentions.core.base import BaseAttention
from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("mixattention_moska")
class MixAttentionMoSKA(BaseAttention):
    """
    MixAttention‑MoSKA: batch‑shared KV as GEMM (simplified) and FASA‑pruning
    for unique sequences.
    """
    def __init__(self, config, rank=64, fasa_keep_ratio=0.189):
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.dim = config.hidden_size
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = self.dim // self.num_heads
        self.fasa_keep_ratio = fasa_keep_ratio

        self.W_q = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
        self.W_k = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
        self.W_v = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim)

    def _fasa_prune(self, k, v):
        # k: (B, H, L, D_h)
        B, H, L, D_h = k.shape
        # Token importance = variance across time dimension (mean over heads)
        importance = k.var(dim=-1).mean(dim=1)   # (B, L)
        keep = max(1, int(L * self.fasa_keep_ratio))
        _, idx = torch.topk(importance, keep, dim=-1)
        idx = idx.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, H, -1, D_h)
        k_pruned = k.gather(2, idx)
        v_pruned = v.gather(2, idx)
        return k_pruned, v_pruned

    def forward(self, x, mask=None, **kwargs):
        B, L, D = x.shape
        H = self.num_heads
        q = self.W_q(x).view(B, L, H, self.head_dim).permute(0,2,1,3)
        k = self.W_k(x).view(B, L, H, self.head_dim).permute(0,2,1,3)
        v = self.W_v(x).view(B, L, H, self.head_dim).permute(0,2,1,3)

        k_p, v_p = self._fasa_prune(k, v)
        scale = self.head_dim ** -0.5
        attn = torch.matmul(q, k_p.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
        if mask is not None:
            # adapt mask to pruned length: simple approach – ignore, or gather mask similarly.
            raise NotImplementedError("mask handling with pruned length not implemented yet")
        attn = F.softmax(attn, dim=-1)
        out = torch.matmul(attn, v_p).permute(0,2,1,3).contiguous().view(B, L, D)
        return self.out_proj(out), None
══════════════════ nsa_attention.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import math
from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("nsa")
class NativeSparseAttention(nn.Module):
    """
    Native Sparse Attention (NSA): Combines local sliding window with
    top‑k global sparse attention per head.
    """
    def __init__(self, config, window_size=128, top_k=32):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.window_size = window_size
        self.top_k = top_k
        self.q_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.k_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.v_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(config.attention_probs_dropout_prob)

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, **kwargs):
        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        H, d = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        Q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, d).permute(0,2,1,3)
        K = self.k_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, d).permute(0,2,1,3)
        V = self.v_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, d).permute(0,2,1,3)

        scale = d ** -0.5

        # Local window mask
        idx = torch.arange(L, device=Q.device)
        local_mask = (idx.unsqueeze(1) - idx.unsqueeze(0)).abs() <= self.window_size
        local_mask = local_mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)   # (1,1,L,L)

        # Global sparse: compute attention scores globally but keep only top_k per query
        # Compute full scores (quadratic) – for true NSA this would be approximated; we'll compute full and then zero out non‑top‑k.
        scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale   # (B, H, L, L)
        # Combine local + global: we retain local window fully, plus top_k outside window
        # For each query position, get top_k outside its window
        topk_mask = torch.zeros_like(scores)
        for i in range(L):
            # get scores for query position i
            s_i = scores[:, :, i, :]   # (B, H, L)
            # mask out local window tokens
            local_indices = local_mask[0,0,i]  # (L) bool
            s_i = s_i.masked_fill(local_indices.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), float('-inf'))
            # select top_k
            _, topk_ind = torch.topk(s_i, min(self.top_k, L), dim=-1)  # (B, H, K)
            # set mask
            topk_mask[:, :, i, :] = topk_mask[:, :, i, :].scatter(2, topk_ind.unsqueeze(2), 1.0)
        # ensure local tokens are also included
        topk_mask = topk_mask + local_mask.float()
        scores = scores.masked_fill(topk_mask == 0, float('-inf'))

        if attention_mask is not None:
            scores = scores + attention_mask.unsqueeze(1)

        attn = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
        attn = self.dropout(attn)
        context = torch.matmul(attn, V).permute(0,2,1,3).contiguous().view(B, L, D)
        return self.out_proj(context), None
══════════════════ adaptive_span_attention.py ══════════════════
# framework/attentions/specialized/adaptive_span_attention.py
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
import math
from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("adaptive_span")
class AdaptiveSpanAttention(nn.Module):
    """
    Adaptive Attention Span for Transformers.
    Each head learns a scalar span that soft‑masks tokens far in the future.
    """
    def __init__(self, config, max_span=1024):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.max_span = max_span
        self.q_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.k_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.v_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.hidden_size)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(config.attention_probs_dropout_prob)
        # per‑head span parameter (softplus to ensure positivity)
        self.span_raw = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.num_heads))

    def forward(self, hidden_states, attention_mask=None, **kwargs):
        batch_size, seq_len, _ = hidden_states.shape
        H = self.num_heads
        d = self.head_dim

        Q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(batch_size, seq_len, H, d).permute(0,2,1,3)
        K = self.k_proj(hidden_states).view(batch_size, seq_len, H, d).permute(0,2,1,3)
        V = self.v_proj(hidden_states).view(batch_size, seq_len, H, d).permute(0,2,1,3)

        # Learnable span: z = log(span - 1) -> span = softplus(z) + 1 to have minimum span = 1
        head_span = F.softplus(self.span_raw) + 1    # (H,)
        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(d)
        scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale

        # Causal mask with per‑head soft masking
        positions = torch.arange(seq_len, device=scores.device).float()
        relative_dist = (positions.unsqueeze(0) - positions.unsqueeze(1)).abs()  # (L, L)
        # For j > i (future), distance = relative_dist; for j <= i, set distance to 0 to keep.
        distance = torch.where(relative_dist > 0, relative_dist, torch.zeros_like(relative_dist))
        # Compute soft mask: 1 - sigmoid( (distance - span) / temp )
        temp = 1.0
        # shape (H, L, L)
        mask_val = torch.sigmoid((distance - head_span.view(-1, 1, 1)) / temp)
        # For j <= i, we want full attention (no masking), force mask_val to 0
        mask_val = torch.where(relative_dist > 0, mask_val, torch.zeros_like(mask_val))
        # Apply mask to scores: subtract 1e9 * mask_val
        scores = scores - 1e9 * mask_val.unsqueeze(0)  # (1, H, L, L)

        if attention_mask is not None:
            scores = scores + attention_mask.unsqueeze(1)

        attn_probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
        attn_probs = self.dropout(attn_probs)

        context = torch.matmul(attn_probs, V)  # (B, H, L, d)
        context = context.permute(0,2,1,3).contiguous().view(batch_size, seq_len, -1)
        out = self.out_proj(context)
        return out, attn_probs
══════════════════ block_transformer_hiba.py ══════════════════
# framework/attentions/specialized/block_transformer_hiba.py
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from framework.attentions.core.base import BaseAttention
from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("block_transformer_hiba")
class BlockTransformerHIBA(BaseAttention):
    """
    Block Transformer with HIBA: intra‑block full attention + inter‑block sparse attention
    and periodic dense rectification to prevent fragmentation.
    """
    def __init__(self, config, rank=64, block_size=512, top_k_inter=32, rectification_interval=4):
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.dim = config.hidden_size
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = self.dim // self.num_heads
        self.block_size = block_size
        self.top_k_inter = top_k_inter
        self.rectification_interval = rectification_interval

        self.W_q = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
        self.W_k = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
        self.W_v = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim, bias=False)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(self.dim, self.dim)

        self.layer_idx = 0   # incremented each forward pass

    def _intra_block_attention(self, q, k, v, causal=True):
        """Standard scaled dot‑product attention, optionally causal."""
        scale = self.head_dim ** -0.5
        attn = torch.matmul(q, k.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
        if causal:
            L = q.size(2)
            mask = torch.tril(torch.ones(L, L, device=q.device)).bool()
            attn = attn.masked_fill(~mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), float('-inf'))
        return torch.matmul(F.softmax(attn, dim=-1), v)

    def _inter_block_sparse(self, q_b, k_all, v_all, block_idx, num_blocks):
        """Sparse attention to top‑k tokens from adjacent blocks."""
        B, H, _, D = q_b.shape
        total_len = k_all.size(2)
        start_b = block_idx * self.block_size
        end_b = min((block_idx+1)*self.block_size, total_len)

        k_select = []
        v_select = []
        if block_idx > 0:
            k_select.append(k_all[:, :, :start_b, :])
            v_select.append(v_all[:, :, :start_b, :])
        if (block_idx+1)*self.block_size < total_len:
            k_select.append(k_all[:, :, end_b:, :])
            v_select.append(v_all[:, :, end_b:, :])

        if not k_select:
            return torch.zeros_like(q_b)
        k_neighbors = torch.cat(k_select, dim=2)
        v_neighbors = torch.cat(v_select, dim=2)

        scale = self.head_dim ** -0.5
        scores = torch.matmul(q_b, k_neighbors.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
        top_k = min(self.top_k_inter, k_neighbors.size(2))
        _, topk_idx = torch.topk(scores, top_k, dim=-1)
        mask = torch.zeros_like(scores).scatter_(-1, topk_idx, 1.0)
        scores = scores.masked_fill(mask == 0, float('-inf'))
        return torch.matmul(F.softmax(scores, dim=-1), v_neighbors)

    def forward(self, x, mask=None, **kwargs):
        B, L, D = x.shape
        H = self.num_heads
        head_dim = self.head_dim

        q = self.W_q(x).view(B, L, H, head_dim).permute(0,2,1,3)  # (B, H, L, d)
        k = self.W_k(x).view(B, L, H, head_dim).permute(0,2,1,3)
        v = self.W_v(x).view(B, L, H, head_dim).permute(0,2,1,3)

        num_blocks = (L + self.block_size - 1) // self.block_size
        use_rectify = (self.layer_idx % self.rectification_interval == 0)
        self.layer_idx += 1

        if use_rectify:
            # Global full attention (dense rectification)
            scale = head_dim ** -0.5
            scores = torch.matmul(q, k.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
            if mask is not None:
                scores = scores + mask.unsqueeze(1)
            attn_probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
            out = torch.matmul(attn_probs, v)
        else:
            out_list = []
            for b_idx in range(num_blocks):
                start = b_idx * self.block_size
                end = min(start + self.block_size, L)
                q_b = q[:, :, start:end, :]
                k_b = k[:, :, start:end, :]
                v_b = v[:, :, start:end, :]

                intra_out = self._intra_block_attention(q_b, k_b, v_b, causal=True)
                inter_out = self._inter_block_sparse(q_b, k, v, b_idx, num_blocks)
                out_b = intra_out + inter_out
                out_list.append(out_b)
            out = torch.cat(out_list, dim=2)   # (B, H, L, d)

        out = out.permute(0,2,1,3).contiguous().view(B, L, D)
        return self.out_proj(out), None
══════════════════ template_compressor.py ══════════════════
"""
Template for creating new compression methods.
Ready to use – copy and customize the compress flow.
"""

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, Any, Tuple, Optional, List
from ...core.registry import COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY
from ...core.base import BaseCompressor


# Simple low‑rank linear layer used for demonstration; you can replace with your own.
class SVDLinearLayer(nn.Module):
    """Low‑rank (U @ diag(S) @ V) layer created from an existing Linear."""
    def __init__(self, original_layer: nn.Linear, rank: int):
        super().__init__()
        out_features, in_features = original_layer.weight.shape
        self.rank = rank
        W = original_layer.weight.data.float()
        U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
        self.U = nn.Linear(rank, out_features, bias=False)
        self.V = nn.Linear(in_features, rank, bias=False)
        self.U.weight.data = (U[:, :rank] * S[:rank].unsqueeze(0)).float()
        self.V.weight.data = Vt[:rank, :].float()
        self.bias = None
        if original_layer.bias is not None:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(original_layer.bias.data.clone())

    def forward(self, x):
        x = self.V(x)
        x = self.U(x)
        if self.bias is not None:
            x = x + self.bias
        return x


@COMPRESSOR_REGISTRY.register("your_compressor_name")
class YourCustomCompressor(BaseCompressor):
    """
    Your custom compression method.
    
    This template supports:
        - Low‑rank (SVD)
        - Pruning (top‑k by row norm)
        - Quantization (uniform)
        - Hybrid (prune + low‑rank)
    
    Simply change `self.method` and adjust parameters.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        target_sparsity: float = 0.5,
        target_rank: int = 64,
        method: str = "standard",
        **kwargs
    ):
        super().__init__(model, name="your_compressor", **kwargs)
        self.target_sparsity = target_sparsity
        self.target_rank = target_rank
        self.method = method
        self.custom_param = kwargs.get('custom_param', 0.1)   # e.g., bits for quantization

        self.compressed_layers = []
        self.compression_stats = {}

    def compress(self) -> Tuple[nn.Module, Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Apply compression and return (compressed_model, stats)."""
        self._compress_embeddings()
        self._compress_attention_layers()
        self._compress_ffn_layers()
        self._compress_classifier()

        self._compressed_params = self._count_params(self.model)
        self._compression_stats = self._compute_stats()
        return self.model, self.get_compression_stats()

    def _compress_embeddings(self):
        if hasattr(self.model, 'embeddings'):
            emb_layer = self.model.embeddings.word_embeddings
            self._compress_linear_layers(emb_layer, "embeddings", "embedding")

    def _compress_attention_layers(self):
        for name, module in self.model.named_modules():
            if 'attention' in name.lower():
                self._compress_linear_layers(module, name, "attention")

    def _compress_ffn_layers(self):
        for name, module in self.model.named_modules():
            if 'ffn' in name.lower() or 'intermediate' in name.lower() or 'output' in name.lower():
                self._compress_linear_layers(module, name, "ffn")

    def _compress_classifier(self):
        if hasattr(self.model, 'classifier'):
            self._compress_linear_layers(self.model.classifier, "classifier", "classifier")

    def _compress_linear_layers(self, module: nn.Module, parent_name: str, layer_type: str):
        for child_name, child in module.named_modules():
            if isinstance(child, nn.Linear):
                original_size = child.weight.shape
                compressed_size = self._get_compressed_size(original_size)

                if self.method == "low_rank":
                    new_layer = SVDLinearLayer(child, self.target_rank)
                elif self.method == "pruning":
                    new_layer = self._create_pruned_layer(child, compressed_size)
                elif self.method == "quantization":
                    new_layer = self._create_quantized_layer(child)
                else:
                    new_layer = self._create_hybrid_layer(child, compressed_size)

                full_name = f"{parent_name}.{child_name}" if child_name else parent_name
                self._replace_layer(module, child_name, new_layer, full_name)
                self.compressed_layers.append({
                    'name': full_name,
                    'original_size': original_size,
                    'compressed_size': compressed_size if not isinstance(new_layer, SVDLinearLayer) else (new_layer.V.out_features, new_layer.U.in_features),
                    'type': layer_type,
                    'method': self.method
                })

    def _get_compressed_size(self, original_size: Tuple[int, int]) -> Tuple[int, int]:
        out_f, in_f = original_size
        if self.method == "pruning":
            new_out = max(1, int(out_f * (1 - self.target_sparsity)))
            new_in = max(1, int(in_f * (1 - self.target_sparsity)))
            return (new_out, new_in)
        return original_size

    def _create_pruned_layer(self, original_layer: nn.Linear, compressed_size: Tuple[int, int]) -> nn.Linear:
        new_out, new_in = compressed_size
        new_layer = nn.Linear(new_in, new_out)
        with torch.no_grad():
            weight = original_layer.weight.data
            row_norms = torch.norm(weight, dim=1)
            _, indices = torch.topk(row_norms, new_out)
            new_layer.weight.data = weight[indices][:, :new_in]
            if original_layer.bias is not None:
                new_layer.bias.data = original_layer.bias.data[indices]
        return new_layer

    def _create_quantized_layer(self, original_layer: nn.Linear) -> nn.Linear:
        bits = int(self.custom_param) if isinstance(self.custom_param, (int, float)) else 8
        scale = (2 ** (bits - 1)) - 1
        with torch.no_grad():
            weight = original_layer.weight.data
            weight_quant = torch.clamp(weight * scale, -scale, scale)
            weight_quant = torch.round(weight_quant) / scale
        new_layer = nn.Linear(original_layer.in_features, original_layer.out_features)
        new_layer.weight.data = weight_quant
        if original_layer.bias is not None:
            new_layer.bias.data = original_layer.bias.data
        return new_layer

    def _create_hybrid_layer(self, original_layer: nn.Linear, compressed_size: Tuple[int, int]) -> SVDLinearLayer:
        # first prune, then low‑rank
        pruned = self._create_pruned_layer(original_layer, compressed_size)
        rank = min(self.target_rank // 2, min(pruned.weight.shape))
        return SVDLinearLayer(pruned, rank)

    def _replace_layer(self, parent_module: nn.Module, child_name: str, new_layer: nn.Module, full_name: str):
        if hasattr(parent_module, child_name):
            setattr(parent_module, child_name, new_layer)
        else:
            print(f"Warning: could not find {child_name} in {full_name}")

    def _compute_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'total_layers_compressed': len(self.compressed_layers),
            'compression_ratio': self._compressed_params / self._original_params if self._original_params > 0 else 1.0,
            'parameters_saved': self._original_params - self._compressed_params,
            'reduction_percentage': (1 - self._compressed_params / self._original_params) * 100 if self._original_params > 0 else 0,
            'compressed_layers': self.compressed_layers,
            'compression_method': self.method,
        }

    def get_layer_wise_report(self) -> "pd.DataFrame":
        import pandas as pd
        return pd.DataFrame(self.compressed_layers)

    def visualize_compression(self, save_path: Optional[str] = None):
        import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
        if not self.compressed_layers:
            print("No compression statistics available")
            return

        layers = [l['name'].split('.')[-1][:20] for l in self.compressed_layers]
        orig_params = [l['original_size'][0]*l['original_size'][1] for l in self.compressed_layers]
        comp_params = [l['compressed_size'][0]*l['compressed_size'][1] for l in self.compressed_layers]

        fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(14, 5))
        x = range(len(layers))
        width = 0.35
        axes[0].bar([i - width/2 for i in x], orig_params, width, label='Original', alpha=0.7)
        axes[0].bar([i + width/2 for i in x], comp_params, width, label='Compressed', alpha=0.7)
        axes[0].set_xticks(x)
        axes[0].set_xticklabels(layers, rotation=45, ha='right')

══════════════════ template_attention.py ══════════════════
"""
Template for creating new attention mechanisms.
Customizable and ready to run.
"""

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Optional, Tuple
from ...core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY
from ...core.base import BaseAttention


@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("your_attention_name")
class YourCustomAttention(BaseAttention):
    """
    Your custom attention mechanism.
    
    Currently implements a standard Transformer attention with an optional
    low‑rank modulation based on pooled hidden states.
    """

    def __init__(self, config, rank: Optional[int] = None, **kwargs):
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.hidden_size = config.hidden_size
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = self.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.dropout = config.attention_probs_dropout_prob

        self.custom_param = kwargs.get('custom_param', 0.1)

        self.query = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.hidden_size)
        self.key = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.hidden_size)
        self.value = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.hidden_size)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.hidden_size)

        if rank is not None and rank > 0:
            self.rank_proj = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, rank, bias=False)
        else:
            self.rank_proj = None

        self._init_weights()

    def _init_weights(self):
        for module in [self.query, self.key, self.value, self.out_proj]:
            nn.init.xavier_uniform_(module.weight)
            if module.bias is not None:
                nn.init.zeros_(module.bias)

    def _reshape(self, x: torch.Tensor, batch_size: int) -> torch.Tensor:
        x = x.view(batch_size, -1, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
        return x.permute(0, 2, 1, 3)

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
        output_attentions: bool = False,
        **kwargs
    ) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, Optional[torch.Tensor]]:
        batch_size, seq_len, _ = hidden_states.shape

        Q = self._reshape(self.query(hidden_states), batch_size)
        K = self._reshape(self.key(hidden_states), batch_size)
        V = self._reshape(self.value(hidden_states), batch_size)

        scale = self.head_dim ** -0.5
        scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale

        # --- Your custom modification ---
        if self.rank_proj is not None:
            # Pool sequence dimension → global vector, then project to rank
            H_pool = hidden_states.mean(dim=1)          # (B, D)
            H_rank = self.rank_proj(H_pool)            # (B, rank)
            modulation = torch.sigmoid(H_rank).mean(dim=-1, keepdim=True)  # (B, 1)
            modulation = modulation.view(batch_size, 1, 1, 1)
            scores = scores * modulation

        if attention_mask is not None:
            scores = scores + attention_mask.unsqueeze(1)

        attn_probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
        attn_probs = F.dropout(attn_probs, p=self.dropout, training=self.training)

        context = torch.matmul(attn_probs, V)
        context = context.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(batch_size, seq_len, self.hidden_size)
        output = self.out_proj(context)

        if output_attentions:
            return output, attn_probs
        return output, None

    @property
    def complexity(self) -> str:
        return "quadratic" if self.rank_proj is None else "quadratic (with rank modulation)"

    def extra_repr(self) -> str:
        return f"num_heads={self.num_heads}, head_dim={self.head_dim}, rank={self.rank}"
══════════════════ nexus.py ══════════════════
"""
NEXUS  Neural Efficient X-scoring Unified Sparse Attention

=============================================================
Novel hybrid attention that fuses the best of ICML/NeurIPS 2025:

 1. XAttention antidiagonal scoring (ICML 2025)      - training-free block scoring
 2. SeerAttention learnable gating (NeurIPS 2025)    - per-head sparsity
 3. DuSA dual-stage sparse (NeurIPS 2025)            - intra + inter block
 4. AnchorAttention stripe granularity (2025)         - fine-grain stripes
 5. Spectraformer random-feature rectification (2025) - global linear fallback
 6. Softpick rectified softmax (2025)                 - natural ~47% sparsity

Papers:
  • XAttention: Xu et al., ICML 2025 (arXiv:2503.16428)
  • SeerAttention: Gao et al., NeurIPS 2025
  • DuSA: Cao et al., NeurIPS 2025
  • AnchorAttention: Zhu et al., 2025 (arXiv:2505.23523)
  • Spectraformer: Nguyen et al., 2025 (arXiv:2405.15310)
  • Softpick: Zuhri et al., arXiv 2504.20966 (2025)

Author: Taha Majs  2026
"""

import math
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Optional, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass
from framework.attentions.core.base import BaseAttention
from framework.attentions.core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY

# ============================================================================
# Configuration
# ============================================================================
@dataclass
class NEXUSConfig:
    # --- Block structure ---
    block_size: int = 32                     # tokens per block (smaller = finer)
    top_k_blocks: int = 4                    # how many inter-block blocks to keep

    # --- XAttention antidiagonal scoring ---
    xattn_enabled: bool = True
    xattn_stride: int = 2                    # stride for antidiagonal sampling

    # --- SeerAttention gating ---
    seer_enabled: bool = True
    seer_gate_rank: int = 16                 # hidden dim of gate network
    seer_top_k_blocks: int = 4               # blocks kept by the learned gate
    seer_temperature: float = 1.0

    # --- DuSA dual-stage ---
    dual_stage_enabled: bool = True          # (unused here, always on)
    intra_block_full: bool = True            # dense inside each block

    # --- AnchorAttention stripe ---
    stripe_enabled: bool = True
    stripe_width: int = 4                    # tokens per stripe

    # --- Spectraformer global rectification ---
    rectify_interval: int = 4                # every Nth layer uses kernel attn
    rff_features: int = 128

    # --- Softpick ---
    softpick_enabled: bool = True
    softpick_temperature: float = 1.0

    dropout: float = 0.1


# ============================================================================
# Utility: Softpick rectified softmax
# ============================================================================
def softpick(x: torch.Tensor, dim: int = -1, temperature: float = 1.0) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Rectified ~47% sparse softmax, no attention sink."""
    m = x.max(dim=dim, keepdim=True).values
    e = torch.exp((x - m) / temperature)
    denom = e - torch.exp(-m / temperature)
    p = F.relu(denom)
    return p / (p.sum(dim=dim, keepdim=True) + 1e-9)


# ============================================================================
# 1. XAttention antidiagonal block scoring
# ============================================================================
class XAttentionScorer:
    """
    Training‑free block importance via antidiagonal sums.
    Reference: XAttention, Xu et al., ICML 2025.
    """
    @staticmethod
    def score_blocks(Q: torch.Tensor, K: torch.Tensor,
                     block_size: int, stride: int = 2) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Q, K: (B, H, N, D)
        Returns: block_score (B, H, n_blocks, n_blocks)
        """
        B, H, N, D = Q.shape
        n_blocks = (N + block_size - 1) // block_size

        # sample tokens at stride for speed
        Q_sample = Q[:, :, ::stride, :]   # (B, H, N_s, D)
        K_sample = K[:, :, ::stride, :]

        # compute a tiny attention map on sampled tokens
        scale = D ** -0.5
        attn_sample = torch.matmul(Q_sample, K_sample.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale

        # for each block, sum antidiagonal values
        scores = torch.zeros(B, H, n_blocks, n_blocks, device=Q.device, dtype=Q.dtype)
        N_s = Q_sample.size(2)

        for bi in range(n_blocks):
            q_start = bi * (block_size // stride)
            q_end = min(q_start + block_size // stride, N_s)
            for bj in range(n_blocks):
                k_start = bj * (block_size // stride)
                k_end = min(k_start + block_size // stride, N_s)
                # antidiagonal: mean over the block
                block = attn_sample[:, :, q_start:q_end, k_start:k_end]
                scores[:, :, bi, bj] = block.mean(dim=(2, 3))

        return scores   # (B, H, n_blocks, n_blocks)


# ============================================================================
# 2. SeerAttention learnable gating
# ============================================================================
class SeerGating(nn.Module):
    """
    Learnable block‑level sparsity gate.
    Reference: SeerAttention, Gao et al., NeurIPS 2025.
    """
    def __init__(self, head_dim: int, num_heads: int,
                 gate_rank: int, top_k: int, temperature: float = 1.0):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_heads = num_heads
        self.head_dim = head_dim
        self.top_k = top_k
        self.temperature = temperature

        # pooling projection for Q and K
        self.q_pool = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(head_dim, gate_rank),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Linear(gate_rank, gate_rank),
        )
        self.k_pool = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(head_dim, gate_rank),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Linear(gate_rank, gate_rank),
        )

    def forward(self, Q: torch.Tensor, K: torch.Tensor,
                n_blocks: int, training: bool = True) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Q, K: (B, H, N, D)
        Returns: hard_mask (B, H, n_blocks, n_blocks)
        """
        B, H, N, D = Q.shape
        gr = self.q_pool[0].out_features   # gate_rank

        # pool over sequence: mean pool Q and K for each head
        Q_pooled = Q.mean(dim=2)   # (B, H, D)
        K_pooled = K.mean(dim=2)

        # project through gate networks
        q_gate = self.q_pool(Q_pooled).view(B, H, 1, gr)   # (B, H, 1, gr)
        k_gate = self.k_pool(K_pooled).view(B, H, gr, 1)

        # produce a scalar gate score per head (we need per‑block)
        gate_score = torch.sigmoid(
            torch.matmul(q_gate, k_gate).squeeze(-1).squeeze(-1) / self.temperature
        )   # (B, H)

        # for each head, build a block mask:
        keep_per_head = torch.clamp(
            (gate_score * n_blocks).long(), min=1, max=n_blocks
        )   # (B, H)

        mask = torch.zeros(B, H, n_blocks, n_blocks, device=Q.device)
        for h in range(H):
            k = keep_per_head[0, h].item()   # batch‑uniform
            mask[:, h, :k, :k] = 1.0

        return mask.bool()

# 3. Dual‑Stage + Stripe Attention (core engine)
# ============================================================================
class DualStageStripeAttention(nn.Module):
    """
    DuSA‑style intra‑block full + inter‑block XAttention‑scored top‑k,
    with AnchorAttention‑style stripe granularity inside blocks.
    """
    def __init__(self, cfg: NEXUSConfig, head_dim: int):
        super().__init__()

══════════════════ fusion_attn.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Optional, Tuple

class FUSIONATTN(nn.Module):
    """
    FUSIONATTN: A hybrid sparse attention module for training-free acceleration.
    Combines SpargeAttn, SeerAttention, MoBA, and Kascade into a single module.
    """
    def __init__(self, hidden_size: int, num_heads: int, block_size: int = 64, top_k_blocks: int = 4):
        super().__init__()
        self.hidden_size = hidden_size
        self.num_heads = num_heads
        self.head_dim = hidden_size // num_heads
        self.block_size = block_size
        self.top_k_blocks = top_k_blocks

        # Standard projections
        self.q_proj = nn.Linear(hidden_size, hidden_size)
        self.k_proj = nn.Linear(hidden_size, hidden_size)
        self.v_proj = nn.Linear(hidden_size, hidden_size)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(hidden_size, hidden_size)

        # SeerAttention-Inspired Gate (only gate is trainable, but works without training)
        self.attn_gate = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(self.head_dim, 4 * self.head_dim),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Linear(4 * self.head_dim, 1)
        )

        # Learnable temperature for gate sharpness
        self.gate_temp = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(1) * 1.0)

    def _sparge_predict(self, q: torch.Tensor, k: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        SpargeAttn-style fast attention map prediction.
        Uses a low-cost approximation before full computation.
        """
        # Fast approximate attention score computation
        # Using reduced precision and smaller feature dimension
        q_approx = q[..., :self.head_dim//2]  # Use half the features for speed
        k_approx = k[..., :self.head_dim//2]
        
        # Fast approximate scores (not full precision)
        scores_approx = torch.matmul(q_approx, k_approx.transpose(-2, -1))
        scores_approx = scores_approx / ((self.head_dim//2) ** 0.5)
        
        # Identify dense regions (high score areas)
        dense_mask = scores_approx > scores_approx.mean(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
        
        return dense_mask

    def _seer_gate_blocks(self, q: torch.Tensor, k: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        SeerAttention-style block gating.
        Predicts which blocks of keys are important for each query.
        """
        B, H, N, D = q.shape
        
        # Reshape into blocks
        num_blocks = (N + self.block_size - 1) // self.block_size
        q_blocks = q.reshape(B, H, num_blocks, self.block_size, D).mean(dim=-2)  # (B, H, num_blocks, D)
        k_blocks = k.reshape(B, H, num_blocks, self.block_size, D).mean(dim=-2)
        
        # Compute gate scores for each query-key block pair
        q_gate = self.attn_gate(q_blocks)  # (B, H, num_blocks, 1)
        k_gate = self.attn_gate(k_blocks)
        
        # Block-level attention scores
        gate_scores = torch.matmul(q_gate, k_gate.transpose(-2, -1))  # (B, H, num_blocks, num_blocks)
        gate_scores = gate_scores.squeeze(-1) / self.gate_temp
        
        return gate_scores

    def _moba_block_attention(
        self,
        q: torch.Tensor,
        k: torch.Tensor,
        v: torch.Tensor,
        gate_scores: torch.Tensor,
        dense_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        MoBA-style block-sparse attention with SpargeAttn filter.
        Only attends to top-k blocks based on gate scores.
        """
        B, H, N, D = q.shape
        num_blocks = (N + self.block_size - 1) // self.block_size
        
        # Select top-k blocks per query block
        top_k = min(self.top_k_blocks, num_blocks)
        _, top_indices = torch.topk(gate_scores, top_k, dim=-1)  # (B, H, num_blocks, top_k)
        
        # Reshape into blocks
        q_blocks = q.reshape(B * H, num_blocks, self.block_size, D)
        k_blocks = k.reshape(B * H, num_blocks, self.block_size, D)
        v_blocks = v.reshape(B * H, num_blocks, self.block_size, D)
        
        # Gather top-k blocks for each query block
        out_blocks = []
        for b in range(num_blocks):
            # Get indices for current block
            block_indices = top_indices[:, :, b, :]  # (B, H, top_k)
            
            # Gather corresponding k,v blocks
            k_selected = k_blocks[:, block_indices[0, 0], :, :].unsqueeze(0)  # Simplified gathering
            v_selected = v_blocks[:, block_indices[0, 0], :, :].unsqueeze(0)
            
            # Optional: Apply SpargeAttn filter to further prune within blocks
            if dense_mask is not None:
                mask_block = dense_mask[:, :, b*self.block_size:(b+1)*self.block_size, 
                                        :k_selected.shape[2]]
                # Apply mask during attention computation
                attn_block = torch.matmul(q_blocks[:, b:b+1, :, :], 
                                         k_selected.transpose(-2, -1)) / (D ** 0.5)
                attn_block = attn_block.masked_fill(~mask_block.unsqueeze(0), -1e9)
            else:
                attn_block = torch.matmul(q_blocks[:, b:b+1, :, :], 
                                         k_selected.transpose(-2, -1)) / (D ** 0.5)
            
            attn_block = F.softmax(attn_block, dim=-1)
            out_block = torch.matmul(attn_block, v_selected)
            out_blocks.append(out_block)
        
        # Concatenate block outputs
        out = torch.cat(out_blocks, dim=2)  # (B*H, N, D)
        out = out.reshape(B, H, N, D)
        
        return out

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
        use_sparge: bool = True,
        use_kascade_stability: bool = False,
        **kwargs
    ) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, Optional[torch.Tensor]]:
        B, N, D = hidden_states.shape
        
        # Project to Q, K, V
        q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).reshape(B, N, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)
        k = self.k_proj(hidden_states).reshape(B, N, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)
        v = self.v_proj(hidden_states).reshape(B, N, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)
        
        # Step 1: SpargeAttn - Fast prediction of dense regions
        dense_mask = None
        if use_sparge:
            dense_mask = self._sparge_predict(q, k)
        
        # Step 2: SeerAttention-style block gating
        gate_scores = self._seer_gate_blocks(q, k)
        
        # Step 3: MoBA-style top-k block attention
        context = self._moba_block_attention(q, k, v, gate_scores, dense_mask)
        
        # Optional: Kascade-style mask reuse (simplified)
        # In practice, you'd cache gate_scores from anchor layers and reuse them
        
        # Apply attention mask if provided
        if attention_mask is not None:
            if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
                mask = attention_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)
            elif attention_mask.dim() == 3:
                mask = attention_mask.unsqueeze(2)
            else:
                mask = attention_mask
            context = context * mask.to(context.dtype)
        
        # Reshape and project
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N, D)
        output = self.out_proj(context)
        
        return output, None

    def get_ortho_loss(self):
        """Compatibility with your CompressionTrainer."""
        return torch.tensor(0.0, device=self.gate_temp.device)
══════════════════ __init__.py ══════════════════
# Auto-generated __init__.py
from . import bam_umoe

══════════════════ bam_umoe.py ══════════════════
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from ..core.base import BaseAttention
from ..core.registry import ATTENTION_REGISTRY


class UnifiedMoE(nn.Module):
    """Mixture of Experts with top‑k gating. Can be used for any projection."""
    def __init__(self, dim, num_experts=8, top_k=2, output_dim=None):
        super().__init__()
        self.dim = dim
        self.num_experts = num_experts
        self.top_k = top_k
        out_dim = output_dim or dim
        self.out_dim = out_dim

        self.router = nn.Linear(dim, num_experts, bias=False)
        # Each expert is an FFN‑style block
        self.experts = nn.ModuleList([
            nn.Sequential(
                nn.Linear(dim, out_dim * 2),
                nn.GELU(),
                nn.Linear(out_dim * 2, out_dim)
            ) for _ in range(num_experts)
        ])

    def forward(self, x):
        B, L, D = x.shape
        logits = self.router(x)                     # (B, L, E)
        topk_vals, topk_idx = torch.topk(logits, self.top_k, dim=-1)   # (B, L, k)
        topk_vals = F.softmax(topk_vals, dim=-1)    # normalise the selected weights

        out = torch.zeros(B, L, self.out_dim, device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype)
        for k in range(self.top_k):
            expert_idx = topk_idx[..., k]           # (B, L)
            scale = topk_vals[..., k:k+1]           # (B, L, 1)
            for e in range(self.num_experts):
                mask = (expert_idx == e)
                if mask.any():
                    expert_out = self.experts[e](x)  # (B, L, out_dim)
                    out = out + mask.float().unsqueeze(-1) * scale * expert_out
        return out


@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("bam_umoe")
class BAMUMoEBlock(BaseAttention):
    """
    BAM!‑UMoE: Unified Mixture of Experts for Attention and FFN.
    - Shared experts produce Q, K, V projections (via an MoE that outputs 3*dim).
    - Standard multi‑head attention is computed on the resulting Q, K, V.
    - Another shared MoE (possibly a separate instance) acts as the feed‑forward network.
    """
    def __init__(self, config, rank=64, num_experts=8, top_k=2):
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.dim = config.hidden_size
        self.num_heads = config.num_attention_heads
        self.head_dim = config.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim

        # MoE that outputs Q, K, V concatenated (3*all_head_size)
        self.qkv_moe = UnifiedMoE(self.dim, num_experts, top_k, output_dim=3 * self.all_head_size)
        # MoE for the feed‑forward network (output same dim)
        self.ffn_moe = UnifiedMoE(self.dim, num_experts, top_k, output_dim=self.dim)

        self.output_proj = nn.Linear(self.all_head_size, self.dim)  # final projection
        self.norm1 = nn.LayerNorm(self.dim)
        self.norm2 = nn.LayerNorm(self.dim)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(config.attention_probs_dropout_prob)

    def forward(self, x, mask=None, **kwargs):
        B, L, D = x.shape
        H = self.num_heads
        d = self.head_dim

        # 1. Attention branch
        qkv = self.qkv_moe(x)                     # (B, L, 3*all_head_size)
        q, k, v = qkv.chunk(3, dim=-1)

        q = q.view(B, L, H, d).transpose(1, 2)    # (B, H, L, d)
        k = k.view(B, L, H, d).transpose(1, 2)
        v = v.view(B, L, H, d).transpose(1, 2)

        scale = d ** -0.5
        attn_scores = torch.matmul(q, k.transpose(-2, -1)) * scale   # (B, H, L, L)

        if mask is not None:
            # Assume mask is (B, L) with 1 for keep, 0 for pad
            if mask.dim() == 2:
                mask = mask[:, None, None, :].to(dtype=attn_scores.dtype)
                attn_scores = attn_scores + (1.0 - mask) * -1e9
            else:
                attn_scores = attn_scores + mask.unsqueeze(1)

        attn_weights = F.softmax(attn_scores, dim=-1)
        attn_weights = self.dropout(attn_weights)

        context = torch.matmul(attn_weights, v)    # (B, H, L, d)
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, -1)  # (B, L, D)
        attn_out = self.output_proj(context)

        # Residual + norm
        x = self.norm1(x + attn_out)

        # 2. FFN branch (unified MoE)
        ffn_out = self.ffn_moe(x)
        x = self.norm2(x + ffn_out)

        return x, attn_weights
══════════════════ phantom.py ══════════════════
"""
PHANTOM — Parameter‑Hashed AttentioN with Token‑wise Orthogonal Mapping
CUR‑hashed projections + LSH attention for DistilBERT.
"""

import math
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from typing import Optional, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass
class PHANTOMConfig:
    cur_rank: int = 16
    lsh_rounds: int = 4
    lsh_bucket_size: int = 32
    softpick_enabled: bool = True
    softpick_temperature: float = 1.0
    dropout: float = 0.1


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Softpick activation (sparse‑friendly rectified softmax)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def softpick(x: torch.Tensor, dim: int = -1, temperature: float = 1.0) -> torch.Tensor:
    m = x.max(dim=dim, keepdim=True).values
    e = torch.exp((x - m) / temperature)
    denom = e - torch.exp(-m / temperature)
    p = F.relu(denom)
    return p / (p.sum(dim=dim, keepdim=True) + 1e-9)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# CUR‑based factorised linear layer
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class FactorisedLinear_CUR(nn.Module):
    """
    Approximate a weight matrix W via CUR decomposition:
      W ≈ C @ U @ R, rank = r.
    C and R are column and row subsets respectively, U is a small linking matrix.
    The compressed representation uses two linear layers:
      down: x -> R space (input to row subspace)
      up:   -> output (column subspace)
    Weights are randomly initialised from CUR.
    """
    def __init__(self, pretrained_weight: torch.Tensor, pretrained_bias: Optional[torch.Tensor],
                 rank: int):
        super().__init__()
        out_dim, in_dim = pretrained_weight.shape
        self.rank = rank

        # Perform CUR decomposition (approximate leverage score sampling)
        with torch.no_grad():
            W = pretrained_weight.float()
            # Compute leverage scores for rows and columns using SVD
            U_full, S_full, Vt_full = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
            # Column leverage scores (from V)
            col_leverage = (Vt_full ** 2).sum(dim=0)
            col_leverage /= col_leverage.sum()
            # Row leverage scores (from U)
            row_leverage = (U_full ** 2).sum(dim=1)
            row_leverage /= row_leverage.sum()

            # Sample r rows and columns with probability proportional to leverage
            # For simplicity, take top‑r according to scores (deterministic)
            _, col_idx = torch.topk(col_leverage, k=rank, largest=True)
            _, row_idx = torch.topk(row_leverage, k=rank, largest=True)

            C = W[:, col_idx]                            # (out_dim, r)
            R = W[row_idx, :]                            # (r, in_dim)
            W_intersection = W[row_idx][:, col_idx]      # (r, r)
            # Compute pseudoinverse of the intersection to get U
            U_int = torch.linalg.pinv(W_intersection.float())  # (r, r)

            # CUR weights: W ≈ C @ U_int @ R
            # We'll embed C and (U_int @ R) into the two linear layers.
            # down: weights = R (so that down(x) = R @ x), output size = r
            # up:   weights = C @ U_int (size: out_dim × r)
            self.down = nn.Linear(in_dim, rank, bias=False)
            self.up   = nn.Linear(rank, out_dim, bias=(pretrained_bias is not None))

            self.down.weight.data = R.float()
            self.up.weight.data   = (C @ U_int).float()

            if pretrained_bias is not None:
                self.up.bias.data = pretrained_bias.float()
            else:
                self.up.bias.data.zero_()

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.up(self.down(x))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# LSH attention (angular hash)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def lsh_attention(Q: torch.Tensor, K: torch.Tensor, V: torch.Tensor,
                  rounds: int, bucket_size: int,
                  softpick_temp: float = 1.0,
                  dropout: Optional[nn.Module] = None,
                  token_valid: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None) -> torch.Tensor:
    """
    Compute attention using multi‑round angular LSH.
    Q, K, V: (B, heads, N, D)
    token_valid: (B, N) boolean mask (True = keep)
    """
    B, H, N, D = Q.shape
    scale = D ** -0.5

    # Normalise for angular LSH
    Q_norm = F.normalize(Q, p=2, dim=-1)
    K_norm = F.normalize(K, p=2, dim=-1)

    out = torch.zeros_like(V)
    for r in range(rounds):
        # Generate random hyperplanes for hashing
        # Use a different projection each round (shared across batch and heads)
        hash_proj = torch.randn(D, bucket_size, device=Q.device, dtype=Q.dtype)
        hash_proj = F.normalize(hash_proj, p=2, dim=0)

        # Hash code: sign(Q_norm @ proj) > 0 → bucket index
        # Turn into a binary code and reduce to integer
        hash_code_q = (torch.matmul(Q_norm, hash_proj) > 0).int()   # (B, H, N, bucket_size)
        hash_code_q = hash_code_q.mul(torch.tensor([2**i for i in range(bucket_size)],
                                                   device=Q.device).view(1,1,1,-1)).sum(dim=-1)  # (B, H, N)

        hash_code_k = (torch.matmul(K_norm, hash_proj) > 0).int()
        hash_code_k = hash_code_k.mul(torch.tensor([2**i for i in range(bucket_size)],
                                                   device=Q.device).view(1,1,1,-1)).sum(dim=-1)

        # For each head, sort by hash code and perform chunked attention within each bucket
        for b in range(B):
            for h in range(H):
                q_h = Q[b, h]                     # (N, D)
                k_h = K[b, h]
                v_h = V[b, h]
                if token_valid is not None:
                    valid = token_valid[b]
                    q_h = q_h[valid]
                    k_h = k_h[valid]
                    v_h = v_h[valid]
                    hq = hash_code_q[b, h][valid]
                    hk = hash_code_k[b, h][valid]
                    N_eff = q_h.size(0)
                    if N_eff == 0:
                        continue
                else:
                    hq = hash_code_q[b, h]
                    hk = hash_code_k[b, h]
                    N_eff = N

                # Sort queries by hash code
                sorted_q_idx = torch.argsort(hq)
                q_sorted = q_h[sorted_q_idx]      # (N_eff, D)
                v_sorted = v_h[sorted_q_idx]
                # For keys, we need to lookup by the same sorting; we'll compute scores in a double loop (simplified)
                # Actually efficient bucket attention would use a sorted list. For brevity, we do naive O(N^2) per head
                # but reduce the compute by only attending within same bucket.
                # I'll implement bucket gathering:
                buckets = {}
                for i, code in enumerate(hk):
                    buckets.setdefault(code.item(), []).append(i)
                # For each query, only attend to keys in the same bucket.
                attn_ctx = torch.zeros_like(q_sorted)
                for i, q_i in enumerate(q_sorted):
                    code = hq[sorted_q_idx[i]].item()
                    key_indices = buckets.get(code, [])
                    if not key_indices:
                        continue
                    k_bucket = k_h[key_indices]      # (L_b, D)
                    v_bucket = v_h[key_indices]
                    # Compute scores with softpick
                    scores = (q_i * scale) @ k_bucket.T
                    # Apply softpick
                    attn_w = softpick(scores.unsqueeze(0), dim=-1, temperature=softpick_temp).squeeze(0)
                    if dropout is not None and dropout.p > 0.:
                        attn_w = dropout(attn_w)
                    ctx = attn_w @ v_bucket
                    attn_ctx[i] = ctx
                # Unsort
                unsorted_idx = torch.argsort(sorted_q_idx)
                attn_unsorted = attn_ctx[unsorted_idx]
                # Place back into output
                if token_valid is not None:
                    out[b, h][valid] = attn_unsorted
                else:
                    out[b, h] = attn_unsorted
    return out
